`Documentation.md`:

```md
# AU-Anticheat — feature documentation

This guide describes **beta 1 (1.0.0-beta.1)** as a complete release. For installation, compatibility and credits, see [README.md](README.md).

## Contents

- [Authority and protection limits](#authority-and-protection-limits)
- [Overview and status badge](#overview-and-status-badge)
- [Lobby](#lobby)
- [Alerts](#alerts)
- [Players](#players)
- [Protection](#protection)
- [Detection and points](#detection-and-points)
- [Settings and appearance](#settings-and-appearance)
- [Privacy and saved files](#privacy-and-saved-files)
- [Building and testing](#building-and-testing)
- [Reference review](#reference-review)

## Authority and protection limits

**Host:** local protection plus normal host-authorized moderation, lobby entry rules, saved game settings and public chat features.

**Non-host:** covered messages received by your client can be rejected locally; local alerts, evidence review and chat hiding remain available. You cannot ban other players, take over the host or intercept messages sent only to other clients.

Blocking means preventing this installation from processing a covered message. It does not prove that every other player was protected or that an attacker was identified. A named object owner, claimed sender, repeated pattern, level, outfit or platform is not authenticated identity. Unknown senders must not be blamed on the player named in a forged message.

There is no guarantee of detecting every cheat, remote injection or hotkey. The plugin does not verify cosmetic purchases, detect all passive role viewers, hide everyone's IP addresses, stop network-level DDoS attacks, undo server removals or instantly reverse arbitrary actions for all players. The previously reported unidentified mass-removal incident has not been reproduced and confirmed fixed.

## Overview and status badge

Overview shows whether you are hosting and offers the main **Cheat detection** switch and a short menu guide.

- **ACTIVE:** protection hooks loaded and detection is enabled. Individual guards can still be off.
- **PAUSED:** detection is off; separately enabled guards may still run.
- **INACTIVE:** protection attachment failed. Check the log and compatibility.

The top-right red X badge uses the same status. It is not a guarantee that the lobby is cheat-free.

## Lobby

### Minimum entry level

Open **Lobby entry rule**, enable **Ban below minimum level**, and select a minimum of at least 1. Use the step buttons or **Choose exact minimum...**, enter the number and select **Apply minimum**. The default minimum is 10, but enforcement starts disabled.

The rule can be configured with players already present. It enforces a lobby preference using normal host bans, not cheating points or ErrorKick. The host/local player and saved friend-code exceptions are exempt.

Loaded, nonzero live level data takes priority over a stale join snapshot. If live data is unavailable, a non-conflicting current join record may be used. These are game-reported levels, not verified account levels.

Level 1 needs special care because the game's zero value can also mean data is still loading. Enforcement requires an observed level-one update, complete player data, both records showing zero, no earlier higher level for that player object, and **five stable seconds**. Waiting alone does not turn an unknown level into a real level 1. Missing evidence can therefore leave a genuine level-1 player unconfirmed.

The rule checks host authority, game ban permission and target/client mapping. It allows settling time, requests one removal at a time, waits for departure, then waits two seconds before another. An outstanding request that does not complete within fifteen seconds pauses admission enforcement instead of producing a burst. Changing settings cannot bypass a pending request. Entry-rule notices stay local and do not accuse the player of cheating.

### Friend-code exceptions

Open **Friend-code exceptions**, choose **Type friend code**, enter the complete code, then press Enter or **Add exception**. Close game chat before typing. Backspace edits; Escape stops typing. Use an entry's Remove button to delete it.

Up to 128 normalized, case-insensitive codes are saved locally. Exceptions bypass only the minimum-level rule: they do not exempt a player from detection or an explicit persistent ban. Adding an exception cannot undo a ban already sent. Friend codes are reported identifiers, not spoof-proof authentication.

### Saved lobby rules

Open **Saved lobby rules → Edit rules**. Type your rules, then select **Save rules**. Enter separates rules; Backspace edits. **Cancel editing** or Escape stops editing without saving. Close game chat before typing. You can also edit the saved text file and select **Reload saved rules**.

All rules must fit **one unlabeled message of at most 100 characters**, including separators. Up to eight short rule lines are accepted; blank lines and lines starting with `#` are ignored. The file limit is 4 KB. Invalid or overlong edits do not replace the previous rules; the plugin does not split them into multiple chat messages or silently truncate them.

Enable **Auto-post rules when lobby is full** and choose **1–120 seconds**. This uses the configured normal lobby capacity, from 4 to 15, rather than requiring 15/15.

The same initialized roster must remain full for the chosen delay. Rules post once for that stable full-lobby cycle. A departure or replacement cancels a queued post and restarts the delay; the next eligible refill can post again. An unchanged full roster gets no repeat. Toggling the option or reloading alone does not resend rules already posted to that roster. Game start, loss of host authority and disabling the option cancel unsent output.

### Match summaries

**Post results after each match** is enabled by default. As host, return to the same waiting lobby after a completed match. The plugin waits **five continuous seconds of player, lobby and chat readiness**, then waits for the shared chat cooldown.

It attempts one unlabeled message of at most 100 characters containing the winner, match duration and observed impostors with roles and kill counts. Names may be shortened, marked with `~`. Role keys include `I` = Impostor, `S` = Shapeshifter, `P` = Phantom and `V` = Viper; `k` means observed kills. Full details remain in local Alerts. If the summary cannot safely fit or required data is missing, it stays local rather than inventing names or sending multiple lines.

Kill counts use successful observed results, deduplicated per victim; failed/protected attempts are excluded. Saved round observations survive an impostor leaving before Play Again. They are not server-audited statistics. Starting another game, changing host/room, leaving or disabling the feature prevents stale reports. Current-round roles are not announced during gameplay.

### Shared detection notices and chat timing

**Share evidence-based detections in chat** permits host-only notices from the existing attributable threshold or confirmed-exploit paths, for example:

```text
[AU-Anticheat] Player got detected by anticheat for reason
```

The wording can say banned or kicked for the corresponding normal host removal request; that wording is not a separate server acknowledgment. Only ban/kick/detect notices get the label. Entry-level bans, unknown-sender incidents and mass-disconnect suspicions do not generate public accusations. Names and reasons are sanitized and shortened to fit.

Rules, summaries and these notices share one outgoing queue. Each send waits for at least **max(3 seconds, the game's chat send interval) + 0.25 seconds**. Your successful manual chat also postpones automation. Automatic sends consume the normal cooldown and can delay your next manual message. Clearing the queue or toggling options does not erase an outstanding cooldown.

Messages obey normal chat availability and restrictions. Automatic match-time chat requires a living host in an active meeting; lobby loading and start countdowns delay it. Notices expire after 20 seconds queued; rules/results after 90 seconds. Busy chat may mean a message is skipped. Duplicate pending rules/reports are coalesced, delayed local echoes are suppressed, and rejected sends are not retried in a burst. Local pop-ups do not send chat.

### Lobby extensions

**Accept offered lobby extensions** accepts the game's native extension only when offered. Requests are limited per offer and paced; status distinguishes requested, confirmed, expired and unconfirmed. Missing confirmation produces a warning, not forged activity or repeated requests.

This does **not** disable inactivity expiry, create extension offers, recreate the room or guarantee that players remain connected. Server approval is required.

### Lobby settings loadouts

While hosting a waiting lobby with no start countdown, open **Lobby settings loadouts → Name new loadout**, type a name and choose **Save current lobby settings**. Close chat before typing. Names allow 1–32 letters, numbers, spaces, hyphens and underscores; up to 20 loadouts are stored.

Loadouts store normal game options, including role options—not player identities or anti-cheat preferences. Apply requires confirmation and the **same game build, mode, map and player capacity**. Select those normally first. Applications are at least five seconds apart and use normal game synchronization. The plugin never evicts players to fit a preset. Check the visible settings before starting; a partially sent network update cannot always be rolled back after a failure.

Delete a preset with its Delete button and confirmation. Existing names are not overwritten automatically. Invalid or oversized saved files are refused without automatically replacing them. Resetting preferences preserves loadouts.

### Local moderation and chat filters

Open **Local moderation and chat filters**:

| Option | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| Hide lobby ads locally | Hides recognized recruitment phrases and invite links on your screen. Enabled by default; never bans. |
| Hide chat floods locally | Hides messages beyond five observed messages from the same player object in ten seconds. Enabled by default. |
| Use saved word filter | Hides chat matching your literal phrases. Disabled by default. |
| Hide chat from filtered names | Hides chat from matching reported names; does not change names or ban players. Disabled by default. |
| Enforce my persistent ban list when hosting | Uses your local friend-code ban list in hosted waiting lobbies, with normal paced bans. Enabled by default; initially empty. |
| Show possible AFK players in lobby | Local reminders after 60–600 seconds without observed movement/chat. Disabled by default; never automatically kicks. Lag and menus can look idle. |
| Guard local kick/ban and late-rejection requests | Blocks covered unauthorized/self-targeted requests from this installation and late startup rejection of already-ready clients. Enabled by default; retains ordinary host moderation and legitimate startup handling. |

Chat filters work locally in host and non-host lobbies. Other players may already have received the message. Filter matches and watchlists do not award cheating points, identify a remote menu or prove who sent an attack.

Under **Players**, select a player to add/remove a **local block** (hide their chat) or **local watch** (private review reminder). Hosts can confirm **Save persistent ban**. Matching current friend-code records are required. Persistent bans apply to your hosted waiting lobbies, not to the person's game account everywhere; level exceptions do not override them. The host is exempt.

Remove saved entries from the Lobby panel. Edit `filtered-words.txt` or `filtered-names.txt`, one literal phrase per line, then select **Reload moderation lists and filters**. `#` lines are comments. Each filter supports up to 128 phrases of 100 characters and 16 KB per file. The moderation list holds up to 512 entries. Invalid moderation files retain prior in-memory lists and prevent overwrite until corrected/reloaded. No external community ban list is imported.

## Alerts

Pop-up alerts normally last ten seconds. Accepted notifications remain in the current lobby's history after disappearing, up to 200 entries. History clears on leaving/changing lobby or **Clear alert history**; it is not a permanent cross-session archive. Pop-up queues and event intake are bounded to limit floods. **Show pop-up alerts** controls display; **Technical detail** switches between simpler text and diagnostic detail.

**Activity details** shows bounded join/message observations and recent incidents. Message counts concern the referenced player objects, not authenticated remote keyboard/menu input. Where known, alerts identify the affected target; otherwise they say unknown instead of guessing.

### Sender evidence

This review panel separates claimed actor, object owner, target and conflicting claims. Reported IDs and names can be forged. Blocking a malformed action and proving who sent it are separate decisions. **Clear sender evidence** also clears related-action analysis.

### Related suspicious actions

This is an observational foldout inside Alerts, not a separate menu tab or an automatic-ban detector. It groups existing flagged actions referring to the same lobby owner ID and spawned player object ID.

- At least three observations in at least two action families, spanning at least four seconds within a rolling 45-second window.
- At most one observation per family/object per two seconds; multiple checks on one message count once.
- Conflicting claims, missing player objects and shared-object targets are not used to blame a player.
- Normal round/meeting boundaries and existing vent, movement and ghost-role grace pause or clear relevant sequences.
- Evidence expires and resets with session/round/host changes, departure and object replacement; at most 256 observations and eight displayed groups.

The panel says **Sender UNKNOWN** because the referenced player could be a victim. Receipt order is not necessarily execution order. Correlation creates no points, bans, public messages, remote uploads or persistent identity records. Collection requires Cheat detection and Extra behaviour checks. No pattern does not mean no cheating.

## Players

The roster uses each player's visible in-game outfit color. Tap a name to scroll back to their details at the top. Available game-reported friend code, account ID, level and platform are displayed locally, not verified against a remote account service. Missing data stays unavailable; differing level records are labeled rather than silently borrowing another player's details.

Hosts can confirm **Kick selected player** or **Ban selected player**. Kick removes them from this lobby; Ban also prevents ordinary rejoining of that lobby. These are not global account bans. The host/local player is excluded. There is no deceptive timeout/ErrorKick punishment or non-host removal feature.

## Protection

| Guard | Purpose and boundaries |
| --- | --- |
| ErrorKick / ventilation guard | Filters covered suspicious ventilation sequences referencing players on this client. Requires Detection and UpdateSystem checks. Normal vents, vent cleaning and host moderation remain available. “ErrorKick” is not one uniquely identifiable network attack. |
| Check meeting results | Checks supported result messages for malformed counts, duplicate/invalid voters and related structure while retaining legal skip/dead-target values and supported role fields. Does not force outcomes or repair results processed elsewhere. |
| Filter invalid vote-kick requests | Filters covered malformed/replayed requests without disabling normal votes, vote-kicks or host bans. Claimed voters are not automatically punished. |
| Guard against unwanted teleports | Checks covered unexpected movement of your player with normal transition allowances. Cannot instantly restore everyone else's position or undo arbitrary actions. |
| Check Influencer image messages | Validates supported Influencer/SpiritGuide image messages before processing. Does not disable the legitimate roles. |
| Encrypted connection | Requests DTLS for your connection only. It is not IP masking, a VPN or protection against bandwidth-saturating attacks. |
| Lobby wall check (experimental) | Checks explicit lobby teleports against confidently traced walls/outer bounds and observes sustained positions beyond the loaded static-collision envelope. Does not snap back ordinary walking or ban solely for coordinates. Small wall-edge exploits may be missed. |
| Validate packets before use | Bounds supported message envelopes, nesting, lengths and payload reads before dispatch. |
| Block oversized messages | Rejects covered excessive message sizes to limit malformed-data handling. |
| Check message types | Validates supported game-data message types. Unknown semantics are not automatic proof of cheating. |
| Bound number decoding | Bounds packed-number parsing to limit malformed reads. |
| Guard voting-result memory | Bounds covered voting-result allocations. Does not prevent every crash or denial-of-service attack. |

Advanced safeguards show the blocked-packet count and latest reason. A blocked packet can be untrusted without having a verified sender. The local removal-request guard is also available under Lobby. None of these guards makes a genuine server removal reversible or protects traffic this client never receives.

## Detection and points

**Cheat detection** enables the detection layer; **Show detection alerts** controls its notifications; **Block invalid actions** rejects covered invalid messages before this client applies them. Turning blocking off can allow flagged actions through locally.

**Check platform inconsistencies** compares reported platform fields for contradictions. It does not scan a device, record hardware device IDs or prove which platform/menu someone actually uses.

**Extra behaviour checks** enables additional covered action-pattern checks and related-action review. The implementation checks observable behavior, not menu names or whether an Android/PC player installed a particular app.

Covered checks include supported role/state/target validation; vent, ladder and platform handling; task completion; kill/report/meeting requests; doors and sabotage; names, colors, levels and selected cosmetic/message constraints; scanner/animation replay; and action/message-rate patterns. Cosmetic values are not proof of ownership or purchase. Accepted server-authorized results must not be mistaken for unauthorized requests.

Movement observation compares sustained speed with expected movement and looks for repeated large jumps with tolerance for network catch-up. It excludes/resets around supported meetings, vents, ladders, platforms, role transitions and other normal movement contexts. A single correction or short burst is not enough to identify an attacker. Ordinary death/ejection ghost-role changes and supported Engineer/Influencer/SpiritGuide/Phantom/Shapeshifter behavior have dedicated allowances; this is not a guarantee against every false positive.

### Host response

- Minor attributable evidence: **50 points**; major attributable evidence: **100 points**.
- Default threshold: **100**, adjustable from **50 to 200**.
- **None:** records qualifying evidence without score-based removal.
- **Kick:** normal host removal at the threshold.
- **Ban:** normal host lobby ban at the threshold; the default response.

Confirmed exploit signatures can use a separate immediate-ban path, so selecting None is not a global disable-all-moderation switch. Unknown/unverified sender observations do not justify punitive points or a ban against the referenced player. Admission rules and manually saved bans are separate policies, not proof of cheating. Automatic removal requires host authority; the correlation panel never independently authorizes it.

**Individual message checks** exposes advanced RPC switches. Disabling one turns off that validation, not the player's ordinary ability. Use this for focused troubleshooting rather than assuming fewer alerts means better protection. Lower thresholds leave less room for mistakes.

## Settings and appearance

Preferences save automatically, checked about once per second, and also on normal shutdown/unload. This covers detection/guard switches, individual checks, response/threshold, lobby settings, alerts, size, opacity and window position. Check the menu's save status for failures. A crash can lose a just-made change. Open tabs, foldouts, typing drafts, pending confirmations and live evidence are temporary.

- **Reset all settings to defaults:** asks for confirmation, restores preferences and clears friend-code exceptions. Preserves written rules, loadouts, moderation lists and current alert history; does not undo bans. Minimum-level enforcement and auto-post rules return to disabled.
- **Menu size:** 0.75–1.5x, fitted to the screen.
- **Background opacity:** 35–100%, affecting menu, status badge and pop-ups; text/icons remain readable.
- **Reset appearance:** restores size, position, opacity and the fixed red theme. There is no accent-color selector.
- **Clear session evidence:** clears current scores and observation history, not saved exceptions or existing bans. Alert history has its own clear button.
- **Unload plugin:** confirmed action that stops its protections until the game restarts.

The interface uses the game's compatible loaded font when available, otherwise a readable fallback. Hover highlights borders rather than making the whole menu opaque. Branding uses a red X and the footer **by xav0dev - Boo**.

## Privacy and saved files

The default data directory is inside your game at `BepInEx/config/AU-Anticheat/`:

| File | Contents |
| --- | --- |
| `settings.json` | Saved anti-cheat/menu preferences. |
| `lobby-rules.txt` | Your written rules. |
| `lobby-loadouts.json` | Named normal-game option presets. |
| `moderation-list.json` | Your local friend-code ban/block/watch entries. |
| `filtered-words.txt`, `filtered-names.txt` | Your literal chat/name filter phrases. |

Friend-code exceptions also use `BepInEx/config/com.au.anticheat.cfg`. If this file does not yet exist, the first renamed startup imports the previous `com.xav.anticheat.cfg` without changing the original. An existing new configuration always wins; oversized old files are refused and import failures are logged. Other preferences remain in the unchanged `AU-Anticheat` data directory. These runtime files are not included in the source distribution. Keep local backups before reinstalling; reset is not a substitute for a backup.

The plugin does not add IP lookups, hardware-ID collection, remote process scans, automatic report uploads or third-party verification services. Session evidence/coordinates are bounded local observations. Diagnostic notifications can still be written to the ordinary **BepInEx logs**, including player names, event details and coordinates. Inspect and redact logs before sharing them.

Player details display identifiers already reported through the game. Saved moderation/exception lists intentionally retain friend codes locally. Opt-in/configurable public chat deliberately shares detection notices and completed-match observations with other players, who can record them. The project and menu identify the author as xav0dev - Boo; the project is not anonymous.

## Building and testing

Players install the DLL only. Developers use a .NET SDK capable of targeting .NET 6 and their own generated game/BepInEx libraries:

```powershell
$gamePath = 'C:/Program Files (x86)/Steam/steamapps/common/Among Us'
dotnet build source/AU-Anticheat.csproj -c Release "-p:GameDirectory=$gamePath"
dotnet run --project tests/ProtectionTests.csproj -c Release -- 'source/bin/Release/net6.0/AU-Anticheat.dll' "$gamePath/BepInEx/interop/Assembly-CSharp.dll"
dotnet run --project ui-tests/UiTests.csproj -c Release
dotnet run --project lobby-tests/LobbyTests.csproj -c Release
dotnet run --project role-tests/RoleTests.csproj -c Release
dotnet run --project movement-tests/MovementTests.csproj -c Release
```

Output: `source/bin/Release/net6.0/AU-Anticheat.dll`. `AU-Anticheat.sln` and `.csproj` files describe the build. Tests live beside `source/`, are not compiled into the plugin and are not installed in the game. Proprietary game libraries and excluded upstream cheat utilities are not distributed.

### Recorded beta verification

The 13 October 2026 renamed production Release build recorded zero warnings/errors and these passing assertion counts:

| Suite | Assertions | Scope |
| --- | ---: | --- |
| Protection/parser/metadata | 95,476 | Message structure, malformed-input corpus, bindings, related-action logic, branding and configuration migration. |
| UI | 441 | Menu behavior with managed Unity substitutes. |
| Lobby/chat/meeting | 613 | Admission, rules, summaries, cooldowns, moderation and meeting handling. |
| Roles/teleports | 105 | Supported role and movement-transition cases. |
| Movement/lobby boundary | 27 | Simulated geometry and movement safeguards. |

Some test substitutes emitted unused-field warnings. These results are a recorded check of the packaged code, not a claim that live multiplayer or every game update works. This beta 1 build uses AUAnticheat code identifiers and imports previous preferences when the renamed configuration does not yet exist. The author credit and required upstream attribution remain; detection behavior is unchanged by the rename.

Recorded DLL size: **224,768 bytes**. SHA-256:

```text
E9CD2E284A29CF14C4522FE1455EABCFF5AE5D97B9C6A495E4B66412A5766910
```

### Private-lobby checklist

Test with consenting players: joins and level loading; exceptions and paced bans; rules at different capacities and refills; manual chat near automatic messages; one post-match summary; save/restart/reset; loadout apply/delete; ordinary reports and emergency meetings; kills, doors and sabotage; Engineer vents and vent cleaning; shapeshift/revert; Influencer/SpiritGuide and ghost transitions; ladders/platforms; and delayed network updates. Confirm that ordinary play is not penalized and that non-host mode does not remove players.

Do not reproduce disruptive attacks against public players. Keep the exact error popup and a redacted log if something fails. Automated checks are regression tools, not a certification of universal protection.

## Reference review

These are historical design-review references, not bundled dependencies or endorsements. Selected public material was reviewed on 11–12 October 2026; no external cheat binary or private service was executed. The full original-fork credits and GPL notice are in [README.md](README.md#credits-and-license).

- [Hydra by MrDiamond64](https://github.com/MrDiamond64/Hydra): upstream source of this GPL-3.0 defensive fork.
- [BanMod](https://github.com/GiannBart/BanMod/tree/0dc37d20325289fb8efe30e866da522e78e5525b): targeted moderation/chat review and five-second summary-delay comparison; no private services imported.
- [Town of Host](https://github.com/tukasa0001/TownOfHost/blob/main/Patches/PlayerJoinAndLeftPatch.cs) and [its chat handling](https://github.com/tukasa0001/TownOfHost/blob/main/Patches/ChatCommandPatch.cs): timing/queue comparisons, not copied impersonation or chat-restriction bypasses. Current AU summaries use five seconds, not an older three-second setting.
- [Town of Host Enhanced](https://github.com/EnhancedNetwork/TownofHost-Enhanced/tree/3ab9cefd9a266245430701668ec3d355c2eb6057): selected admission, spam and protection concepts; custom-role assumptions and community ban services were not adopted.
- [BetterAmongUs](https://github.com/D1GQ/BetterAmongUs/tree/6c3df063881de3e33ac5740ad0b1f3090f5a7c4e/src/Patches/Gameplay/Anticheat): selected level initialization, removal pacing, voting, scanner and RPC checks. The separate archived BetterAmongUs-Public repository was metadata/reference discovery, not an imported implementation.
- [Impostor server ownership checks](https://github.com/Impostor/Impostor/blob/703915f06fcfd17bc8804d36dd67655671a66cf2/src/Impostor.Server/Net/Inner/InnerNetObject.Anticheat.cs) and [meeting-result serialization](https://github.com/Impostor/Impostor/blob/master/src/Impostor.Api/Net/Messages/Rpcs/Rpc23VotingComplete.cs): server-side authority and message-layout comparisons; client correlation cannot replace server authentication.
- [Grim violation configuration](https://github.com/GrimAnticheat/Grim/blob/2.0/common/src/main/resources/punishments/en.yml): bounded observation windows and separate alert/action concepts, not Minecraft rules copied into Among Us.
- [NuwaMenu sender fallback](https://github.com/extranig67/NuwaMenu/blob/432a94e4d8bfa31dadabdea7f77e6686b8e3d4f9/network/rpc_guard/NetworkGuard.RpcChecks.cs): recent-join association was rejected as a basis for automatic attribution.
- [wellsanticheat](https://github.com/somewhatpublicacc/wellsanticheat) and [AmongUS-AC-Engine](https://github.com/tatomodrekilaze/AmongUS-AC-Engine): scoped category/server-oriented comparisons, not evidence of universal official-server client protection.

EndlessHostRoles received discovery/overview review only. AmongUsRevamped, MalumMenu, TheOtherRoles/TheOtherHats, GameLogger and NLayer were not source-audited in this revision. NLayer is not required or bundled here. No claim is made to have researched every paid/private menu. Gameplay manipulation, synthetic server messages, remote reporting backends, ban bypasses and non-host forced removal were deliberately not imported.

```

`README.md`:

```md
# <img src="assets/au-anticheat-x.svg" width="32" height="36" alt="AU-Anticheat X badge"> AU-Anticheat — beta 1

**By xav0dev - Boo.** A defensive Among Us anti-cheat and lobby-moderation plugin for BepInEx IL2CPP.

AU-Anticheat checks supported game messages, provides local protection and alerts, and gives hosts normal moderation tools. It keeps normal gameplay in mind, including vents, meetings, role changes after death and delayed network updates.

**Experimental beta:** automated checks have passed, but live multiplayer testing is still required. It cannot guarantee a cheat-free lobby or protection against every removal attack. This project is not affiliated with or endorsed by Innersloth.

## Download

Look under [Releases](https://github.com/xav0dev/AU-Anticheat/releases) for the beta 1 download when available. Players need **AU-Anticheat.dll**, not the source or test folders. GitHub's automatic “Source code” downloads do not contain the compiled plugin.

Version: **1.0.0-beta.1**. The numeric loader/file version is **0.9.0.1**; that is expected.

## Requirements

- Among Us on Windows with a compatible x64 BepInEx IL2CPP installation.
- This build was compiled against Among Us **v19s / build 7489**, using BepInEx **6.0.0-be.788** interop libraries. Compatibility with other game or loader versions is not guaranteed.
- Other players do not need this plugin for ordinary gameplay. That does not mean your installation protects all of their clients.

The plugin is a Windows build. Checking messages from Android or console players does not make the DLL installable on those devices. Game and BepInEx libraries are not bundled.

## Installation

1. Close Among Us.
2. Download **BepInEx 6 Unity IL2CPP** from the [official BepInEx downloads](https://builds.bepinex.dev/projects/bepinex_be). This beta was compiled using **6.0.0-be.788**. Choose the Windows package matching your game executable: `Unity.IL2CPP-win-x64` for 64-bit or `Unity.IL2CPP-win-x86` for 32-bit. Do not choose Mono or BepInEx 5. This release targets the x64 setup listed above; other configurations are not verified.
3. Extract the BepInEx archive into the game folder containing `Among Us.exe`. On Steam, right-click Among Us, then select **Manage → Browse local files**. If a compatible BepInEx setup is already installed, skip extracting another copy. See the [official IL2CPP installation guide](https://docs.bepinex.dev/master/articles/user_guide/installation/unity_il2cpp.html) for help.
4. Launch the game once and let BepInEx generate its files. The first launch can take longer. Close the game afterward.
5. Download `AU-Anticheat.dll` from [Releases](https://github.com/xav0dev/AU-Anticheat/releases) and place it in the game's `BepInEx/plugins` folder.
6. Keep only one active copy of AU-Anticheat. Avoid other overlapping anti-cheat plugins.
7. Start the game and press **Insert** to open the menu. Check the status badge and `BepInEx/LogOutput.log` for startup errors.
8. Test normal gameplay with consenting players in a private lobby before relying on the beta.

Do not put source files, tests, reference projects or the entire source ZIP into the plugins folder. The AU-Anticheat download contains the plugin, not BepInEx or the game.

## Updating

1. Close Among Us and check that the new release supports your game and BepInEx versions.
2. Back up your current anti-cheat DLL **outside** `BepInEx/plugins`.
3. Back up and preserve `BepInEx/config/com.au.anticheat.cfg` and `BepInEx/config/AU-Anticheat/` to keep your settings and saved lists. Keep any older configuration files too; supported configuration migration runs automatically.
4. Download the new `AU-Anticheat.dll` from [Releases](https://github.com/xav0dev/AU-Anticheat/releases).
5. Replace the old DLL in `BepInEx/plugins`. Remove any duplicate or differently named older copies from that folder so only one version loads.
6. Launch the game, press **Insert**, and check your settings and startup log. Test in a private lobby before using the update publicly.

You do not need to reinstall BepInEx for every plugin update. Change it only when the release requirements call for a different compatible version.

## Using the menu

Press **Insert** to open or close it, **Escape** to close, or use its X button. Drag the top bar to move it and scroll inside the content panel.

- **Overview:** status and getting started.
- **Lobby:** entry levels, exceptions, saved rules, match summaries, loadouts and local chat moderation.
- **Alerts:** notification history and suspicious-action review.
- **Players:** color-coded player list, reported details and host moderation.
- **Protection:** covered packet, movement and removal safeguards.
- **Detection:** checks, evidence threshold and host response.
- **Settings:** saving, reset, appearance, session clearing and unload.

Read [Documentation.md](Documentation.md) for every feature, its settings and its limits.

## Troubleshooting and removal

- **INACTIVE or startup errors:** protections did not attach. Check game/loader compatibility and duplicate plugins. Do not assume the badge means all guards are working.
- **False detections or broken normal actions:** stop relying on that check, note the action and settings, and preserve the affected log. Restore your backed-up DLL if necessary.
- **Settings do not save:** check the in-menu save status and write access to the configuration folder.
- **Rules or results do not post:** check host authority, message length, lobby readiness and the shared chat cooldown. See the documentation before treating this as a failure.
- **Uninstall:** close the game and remove only `AU-Anticheat.dll` from `BepInEx/plugins`. Keep the configuration folder if you want your preferences for a later reinstall.

For a bug report, include game build, plugin version, host/non-host status and a short normal-gameplay reproduction. Redact personal information, friend/account IDs, IP addresses and lobby codes from logs or screenshots. Do not publish working crash/removal exploits or test them against public players.

## For developers

The repository contains the readable plugin source and separate regression-test folders. Tests are not built into the plugin and players do not need to install them. Build instructions, test scope and verification results are in [Documentation.md](Documentation.md#building-and-testing).

The assembly/display name is `AU-Anticheat`, the C# namespace is `AUAnticheat`, the entry point is `AUAnticheatPlugin`, and the plugin ID is `com.au.anticheat`. C# identifiers cannot contain hyphens. Historical names remain only in attribution, upgrade guidance, the previous-config lookup and rename-verification tests. Author credits are not project branding.

## Credits and license

AU-Anticheat is based on **[Hydra by MrDiamond64](https://github.com/MrDiamond64/Hydra)**. Hydra is licensed under **GPL-3.0**; this fork retains that license. The full license is in [LICENSE](LICENSE). Renaming the project does not remove its original attribution.

This fork preserves and hardens Hydra's anti-cheat and defensive message-validation work. Its build allowlist excludes upstream trolling, teleportation tools, role manipulation, immortality, device/version spoofing, sanction bypass and non-host forced-kick implementations. Defensive movement guards are distinct from teleportation cheat tools.

AU-Anticheat's additional work includes lobby-scoped evidence handling, message-rate checks, clearer authority boundaries, defensive moderation, local privacy controls, saved preferences and host-authorized lobby admission policies.

Public material from BanMod, Town of Host, Town of Host Enhanced, BetterAmongUs, Impostor, Grim and other projects informed targeted design reviews. Their implementations, assets, endpoints, private services and community ban lists were not bundled by those additions. The scope and source links are in [Documentation.md](Documentation.md#reference-review). No claim is made to have audited every menu or anti-cheat. An MIT notice on an unrelated component does not relicense this Hydra-derived fork.

Among Us belongs to Innersloth. AU-Anticheat is an independent community project, not an official Innersloth product.

```

`lobby-tests/Program.cs`:

```cs
using System.Reflection;
using AUAnticheat.anticheat;
using UnityEngine;
using InnerNet;
using Il2CppInterop.Runtime.InteropTypes.Arrays;
int assertions=0;
void Check(bool result,string name){assertions++;if(!result)throw new Exception(name);}
MethodInfo Hook(Type t,string nested,string method)=>t.GetNestedType(nested,BindingFlags.NonPublic)!.GetMethod(method,BindingFlags.NonPublic|BindingFlags.Static)!;
string saved="";
FriendCodeExceptions.Initialize("old#1234;BAD INPUT;OLD#1234",s=>saved=s);
Check(FriendCodeExceptions.Snapshot().Length==1,"loaded codes are normalized/deduplicated");
Check(FriendCodeExceptions.Add(" Test#1234 ")&&saved.Contains("test#1234"),"persist normalized exception");
Check(!FriendCodeExceptions.Add("test#1234")&&!FriendCodeExceptions.Add("<b>x#1234"),"duplicate/markup rejected");
Check(FriendCodeExceptions.Remove("TEST#1234")&&!saved.Contains("test#1234"),"remove exact exception");
for(int n=0;n<200;n++)FriendCodeExceptions.Add($"player#{n}");
Check(FriendCodeExceptions.Snapshot().Length==128,"exception storage bounded");
FriendCodeExceptions.Initialize("",null);
var client=AmongUsClient.Instance;
PlayerControl Add(int id,uint level,string code)
{
    var p=new PlayerControl {OwnerId=id,NetId=(uint)id,Data=new(){PlayerLevel=level,FriendCode=code,PlayerName=$"Player{id}"}};
    PlayerControl.AllPlayerControls.Add(p);client.Clients[id]=new(){Id=id,Character=p,PlayerLevel=level,FriendCode=code};return p;
}
var host=Add(1,0,"host#1111");PlayerControl.LocalPlayer=host;
var low=Add(2,1,"low#2222");
var levelTick=Hook(typeof(LobbyLevelPolicy),"ObserveLobby","Postfix");
void Scan(){Time.realtimeSinceStartup+=0.2f;levelTick.Invoke(null,null);}
LobbyLevelPolicy.Enabled=true; LobbyLevelPolicy.MinimumLevel=10;
client.Clients[2].IsBeingCreated=true; Scan();Check(client.Removals.Count==0,"loading record waits");
client.Clients[2].IsBeingCreated=false;client.Clients[2].PlayerLevel=2;Scan();Check(client.Removals.Count==0,"conflicting level waits");
client.Clients[2].PlayerLevel=1;low.Data.FriendCode="";Scan();Check(client.Removals.Count==0,"unknown friend code waits for exceptions");
low.Data.FriendCode="low#2222";Scan();Check(client.Removals.Count==0,"enabling rule gives lobby time to settle");
Time.realtimeSinceStartup+=2;Scan();
Check(client.Removals.SequenceEqual(new[]{(2,true)}),"ready sample after settling requests one ban, host exempt");
Scan();Check(client.Removals.Count==1,"no repeated ban request");
var exempt=Add(3,1,"friend#3333");FriendCodeExceptions.Add("friend#3333");Scan();Check(client.Removals.Count==1,"friend-code exception bypasses only admission");
FriendCodeExceptions.Remove("friend#3333");Scan();Check(client.Removals.Count==1,"second removal waits for departure of first");
low.Data.Disconnected=true;Scan();Check(client.Removals.Count==1,"departure starts cooldown");
Time.realtimeSinceStartup+=2;Scan();Check(client.Removals.Last()==(3,true),"removing exception re-enables paced admission");
low.Data.Disconnected=false;
client.AmHost=false;var nonhost=Add(4,1,"guest#4444");Scan();Check(client.Removals.Count==2,"nonhost never removes players");client.AmHost=true;
GameManager.Instance.GameHasStarted=true;Scan();Check(client.Removals.Count==2,"no admission bans during round");GameManager.Instance.GameHasStarted=false;

Time.realtimeSinceStartup=1000;
var offer=Hook(typeof(LobbyExtension),"OnOffer","Postfix");var lobby=LobbyBehaviour.Instance;
LobbyExtension.Tick();Check(lobby.Requests==0,"no timer request without game offer");
offer.Invoke(null,new object[]{lobby,60,true,1,10});
LobbyExtension.Tick();Check(lobby.Requests==1,"requests as soon as extension is offered");
Time.realtimeSinceStartup=1001;LobbyExtension.Tick();LobbyExtension.Tick();Check(lobby.Requests==1,"no repeated request for same offer");
offer.Invoke(null,new object[]{lobby,20,true,1,11});LobbyExtension.Tick();Check(lobby.Requests==1,"changed offer cannot create request flood");
Time.realtimeSinceStartup=1100;offer.Invoke(null,new object[]{lobby,20,false,1,12});LobbyExtension.Tick();Check(lobby.Requests==1,"unavailable extension not requested");
offer.Invoke(null,new object[]{lobby,20,true,2,13});LobbyExtension.Tick();Check(lobby.Requests==1,"wrong host not requested");
offer.Invoke(null,new object[]{lobby,20,true,1,14});client.GameId=43;LobbyExtension.Tick();Check(lobby.Requests==1,"old lobby offer cannot affect new game");client.GameId=42;
Hook(typeof(LobbyExtension),"OnExtended","Postfix").Invoke(null,new object[]{lobby});Check(!LobbyExtension.Status.Contains("game confirmed"),"late confirmation for stale game state ignored");
offer.Invoke(null,new object[]{lobby,60,true,1,15});LobbyExtension.Tick();
Time.realtimeSinceStartup+=11;LobbyExtension.Tick();Check(LobbyExtension.Status.Contains("No extension confirmation"),"request without acknowledgement warns instead of promising extension");
Hook(typeof(LobbyExtension),"OnExtended","Postfix").Invoke(null,new object[]{lobby});Check(LobbyExtension.Status.Contains("game confirmed"),"valid extension confirmation is separate from request");

var round=new RoundLedger();round.Begin(10);round.Observe(1,"<b>Name\n", "Phantom",true);round.Observe(2,"Crew", "Crewmate",false);
round.Kill(1,2);round.Kill(1,2);round.Kill(1,1);Check(round.Lines().Count==0,"no report or role leak during live game");
round.End(135,3);Check(round.Lines()[0].Contains("Impostors won")&&round.Lines()[0].Contains("2m 5s"),"correct winner/duration");
Check(round.Lines()[1].Contains("1 kill(s)")&&!round.Lines()[1].Contains('<'),"kill deduplication and safe chat text");
for(int reason=0;reason<=8;reason++)Check(RoundLedger.Winner(reason)==(new[]{0,1,5,7}.Contains(reason)?"Crewmates":"Impostors"),"native outcome mapping");
round.Begin(100);Check(round.Lines().Count==0,"next round discards previous report");

host.Data.RoleType=RoleTypes.Shapeshifter;client.GameState=InnerNetClient.GameStates.Started;GameManager.Instance.GameHasStarted=true;
host.Data.RoleWhenAlive=RoleTypes.Crewmate; // A stale alive-role field must not override a living role.
LobbyChat.Clear();PlayerControl.Sent.Clear();RoundReports.Begin();
var killPrefix=Hook(typeof(RoundReports),"OnKillResult","Prefix");var killPostfix=Hook(typeof(RoundReports),"OnKillResult","Postfix");
object[] killArguments={low,MurderResultFlags.Succeeded,false};killPrefix.Invoke(null,killArguments);killPostfix.Invoke(null,new object[]{host,low,killArguments[2]});killPostfix.Invoke(null,new object[]{host,low,killArguments[2]});
RoundReports.Tick();LobbyChat.Tick();Check(PlayerControl.Sent.Count==0,"production report never sends during live gameplay");
var end=Hook(typeof(RoundReports),"OnEnd","Prefix");Time.realtimeSinceStartup+=125;end.Invoke(null,new object[]{new EndGameResult{GameOverReason=GameOverReason.CrewmatesByTask}});
RoundReports.Tick();LobbyChat.Tick();Check(PlayerControl.Sent.Count==0,"end event alone does not leak report before waiting lobby");
client.GameState=InnerNetClient.GameStates.Joined;GameManager.Instance.GameHasStarted=false;
client.Clients[1].IsBeingCreated=true;RoundReports.Tick();Time.realtimeSinceStartup+=10;RoundReports.Tick();LobbyChat.Tick();
Check(PlayerControl.Sent.Count==0,"return waits for host spawn to finish");client.Clients[1].IsBeingCreated=false;
RoundReports.Tick();Time.realtimeSinceStartup+=2;RoundReports.Tick();LobbyChat.Tick();Check(PlayerControl.Sent.Count==0,"two seconds is too early for result");
var chat=HudManager.Instance.Chat;HudManager.Instance.Chat=null;RoundReports.Tick();Time.realtimeSinceStartup+=10;
HudManager.Instance.Chat=chat;RoundReports.Tick();Time.realtimeSinceStartup+=2;RoundReports.Tick();LobbyChat.Tick();
Check(PlayerControl.Sent.Count==0,"HUD interruption restarts continuous readiness delay");
LobbyBehaviour.Instance.Id++;RoundReports.Tick();Time.realtimeSinceStartup+=2;RoundReports.Tick();LobbyChat.Tick();
Check(PlayerControl.Sent.Count==0,"new lobby instance restarts delay");
Time.realtimeSinceStartup+=3;RoundReports.Tick();chat.timeSinceLastMessage=0;LobbyChat.Tick();
Check(PlayerControl.Sent.Count==0,"ready results wait for the shared manual chat cooldown");
Time.realtimeSinceStartup+=3;LobbyChat.Tick();Check(PlayerControl.Sent.Count==0,"native cooldown also has a quarter-second safety margin");
Time.realtimeSinceStartup+=0.25f;LobbyChat.Tick();Check(PlayerControl.Sent.Count==1&&chat.timeSinceLastMessage==0,"automatic result consumes normal chat cooldown");
host.Data.IsDead=true;Time.realtimeSinceStartup+=5;RoundReports.Tick();LobbyChat.Tick();Check(PlayerControl.Sent.Count==1,"stale dead host cannot send queued results");
host.Data.IsDead=false;RoundReports.Tick();Time.realtimeSinceStartup+=3;RoundReports.Tick();LobbyChat.Tick();
Check(PlayerControl.Sent.Count==1 && PlayerControl.Sent[0].Contains("Crewmates won")&&PlayerControl.Sent[0].Contains("2:05")&&PlayerControl.Sent[0].Contains("Player1(S):1k")&&PlayerControl.Sent[0].Length<=100,"all production stats in one bounded message with role and deduplicated kills");
Check(AUAnticheat.AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Messages.Any(m=>m.Title=="Match results"&&m.Body.Contains("Shapeshifter")&&m.Body.Contains("1 kill(s)")),"full role and kill details retained in local Alerts");
RoundReports.Tick();Time.realtimeSinceStartup+=3.25f;LobbyChat.Tick();Check(PlayerControl.Sent.Count==1,"summary sent once");
LobbyChat.QueueNotice("old room");client.GameId=77;LobbyChat.Tick();Check(PlayerControl.Sent.Count==1,"chat cleared on room change");
LobbyChat.QueueNotice(new string('x',300));LobbyChat.Tick();Check(PlayerControl.Sent.Last().Length<=100,"public messages bounded");

var join=Hook(typeof(ActivityTracker),"OnJoin","Postfix");join.Invoke(null,new object[]{client.Clients[2]});
ActivityTracker.Observe(2,2,true);Check(ActivityTracker.Summary().Contains("1 messages"),"activity begins on join");ActivityTracker.Forget(2);Check(ActivityTracker.Count==0,"leaving removes session activity");
Check(MeetingResultRules.UniqueVoters(new byte[]{0,1,2})&&!MeetingResultRules.UniqueVoters(new byte[]{0,0}),"duplicate result voter rejected");
Check(MeetingResultRules.UniqueVoters(Array.Empty<byte>())&&!MeetingResultRules.UniqueVoters(new byte[]{255}),"empty result tolerated, sentinel voter rejected");
var resultGuard=Hook(typeof(MeetingProtection),"BeforeResults","Prefix");
var legal=new Il2CppStructArray<MeetingHud.VoterState>(new[]{new MeetingHud.VoterState{VoterId=0,VotedForId=253},new MeetingHud.VoterState{VoterId=1,VotedForId=255}});
Check((bool)resultGuard.Invoke(null,new object[]{legal})!,"skip/dead target sentinels retained");
var dup=new Il2CppStructArray<MeetingHud.VoterState>(new[]{new MeetingHud.VoterState{VoterId=1},new MeetingHud.VoterState{VoterId=1}});
Check(!(bool)resultGuard.Invoke(null,new object[]{dup})!,"production duplicate results blocked without attribution");
Check(PublicText.Simple("Protection","Rejected packet before processing: malformed vote-kick").Contains("nobody was automatically banned"),"clear non-accusatory notification");
Check(PublicText.Simple("Anti-cheat","Detected several unexpected disconnects").Contains("connection problem"),"disconnect alerts distinguish possible attacks from outages");
LobbyChat.QueueNotice("discard after disconnect");
Hook(typeof(RoundReports),"ClearLobbyServices","Postfix").Invoke(null,null);
int sentBeforeDisconnect=PlayerControl.Sent.Count;
Time.realtimeSinceStartup+=10;RoundReports.Tick();LobbyChat.Tick();
Check(PlayerControl.Sent.Count==sentBeforeDisconnect&&ActivityTracker.Count==0,"disconnect clears queued chat and reports even with reused lobby IDs");
LobbyChat.QueueNotice("cancelled notice");LobbyChat.ShareAlerts=false;LobbyChat.Tick();LobbyChat.ShareAlerts=true;
Check(PlayerControl.Sent.Count==sentBeforeDisconnect,"turning off shared alerts discards queued notices");
LobbyChat.QueueReport(new[]{"cancelled report"});RoundReports.Enabled=false;LobbyChat.Tick();RoundReports.Enabled=true;
Check(PlayerControl.Sent.Count==sentBeforeDisconnect,"turning off match reports discards queued reports");
// A full lobby must never produce a batch of admission bans.
client.GameId++;client.AmHost=true;client.GameState=InnerNetClient.GameStates.Joined;
GameManager.Instance.GameHasStarted=false;MeetingHud.Instance=null;
client.Clients.Clear();PlayerControl.AllPlayerControls.Clear();client.Removals.Clear();
host=Add(1,0,"host#1111");PlayerControl.LocalPlayer=host;
for(int id=2;id<=15;id++) Add(id,1,$"guest#{id}");
LobbyLevelPolicy.Enabled=true;LobbyLevelPolicy.MinimumLevel=10;Scan();Time.realtimeSinceStartup+=2;Scan();

```cs
using System.Reflection;
using UnityEngine;
using AUAnticheat.anticheat;

int count=0;
void Check(bool result,string reason) { count++; if(!result) throw new Exception(reason); }
var observe=typeof(LobbyMovementGuard).GetNestedType("ObserveLobby",BindingFlags.NonPublic)!.GetMethod("Postfix",BindingFlags.NonPublic|BindingFlags.Static)!;
var snap=typeof(LobbyMovementGuard).GetNestedType("BeforeLobbySnap",BindingFlags.NonPublic)!.GetMethod("Prefix",BindingFlags.NonPublic|BindingFlags.Static)!;
var player=new PlayerControl(); player.NetTransform=new CustomNetworkTransform { myPlayer=player }; PlayerControl.AllPlayerControls.Add(player);
void Tick(float time,float x) { Time.realtimeSinceStartup=time; player.transform.position=new Vector2(x,0); observe.Invoke(null,null); }
bool Incoming(float x,byte call=21)
{
    var reader=new Hazel.MessageReader { Buffer=BitConverter.GetBytes((short)(x*100)).Concat(new byte[4]).ToArray() };
    bool accepted=(bool)snap.Invoke(null,new object[]{player.NetTransform,call,reader})!;
    Check(reader.Position==0,"snap inspection preserves reader"); return accepted;
}
LobbyMovementGuard.Reset();
Tick(0,-1);
for(int i=1;i<=40;i++) Tick(i*0.1f,-1);
int casts=Physics2D.Casts;
Tick(4.1f,0); // brush wardrobe
Check(Incoming(1),"wall contact invalidates stale checkpoint");
Tick(4.2f,1); Tick(4.4f,2); Tick(4.6f,-2); // curved/interpolated ordinary movement
Check(Physics2D.Casts==casts && LobbyMovementGuard.BlockedSnaps==0,"ordinary motion never classified by straight-line wall casts");
Check(AUAnticheat.AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Count==0,"walking produces no wall-crossing warning");
Check(!Incoming(2),"explicit fresh cross-wall teleport still locally blocked");
Check(LobbyMovementGuard.BlockedSnaps==1,"explicit snap block counted");
Check(Incoming(0),"endpoint touching geometry is ambiguous, not blocked");
Check(Incoming(-3),"same-side snap allowed");
Check(Incoming(2,99),"unrelated RPC untouched");
Time.realtimeSinceStartup=6;
Check(Incoming(2),"stale checkpoint cannot reject a new snap");
Tick(6,-2); Tick(7.1f,-2); Tick(7.3f,-2); Tick(8.4f,-2);
for(int i=1;i<=12;i++) Tick(8.4f+i*0.1f,-2);
Physics2D.ThrowCast=true;
Check(Incoming(2),"physics query failure is not malformed-packet evidence");
Check(LobbyMovementGuard.Enabled,"geometry failure does not disable unrelated protections");
Physics2D.ThrowCast=false;
Tick(20,-2);for(int i=1;i<=20;i++) Tick(20+i*.1f,-2);
Check(!Incoming(-10),"explicit teleport outside geometry envelope locally rejected even without wall intersection");
Time.realtimeSinceStartup=30;player.transform.position=new(-10,0);
int notices=AUAnticheat.AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Count;
SceneBoundaryMonitor.Tick();Time.realtimeSinceStartup=34;SceneBoundaryMonitor.Tick();
Check(AUAnticheat.AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Count==notices,"new player has boundary settling grace");
for(int i=0;i<=25;i++) { Time.realtimeSinceStartup=35+i*.1f;SceneBoundaryMonitor.Tick(); }
Check(AUAnticheat.AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Count==notices+1 && SenderEvidence.Summary().Contains("Target"),"sustained out-of-bounds position gives one target-labelled review alert");
SceneBoundaryMonitor.Tick();Check(AUAnticheat.AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Count==notices+1,"boundary alerts throttled");
player.Data.Disconnected=true;Time.realtimeSinceStartup+=20;SceneBoundaryMonitor.Tick();
Check(AUAnticheat.AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Count==notices+1,"disconnected players excluded");player.Data.Disconnected=false;
LobbyBehaviour.Instance.Colliders=Array.Empty<Collider2D>();Time.realtimeSinceStartup+=2;
Check(!SceneBoundaryMonitor.OutsideLobby(new(-10,0)),"missing collision geometry cannot block valid traffic");
SceneBoundaryMonitor.Tick();Check(AUAnticheat.AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Count==notices+1,"missing geometry cannot accuse player");
LobbyBehaviour.Instance=null;ShipStatus.Instance=new();MovementContext.Stable=false;Time.realtimeSinceStartup+=10;
for(int i=0;i<80;i++) { Time.realtimeSinceStartup+=.1f;SceneBoundaryMonitor.Tick(); }
Check(AUAnticheat.AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Count==notices+1,"vent/meeting/transition instability excluded in game");
Console.WriteLine($"PASS: {count} production lobby-guard assertions with simulated geometry; not live Unity physics.");

namespace HarmonyLib
{
    public class HarmonyPatch : Attribute { public HarmonyPatch(Type t, string n) { } }
    public class HarmonyPriority : Attribute { public HarmonyPriority(int n) { } }
    public static class Priority { public const int First = 800; }
}
namespace UnityEngine
{
    public struct Vector2
    {
        public float x,y; public Vector2(float x,float y) { this.x=x; this.y=y; }
        public static Vector2 operator -(Vector2 a, Vector2 b) => new(a.x-b.x,a.y-b.y);
        public static Vector2 operator +(Vector2 a, Vector2 b) => new(a.x+b.x,a.y+b.y);
        public static float Distance(Vector2 a, Vector2 b) => MathF.Sqrt((a.x-b.x)*(a.x-b.x)+(a.y-b.y)*(a.y-b.y));
    }
    public class Transform { public Vector2 position; }
    public enum RigidbodyType2D { Static, Dynamic }
    public class Rigidbody2D { public RigidbodyType2D bodyType; }
    public class Component
    {
        public int Id=1; public int GetInstanceID()=>Id;
        public Collider2D[] Colliders={ new(),new() };
        public T[] GetComponentsInChildren<T>()=>Colliders.Cast<T>().ToArray();
    }
    public class GameObject { public int layer; }
    public struct Bounds
    {
        public Vector2 min,max;
        public void Encapsulate(Bounds other) { min=new(Math.Min(min.x,other.min.x),Math.Min(min.y,other.min.y)); max=new(Math.Max(max.x,other.max.x),Math.Max(max.y,other.max.y)); }
    }
    public class Collider2D { public bool enabled=true, isTrigger; public Rigidbody2D attachedRigidbody; public GameObject gameObject=new(); public Bounds bounds=new(){min=new(-5,-5),max=new(5,5)}; }
    public struct RaycastHit2D { public Collider2D collider; public float fraction, distance; }
    public static class Physics2D
    {
        public static int Casts;
        public static bool ThrowCast;
        private static readonly Collider2D wall = new();
        public static Collider2D[] OverlapCircleAll(Vector2 p,float r,int mask) => Math.Abs(p.x)<0.1f ? new[]{wall} : Array.Empty<Collider2D>();
        public static RaycastHit2D[] LinecastAll(Vector2 a,Vector2 b,int mask)
        {
            Casts++;
            if(ThrowCast) throw new InvalidOperationException("scene geometry unavailable");
            return a.x*b.x < 0 ? new[]{ new RaycastHit2D { collider=wall, fraction=0.5f, distance=Vector2.Distance(a,b)/2 } } : Array.Empty<RaycastHit2D>();
        }
    }
    public static class Time { public static float realtimeSinceStartup; }

`role-tests/Program.cs`:

```cs
using System.Reflection;
using AUAnticheat.anticheat;
using AmongUs.GameOptions;
using Il2CppInterop.Runtime.InteropTypes.Arrays;
int count=0;
void Check(bool v,string message){count++;if(!v)throw new Exception(message);}
MethodInfo Hook(Type t,string name)=>t.GetNestedType(name,BindingFlags.NonPublic)!.GetMethod("Prefix",BindingFlags.NonPublic|BindingFlags.Static)!;
var decode=Hook(typeof(InfluencerProtection),"BeforeInfluencerDecode");
var images=Hook(typeof(InfluencerProtection),"BeforeInfluencerImages");
var role=Hook(typeof(GhostRoleTransitionMonitor),"OnGhostRoleAssigned");
var feed=new SocialMediumFeedSystem();
bool Display(params byte[] list)=>(bool)images.Invoke(null,new object[]{feed,new Il2CppStructArray<byte>(list)})!;
InfluencerProtection.Reset();
Check(Display(0)&&Display(0,1)&&Display(0,1,2),"all legal message sizes reach feed");
Check(!Display()&&!Display(0,1,2,0),"invalid image counts blocked before display");
Check(!Display(3)&&!Display(255),"out of catalog indices blocked");
Check(Display(2,2),"repeated image selection not invented as a cheat");
feed.PossibleSpriteList=null!;
Check(!Display(0),"uninitialized catalog protected without player accusation");
feed.PossibleSpriteList=new(){new(),new(),new()};
InfluencerProtection.Reset();
for(int n=0;n<64;n++)Check(Display(0),"normal burst accepted");
Check(!Display(0),"feed flood stopped before rendering");
feed.Id++;
Check(Display(0),"recreated feed does not inherit previous limit");
InfluencerProtection.Reset();
Check(Display(0),"lobby reset clears resource limit");
var reader=new Hazel.MessageReader {Buffer=new byte[]{99,99,2,0,1},Offset=1,Position=1,Length=4};
Check((bool)decode.Invoke(null,new object[]{reader})!,"native entry permits legal nonzero-offset buffer");
Check(reader.Position==1,"native entry preserves cursor");
reader.Buffer[2]=255;
Check(!(bool)decode.Invoke(null,new object[]{reader})!,"native entry blocks invalid length");
Check(reader.Position==1,"rejected message preserves cursor");

var player=new PlayerControl();
foreach(var ghost in new[]{RoleTypes.SpiritGuide,RoleTypes.GuardianAngel,RoleTypes.CrewmateGhost,RoleTypes.ImpostorGhost})
{
    Anticheat.Reset(); player.Data.RoleType=RoleTypes.Crewmate; player.Data.IsDead=false;
    role.Invoke(null,new object[]{player,ghost});
    Check(Anticheat.GhostGrace&&Anticheat.MoveGrace,"incoming ghost role grants grace before death replication");
    Check(!MovementContext.StableLivingPlayer(player),"grace covers old living role snapshot");
    Anticheat.Reset(); player.Data.RoleType=ghost;
    Check(!MovementContext.StableLivingPlayer(player),"ghost role excluded even before IsDead arrives");
}
player.Data.RoleType=RoleTypes.Crewmate; Anticheat.Reset();
Check(MovementContext.StableLivingPlayer(player),"stable live player inspected");
void ContextCase(Action set,Action clear,string reason){set();Check(!MovementContext.StableLivingPlayer(player),reason);clear();}
ContextCase(()=>player.Data.IsDead=true,()=>player.Data.IsDead=false,"death excluded");
ContextCase(()=>player.roleAssigned=false,()=>player.roleAssigned=true,"uninitialized role excluded");
ContextCase(()=>player.inVent=true,()=>player.inVent=false,"engineer/vent movement excluded");
ContextCase(()=>player.onLadder=true,()=>player.onLadder=false,"ladder excluded");
ContextCase(()=>player.inMovingPlat=true,()=>player.inMovingPlat=false,"platform excluded");
ContextCase(()=>player.MyPhysics.DoingCustomAnimation=true,()=>player.MyPhysics.DoingCustomAnimation=false,"custom animation excluded");
ContextCase(()=>MeetingHud.Instance=new(),()=>MeetingHud.Instance=null,"meeting excluded");
ContextCase(()=>Anticheat.MeetingGrace=true,()=>Anticheat.MeetingGrace=false,"post-meeting grace excluded");
ContextCase(()=>Anticheat.RoundGrace=true,()=>Anticheat.RoundGrace=false,"round grace excluded");
ContextCase(()=>LobbyBehaviour.Instance=new(),()=>LobbyBehaviour.Instance=null,"lobby excluded");
ContextCase(()=>GameManager.Instance.GameHasStarted=false,()=>GameManager.Instance.GameHasStarted=true,"round ended excluded");
var snap=new AUAnticheat.anticheat.rpc.SnapTo();
bool Jump(){Anticheat.MoveGrace=false;return snap.Validate(player,new Hazel.MessageReader {Buffer=new byte[]{20,0,0,0,1,0},Length=6});}
Check(!Jump(),"unexpected large snap rejected by local authority before applying");
player.Data.RoleType=RoleTypes.SpiritGuide;
Check(Jump(),"Influencer snap not rejected as living teleport");
player.Data.RoleType=RoleTypes.Crewmate; player.inVent=true;
Check(Jump(),"vent snap allowed");player.inVent=false;
AmongUsClient.Instance.AmHost=false;
Check(Jump(),"observer does not override authoritative remote state");AmongUsClient.Instance.AmHost=true;
AUAnticheat.Utilities.ServerAuthority=true;
Check(Jump(),"official server correction allowed");
Console.WriteLine($"PASS: {count} production role/teleport adapter assertions with managed doubles; not live native gameplay.");

`source/AUAnticheatPlugin.cs`:

```cs
using BepInEx;
using BepInEx.Logging;
using BepInEx.Unity.IL2CPP;
using HarmonyLib;
using AUAnticheat.ui;
using UnityEngine;

namespace AUAnticheat;

[BepInPlugin("com.au.anticheat", "AU-Anticheat", "0.9.0.1")]
[BepInProcess("Among Us.exe")]
internal class AUAnticheatPlugin : BasePlugin
{
	internal const string DisplayVersion = "1.0.0-beta.1";
	internal static new ManualLogSource Log;
	private static readonly Harmony harmony = new Harmony(MyPluginInfo.PLUGIN_GUID);

	private static MainUI mainUI;
	private static StatusIndicator statusIndicator;
	public static NotificationManager notifications;
	internal static bool ProtectionsAttached { get; private set; }

	public override void Load()
	{
		Log = base.Log;
		Log.LogInfo($"AU-Anticheat {DisplayVersion} (pre-release)");
		ProtectionsAttached = false;
		try
		{
			if(anticheat.ConfigMigration.ImportIfMissing(Paths.ConfigPath))
			{
				Config.Reload();
				Log.LogInfo("Imported previous plugin preferences into the AU-Anticheat configuration.");
			}
		}
		catch(System.Exception error)
		{
			Log.LogWarning($"Previous configuration could not be imported ({error.GetType().Name}). Original file retained; review lobby preferences.");
		}
		var exceptions = Config.Bind("Admission", "FriendCodeExceptions", "", "Semicolon-separated friend codes exempt only from the minimum-level rule (maximum 128).");
		anticheat.FriendCodeExceptions.Initialize(exceptions.Value, value => exceptions.Value = value);
		var autoRules = Config.Bind("LobbyRules", "Enabled", false, "Post saved rules once after a stable full waiting lobby (any supported capacity) reaches the chosen delay. Host only.");
		var rulesDelay = Config.Bind("LobbyRules", "FullLobbySeconds", 10, "Continuous full-lobby delay in seconds (1-120).");
		anticheat.LobbyRules.Initialize(System.IO.Path.Combine(Paths.ConfigPath, "AU-Anticheat"), autoRules.Value, rulesDelay.Value,
			(use, seconds) => { autoRules.Value = use; rulesDelay.Value = seconds; });

		mainUI = AddComponent<MainUI>();
		statusIndicator = AddComponent<StatusIndicator>();
		notifications = AddComponent<NotificationManager>();
		anticheat.SavedSettings.Initialize(System.IO.Path.Combine(Paths.ConfigPath, "AU-Anticheat"));
		anticheat.LobbyLoadouts.Initialize(System.IO.Path.Combine(Paths.ConfigPath, "AU-Anticheat"));
		anticheat.ModerationStore.Initialize(System.IO.Path.Combine(Paths.ConfigPath, "AU-Anticheat"));

		try
		{
			harmony.PatchAll();
			ProtectionsAttached = true;
		}
		catch(System.Exception exception)
		{
			anticheat.Anticheat.Enabled = false;
			Log.LogError($"Protection startup failed: {exception}");
			// PatchAll may have attached earlier hooks before a later hook failed.
			try { harmony.UnpatchSelf(); }
			catch(System.Exception cleanupError) { Log.LogError($"Partial-hook cleanup failed: {cleanupError}"); }
			notifications.Send("Startup Error", "Protections are inactive: a game hook failed to load. Check BepInEx/LogOutput.log and restart with a compatible build.", 9999);
			throw;
		}

		Log.LogInfo($"Plugin {MyPluginInfo.PLUGIN_GUID} has loaded!");
	}

	public static void Eject()
	{
		anticheat.SavedSettings.Save();
		ProtectionsAttached = false;
		harmony.UnpatchSelf();

		notifications.ClearNotifications();

		Object.Destroy(mainUI);
		Object.Destroy(statusIndicator);
		Object.Destroy(notifications);

		HideBuiltInModStamp();
	}

	private static void HideBuiltInModStamp()
	{
		if(ModManager.Instance != null && ModManager.Instance.ModStamp != null)
		{
			ModManager.Instance.ModStamp.enabled = false;
		}
	}

	[HarmonyPatch(typeof(ModManager), nameof(ModManager.ShowModStamp))]
	private static class HideModStamp
	{
		private static bool Prefix()
		{
			HideBuiltInModStamp();
			return false;
		}
	}

	[HarmonyPatch(typeof(MainMenuManager), nameof(MainMenuManager.Awake))]
	class OnGameLoad
	{
		public static void Postfix()
		{
			anticheat.Anticheat.ResetSession();
			HideBuiltInModStamp();
			Log.LogInfo(ProtectionsAttached ? "AU-Anticheat protections are attached" : "AU-Anticheat protections are inactive");
		}
	}
}

`source/Utilities.cs`:

```cs
using AmongUs.GameOptions;
using InnerNet;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using static AUAnticheat.network.Constants;

namespace AUAnticheat
{
	internal static class Utilities
	{
		private static readonly HashSet<SystemTypes> SkeldSabotages = new HashSet<SystemTypes>
		{
			SystemTypes.Reactor, SystemTypes.LifeSupp, SystemTypes.Electrical, SystemTypes.Comms
		};

		private static readonly HashSet<SystemTypes> PolusSabotages = new HashSet<SystemTypes>
		{
			SystemTypes.Laboratory, SystemTypes.Electrical, SystemTypes.Comms
		};

		private static readonly HashSet<SystemTypes> AirshipSabotages = new HashSet<SystemTypes>
		{
			SystemTypes.HeliSabotage, SystemTypes.Electrical, SystemTypes.Comms
		};

		private static readonly HashSet<SystemTypes> FungleSabotages = new HashSet<SystemTypes>
		{
			SystemTypes.Reactor, SystemTypes.Comms, SystemTypes.MushroomMixupSabotage
		};

		public static MapNames GetCurrentMap()
		{
			if(ShipStatus.Instance == null)
			{
				if(AmongUsClient.Instance.NetworkMode == NetworkModes.FreePlay)
				{
					return (MapNames)AmongUsClient.Instance.TutorialMapId;
				}

				return (MapNames)GameOptionsManager.Instance.CurrentGameOptions.MapId;
			}

			return (SpawnType)ShipStatus.Instance.SpawnId switch
			{
				SpawnType.SkeldShipStatus => MapNames.Skeld,
				SpawnType.DleksShipStatus => MapNames.Dleks,
				SpawnType.MiraShipStatus => MapNames.MiraHQ,
				SpawnType.PolusShipStatus => MapNames.Polus,
				SpawnType.AirshipShipStatus => MapNames.Airship,
				SpawnType.FungleShipStatus => MapNames.Fungle,
				_ => MapNames.Skeld
			};
		}

		public static bool IsAnticheatPresent()
		{
			if(Constants.IsVersionModded() || PlayerControl.LocalPlayer == null || PlayerControl.LocalPlayer.Data == null) return false;
			return PlayerControl.LocalPlayer.Data.OwnerId != (int)OwnerIds.Host;
		}

		public static bool IsValidSabotage(SystemTypes system)
		{
			return GetCurrentMap() switch
			{
				MapNames.Skeld or MapNames.Dleks or MapNames.MiraHQ => SkeldSabotages.Contains(system),
				MapNames.Polus => PolusSabotages.Contains(system),
				MapNames.Airship => AirshipSabotages.Contains(system),
				MapNames.Fungle => FungleSabotages.Contains(system),
				_ => false
			};
		}
	}
}

```

`source/anticheat/ActivityTracker.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using HarmonyLib;
using InnerNet;
using UnityEngine;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
	internal static class ActivityTracker
	{
		private sealed class Activity { public float Joined; public uint? ObjectId; public int Messages; }
		private static readonly Dictionary<int, Activity> Players = new();
		private static string session = "";
		public static void Reset() { Players.Clear(); session = ""; }
		public static int Count { get { Ensure(); return Players.Count; } }
		private static void Ensure()
		{
			string current = AmongUsClient.Instance == null ? "offline" : $"{AmongUsClient.Instance.GameId}:{AmongUsClient.Instance.HostId}";
			if(current == session) return;
			session = current; Players.Clear();
		}
		public static void Forget(int id) { Ensure(); Players.Remove(id); }
		public static void Observe(int id, uint? netId, bool message)
		{
			Ensure();
			if(!Players.TryGetValue(id, out var state))
			{
				if(Players.Count >= 64) return;
				Players[id] = state = new Activity { Joined = Time.realtimeSinceStartup, ObjectId = netId };
			}
			if(netId.HasValue && state.ObjectId.HasValue && state.ObjectId != netId)
				Players[id] = state = new Activity { Joined = Time.realtimeSinceStartup, ObjectId = netId };
			if(netId.HasValue) state.ObjectId = netId;
			if(message) state.Messages = Math.Min(1000000, state.Messages + 1);
		}
		public static string Summary()
		{
			Ensure(); var rows = new List<string>();
			foreach(var pair in Players) rows.Add($"Lobby ID {pair.Key}: observed {Math.Max(0, (int)(Time.realtimeSinceStartup - pair.Value.Joined))}s, {pair.Value.Messages} messages affecting its objects.");
			return string.Join("\n", rows);
		}
		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(AmongUsClient), nameof(AmongUsClient.OnPlayerJoined))]
		private static class OnJoin
		{
			private static void Postfix(ClientData __0)
			{
				if(__0 == null) return;
				Forget(__0.Id); Observe(__0.Id, null, false);
			}
		}
		public static void Tick()
		{
			Ensure();
			foreach(var player in PlayerControl.AllPlayerControls)
				if(player != null && player.Data != null && !player.Data.Disconnected) Observe(player.OwnerId, player.NetId, false);
		}
	}
}

```

		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(GameManager), nameof(GameManager.StartGame))]

		{
			private static void Postfix()
			{
				ResetSession();
				SenderEvidence.PauseSequences(null, 15);
				roundStartedUtc = DateTime.UtcNow;
				StartEmergencyCooldown();
				RoundReports.Begin();
			}
		}

		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(ExileController), nameof(ExileController.WrapUp))]

		{
			private static void Postfix()
			{

				GrantMeetingTransitionGrace(3);
				foreach(var player in PlayerControl.AllPlayerControls) GrantMovementGrace(player, 4);
			}
		}

		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(GameData), nameof(GameData.ShowNotification))]
		private static class OnDisconnectNotification
		{
			private static void Postfix(string playerName, DisconnectReasons reason)
			{
				ObserveDisconnect(playerName, reason);
			}
		}

		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(AmongUsClient), nameof(AmongUsClient.OnPlayerLeft))]
		private static class OnPlayerLeft
		{
			private static DateTime windowStart;
			private static int logged;
			private static void Prefix(ClientData __0, DisconnectReasons __1)
			{
				if(__0 == null) return;
				DateTime now = DateTime.UtcNow;
				if((now - windowStart).TotalSeconds >= 5) { windowStart = now; logged = 0; }
				if(++logged > 32) return; // Bound diagnostic output during a raid.
				var client = AmongUsClient.Instance;
				AUAnticheatPlugin.Log.LogWarning($"[Departure {now:O}] Affected: {PublicText.Name(__0.PlayerName)} (lobby ID {__0.Id}); reason={__1}; localHost={client?.AmHost}; state={client?.GameState}; sender=UNKNOWN. Last local packet block (may be unrelated): {PacketPreflight.LastBlockReason}.");
			}
			// Bind by argument index, not the generated interop parameter name.
			private static void Postfix(ClientData __0)
			{
				if(__0 == null) return;
				Violations.Remove(__0.Id);
				SenderEvidence.ForgetSequences(__0.Id);
				ActivityTracker.Forget(__0.Id);
				MovementMonitor.Forget(__0.Id);
				LobbyMovementGuard.Forget(__0.Id);
				VoteKickGuard.Forget(__0.Id);
				LobbyLevelPolicy.Forget(__0.Id);
			}
		}
