```cs
		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(NetworkedPlayerInfo), nameof(NetworkedPlayerInfo.HandleRpc))]
		private static class OnPlayerInfoRpc
		{
			private static bool Prefix(NetworkedPlayerInfo __instance, byte callId)
			{
				if(!Enabled || callId != (byte)RpcCalls.SetTasks) return true;
				if(AmongUsClient.Instance == null) return true;
				// Server task assignments can precede local role/round replication.
				if(Utilities.IsAnticheatPresent()) return true;

				PlayerControl target = __instance.Object;
				using var evidence = SenderEvidence.Begin("Player tasks", callId, target?.NetId, target?.OwnerId, SafeName(target));
				if(AmongUsClient.Instance.AmHost && !Utilities.IsAnticheatPresent())
				{
					Flag("Blocked a remote host-only SetTasks message. The affected player object does not identify its sender; no player penalized.");
					return false;
				}

				if(LobbyBehaviour.Instance == null && IsRoundStartGrace()) return true;

				string timing = LobbyBehaviour.Instance != null ? "in the lobby" : "after the assignment window";
				Flag($"Blocked a task assignment for {SafeName(target)} {timing}. Sender unverified; no player penalized.");
				return false;
			}
		}

		private static bool HandleRpc(Type sourceNetObject, PlayerControl player, RpcCalls rpc, MessageReader reader)
		{
			EnsureSession();
			using var evidence = SenderEvidence.Begin(sourceNetObject.Name, (byte)rpc,
				player != null ? player.NetId : null, player != null ? player.OwnerId : null,
				player != null ? SafeName(player) + "'s player object (not proof of who sent it)" : "shared " + sourceNetObject.Name + " object; individual target unknown", rpc.ToString());
			if(!Enabled || !EnhancedMenuBehaviorChecks || (player != null && player.Data == null) ||
				MeetingHud.Instance != null || HasMeetingTransitionGrace() ||
				(GameManager.Instance != null && GameManager.Instance.GameHasStarted && IsRoundStartGrace()))
				SenderEvidence.PauseSequences(player?.OwnerId, 2);
			if(player != null) ActivityTracker.Observe(player.OwnerId, player.NetId, true);
			return HandleRpcCore(sourceNetObject, player, rpc, reader);
		}

		private static bool HandleRpcCore(Type sourceNetObject, PlayerControl player, RpcCalls rpc, MessageReader reader)
		{
			// Structural protection runs before authority/grace fast paths and remains
			// available even with semantic detection switched off.
			if(AUAnticheat.features.Protections.PreflightPackets && !PacketPreflight.ValidateRpc(rpc, reader)) return false;
			NoteRpcTarget(player, rpc, reader);
			if(sourceNetObject == typeof(VoteBanSystem) && rpc == RpcCalls.AddVote &&
				AUAnticheat.features.Protections.FilterVoteKickReplays && !VoteKickGuard.Validate(reader, id => SafeName(AmongUsClient.Instance?.FindClientById(id)?.Character))) return false;
			if(!Enabled || !RpcHandlers.TryGetValue(rpc, out RpcCheck rpcCheck) || !rpcCheck.Enabled) return true;

			// Player network objects can exist briefly before their replicated Data is
			// initialized. State-based validation during that window would turn normal
			// join/scene ordering into a malformed-message conviction.
			if(player != null && player.Data == null) return true;
			if(player != null && AmongUsClient.Instance != null && AmongUsClient.Instance.AmHost &&
				Violations.TryGetValue(player.OwnerId, out ViolationState pendingRemoval) && pendingRemoval.Punished) return false;

			if(sourceNetObject != rpcCheck.GetExpectedNetObject())
			{
				Flag($"Blocked {rpc} on an invalid network object; its owner is not proof of sender identity.");
				return false;
			}

			// In official server-authoritative games these messages are results already
			// approved by the server. The lobby host is only another client and its local
			// role, death, door, meeting, or animation snapshot can be one packet behind.
			// Re-validating those results here caused legitimate kills, door closures,
			// shapeshifts and Phantom transitions to be dropped. Request-side checks still
			// run, and private/modded games where this client is authority still enforce
			// host-only direction below.
			if(Utilities.IsAnticheatPresent() && rpcCheck.IsHostOnly())
			{
				GrantAuthoritativeTransitionGrace(player, rpc);
				return true;
			}

			// On official/server-authoritative lobbies, the lobby host is still a normal
			// client and legitimately receives server-relayed MurderPlayer, Phantom,
			// door and other result RPCs owned by remote player objects. Enforce
			// host-only direction only when this client is the actual network authority.
			bool localIsNetworkAuthority = AmongUsClient.Instance.AmHost && !Utilities.IsAnticheatPresent();
			if(player != null && localIsNetworkAuthority && rpcCheck.IsHostOnly())
			{
				Flag($"Blocked a remote host-only {rpc} result. Its affected player object does not identify the sender; no player penalized.");
				return false;
			}

			if(player == null && localIsNetworkAuthority && rpcCheck.IsHostOnly())
			{
				Flag($"Blocked the host-only {rpc} message from a remote source; this shared-object RPC has no safe sender attribution.");
				return false;
			}

			// The first valid report can create MeetingHud before other reports and
			// gameplay requests that were already in flight are delivered. Do not count
			// those reordered packets as floods or cheats.
			if(rpc == RpcCalls.ReportDeadBody && (MeetingHud.Instance != null || HasMeetingTransitionGrace())) return false;
			if(localIsNetworkAuthority && HasMeetingTransitionGrace() && IsMeetingBoundaryAction(rpc)) return false;

			// Scan-stop is idempotent cleanup after closing a task, dying or meeting.
			// Do not leave the animation stuck because earlier starts exhausted a budget.
			bool scannerCleanup = false;
			if(rpc == RpcCalls.SetScanner && reader != null && reader.BytesRemaining >= 2)
			{
				int position = reader.Position;
				try { scannerCleanup = !reader.ReadBoolean(); }
				finally { reader.Position = position; }
			}
			if(!scannerCleanup && ObserveRpcBurst(player, rpc)) return false;

			int oldPosition = reader.Position;
			// Identity/chat fields and object ownership do not authenticate the
			// network sender. Never let a forged name/ID convict its apparent owner.
			bool sharedObject = player == null || IsAmbiguousIdentityBurst(rpc) || rpc == RpcCalls.SendChat;
			if(sharedObject) unattributedValidationDepth++;
			try
			{
				bool valid = rpcCheck.Validate(player, reader);
				reader.Position = oldPosition;
				return valid || !DiscardInvalidMessages;
			}
			catch(Exception exception)
			{
				reader.Position = oldPosition;
				AUAnticheatPlugin.Log.LogWarning($"Blocked malformed {rpc} message: {exception.GetType().Name}");
				Flag($"Blocked a {rpc} validation failure ({exception.GetType().Name}); this is not proof against the object owner.");
				return false;
			}
			finally
			{
				if(sharedObject) unattributedValidationDepth--;
				reader.Position = oldPosition;
			}
		}

		public static bool HandleGameData(GameDataTypes type, MessageReader reader)
		{
			EnsureSession();
			if(!Enabled || !GameDataHandlers.TryGetValue(type, out GameDataCheck check) || !check.Enabled) return true;

			int oldPosition = reader.Position;
			unattributedValidationDepth++;
			try
			{
				bool valid = check.Validate(reader);
				reader.Position = oldPosition;
				return valid || !DiscardInvalidMessages;
			}
			catch(Exception exception)
			{
				reader.Position = oldPosition;
				AUAnticheatPlugin.Log.LogWarning($"Blocked malformed {type} game-data message: {exception.GetType().Name}");
				Flag($"Blocked a malformed {type} game-data message.");
				return false;
			}
			finally
			{
				unattributedValidationDepth--;
				reader.Position = oldPosition;
			}
		}

		private static void NoteRpcTarget(PlayerControl player, RpcCalls rpc, MessageReader reader)
		{
			if(reader == null) return;
			int position = reader.Position;
			try
			{
				if(rpc == RpcCalls.MurderPlayer || rpc == RpcCalls.CheckMurder || rpc == RpcCalls.ProtectPlayer || rpc == RpcCalls.CheckProtect)
				{
					var target = reader.ReadNetObject<PlayerControl>();
					SenderEvidence.NoteTarget(target == null ? "unknown player target (unresolved reference)" : SafeName(target));
				}
				else if(rpc == RpcCalls.UpdateSystem || rpc == RpcCalls.CloseDoorsOfType || rpc == RpcCalls.StartMeeting || rpc == RpcCalls.SyncSettings)
					SenderEvidence.NoteTarget("shared lobby/game state; potentially all players");
				else if(rpc == RpcCalls.Shapeshift || rpc == RpcCalls.CheckShapeshift)
				{
					var appearance = reader.ReadNetObject<PlayerControl>();
					SenderEvidence.NoteTarget(SafeName(player) + "'s appearance; copied appearance reference: " + SafeName(appearance));
				}
			}
			catch(Exception) { SenderEvidence.NoteTarget("unknown (target could not be read)"); }
			finally { reader.Position = position; }
		}

		public static void Flag(PlayerControl player, string reason, bool shouldPunish = true)
		{
			bool unverified = SenderEvidence.Current != null;
			EnsureSession();
			if(unverified)
			{
				SenderEvidence.NoteCandidate(player != null ? player.OwnerId : null, shouldPunish ? 100 : 50);
				Flag($"Unverified attribution: named players are only affected objects or claims. No score or ban: {reason}");
				return;
			}
			if(unattributedValidationDepth > 0)
			{
				Flag($"Sender unverified; any named player is only a claimed actor. No score or ban: {reason}");
				return;
			}
			if(player == PlayerControl.LocalPlayer) return;

			if(SendNotification) AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Send(NotificationTitle, reason, NotificationDuration);
			if(player == null || player.Data == null) return;

			int weight = shouldPunish ? 100 : 50;
			ViolationState state = GetOrCreateState(player.OwnerId);
			DateTime now = DateTime.UtcNow;
			if((now - state.LastSeenUtc).TotalSeconds > 45)
			{
				state.Score = 0;
				state.Count = 0;
				state.Punished = false;
				state.Shared = false;
			}

			state.Score = Math.Min(1000, state.Score + weight);
			state.Count++;
			state.LastSeenUtc = now;
			AddIncident($"{now:HH:mm:ss} | {SafeName(player)} | score {state.Score} | {reason}");

			if(AmongUsClient.Instance.AmHost && !state.Punished && state.Score >= EnforcementThreshold)
			{
				string name = SafeName(player);
				state.Punished = Punish(player);
				if(!state.Shared) { LobbyChat.EvidenceNotice(name, state.Punished ? Punishment == Punishments.Ban ? "banned" : "kicked" : "detected", reason); state.Shared = true; }
			}
		}

		public static void FlagConfirmedExploit(PlayerControl player, string reason)
		{
			bool unverified = SenderEvidence.Current != null;
			EnsureSession();
			if(unverified)
			{
				SenderEvidence.NoteCandidate(player != null ? player.OwnerId : null, 100);
				Flag($"Exploit-pattern observation; sender unknown. No score or ban: {reason}");
				return;
			}
			if(unattributedValidationDepth > 0)
			{
				Flag($"Exploit-pattern message blocked; sender unverified. No score or ban: {reason}");
				return;
			}
			if(player == PlayerControl.LocalPlayer) return;

			DateTime now = DateTime.UtcNow;
			if(player == null || player.Data == null)
			{
				AddIncident($"{now:HH:mm:ss} | unattributed confirmed signature | {reason}");
				if(SendNotification) AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Send("Anti-cheat", $"Blocked a confirmed exploit signature, but the packet did not contain a trustworthy player identity: {reason}", NotificationDuration);
				return;
			}

			ViolationState state = GetOrCreateState(player.OwnerId);
			// A concrete, attributable menu action is exactly one 100-point
			// violation. It still receives immediate normal host enforcement, so
			// increasing the stored score to an artificial sentinel is unnecessary.
			state.Score = Math.Min(1000, state.Score + 100);
			state.Count++;
			state.LastSeenUtc = now;
			AddIncident($"{now:HH:mm:ss} | {SafeName(player)} | +100 confirmed menu action | score {state.Score} | {reason}");
			if(SendNotification) AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Send(NotificationTitle, $"Confirmed exploit blocked: {reason}", NotificationDuration);

			if(AmongUsClient.Instance != null && AmongUsClient.Instance.AmHost && !state.Punished)
			{
				string name = SafeName(player);
				state.Punished = BanConfirmedExploit(player);
				if(!state.Shared) { LobbyChat.EvidenceNotice(name, state.Punished ? "banned" : "detected", reason); state.Shared = true; }
			}
		}

		public static void Flag(string reason)
		{
			EnsureSession();
			SenderEvidence.Record();
			AddIncident($"{DateTime.UtcNow:HH:mm:ss} | unattributed | {reason}");
			if(SendNotification) AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Send(NotificationTitle, reason, NotificationDuration);
		}

		private static string NotificationTitle => AmongUsClient.Instance != null && !AmongUsClient.Instance.AmHost
			? "Anti-cheat (non-host alert)" : "Anti-cheat";

		public static bool ObserveRpcBurst(PlayerControl player, RpcCalls rpc)
		{
			if(player == null || player == PlayerControl.LocalPlayer || player.Data == null) return false;

			ViolationState state = GetOrCreateState(player.OwnerId);
			DateTime now = DateTime.UtcNow;
			Prune(state.RecentMessages, now, 2);
			FloodSafety.Enqueue(state.RecentMessages, now);

			(int maxEvents, double windowSeconds) = GetRateLimit(rpc);
			if(!state.MessagesByType.TryGetValue(rpc, out Queue<DateTime> messages))
			{
				messages = new Queue<DateTime>();
				state.MessagesByType.Add(rpc, messages);
			}

			Prune(messages, now, windowSeconds);
			FloodSafety.Enqueue(messages, now);

			bool globalBurst = state.RecentMessages.Count > 120;
			bool typeBurst = messages.Count > maxEvents;
			bool categoryBurst = false;
			bool extremeCategoryBurst = false;
			string categoryName = null;

			if(EnhancedMenuBehaviorChecks)
			{
				(string Name, int MaxEvents, double WindowSeconds) category = GetCategoryRateLimit(rpc);
				categoryName = category.Name;
				if(categoryName != null)
				{
					if(!state.MessagesByCategory.TryGetValue(categoryName, out Queue<DateTime> categoryMessages))
					{
						categoryMessages = new Queue<DateTime>();
						state.MessagesByCategory.Add(categoryName, categoryMessages);
					}

					Prune(categoryMessages, now, category.WindowSeconds);
					FloodSafety.Enqueue(categoryMessages, now);
					categoryBurst = categoryMessages.Count > category.MaxEvents;
					extremeCategoryBurst = categoryMessages.Count > category.MaxEvents + 4;
				}
			}

			bool burst = globalBurst || typeBurst || categoryBurst;
			if(!burst) return false;
			// Reliable task packets can be delivered together after a network stall.
			// Keep valid credits; only stop an extreme overall flood, without a ban
			// inferred from the arrival rate alone.
			if(rpc == RpcCalls.CompleteTask && !globalBurst) return false;

			bool ambiguousIdentityBurst = IsAmbiguousIdentityBurst(rpc);
			bool extremeBurst = !ambiguousIdentityBurst && (state.RecentMessages.Count > 180 || messages.Count > maxEvents + 3 || extremeCategoryBurst);
			double minimumAlertInterval = ambiguousIdentityBurst ? 5.0 : (extremeBurst ? 0.25 : 1.0);
			if((now - state.LastBurstAlertUtc).TotalSeconds >= minimumAlertInterval)
			{
				state.LastBurstAlertUtc = now;
				string scope = categoryBurst && categoryName != null ? $"{categoryName} action" : rpc.ToString();
				Flag($"Blocked a {scope} burst associated with {SafeName(player)}; arrival rate and object ownership alone do not authenticate the sender. No automatic penalty.");
			}

			return true;
		}

		public static bool ObserveDistinctTargetSpray(PlayerControl player, PlayerControl target, RpcCalls rpc)
		{
			if(player == null || player.Data == null || target == null || target.Data == null || player == PlayerControl.LocalPlayer) return false;

			(int MaximumTargets, double WindowSeconds) limits = rpc switch
			{
				RpcCalls.CheckMurder => (4, 2),
				RpcCalls.CheckProtect => (4, 3),
				RpcCalls.CheckShapeshift => (5, 3),
				_ => (0, 0)
			};
			if(limits.MaximumTargets == 0) return false;

			ViolationState state = GetOrCreateState(player.OwnerId);
			if(!state.TargetedActionsByType.TryGetValue(rpc, out Queue<TargetedAction> actions))
			{
				actions = new Queue<TargetedAction>();
				state.TargetedActionsByType.Add(rpc, actions);
			}

			DateTime now = DateTime.UtcNow;
			while(actions.Count > 0 && (now - actions.Peek().TimeUtc).TotalSeconds > limits.WindowSeconds) actions.Dequeue();
			FloodSafety.Enqueue(actions, new TargetedAction { TargetPlayerId = target.PlayerId, TimeUtc = now });

			HashSet<byte> distinctTargets = new HashSet<byte>();
			foreach(TargetedAction action in actions) distinctTargets.Add(action.TargetPlayerId);
			if(distinctTargets.Count < limits.MaximumTargets) return false;

			actions.Clear();
			string reason = $"{SafeName(player)} targeted {distinctTargets.Count} different players with {rpc} within {limits.WindowSeconds:F0} seconds.";
			if(rpc == RpcCalls.CheckMurder)
			{
				FlagConfirmedExploit(player, reason);
			}
			else
			{
				Flag(player, reason);
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static bool ObserveRapidColorCycling(PlayerControl player, byte color)
		{
			if(player == null || player.Data == null || player == PlayerControl.LocalPlayer) return false;

			ViolationState state = GetOrCreateState(player.OwnerId);
			DateTime now = DateTime.UtcNow;
			while(state.RecentColorRequests.Count > 0 && (now - state.RecentColorRequests.Peek().TimeUtc).TotalSeconds > 2)
			{
				state.RecentColorRequests.Dequeue();
			}

			FloodSafety.Enqueue(state.RecentColorRequests, new ColorRequest { Color = color, TimeUtc = now });
			HashSet<byte> distinctColors = new HashSet<byte>();
			foreach(ColorRequest request in state.RecentColorRequests) distinctColors.Add(request.Color);

			if(distinctColors.Count >= 10)
			{
				state.RecentColorRequests.Clear();
				FlagConfirmedExploit(player, $"{SafeName(player)} cycled through {distinctColors.Count} different colors within two seconds.");
				return true;
			}

			if(distinctColors.Count == 8)
			{
				Flag(player, $"{SafeName(player)} rapidly cycled through eight different colors; blocked pending further evidence.", false);
				return true;
			}

			return false;
		}

		public static bool ObserveTaskCompletionSweep(PlayerControl player, uint taskIndex)
		{
			if(!EnhancedMenuBehaviorChecks || player == null || player.Data == null || player == PlayerControl.LocalPlayer ||
				IsRoundStartGrace() || MeetingHud.Instance != null || HasMeetingTransitionGrace()) return false;

			ViolationState state = GetOrCreateState(player.OwnerId);
			DateTime now = DateTime.UtcNow;
			PruneIndexed(state.RecentTaskCompletions, now, 1.0);
			FloodSafety.Enqueue(state.RecentTaskCompletions, new IndexedAction { Value = unchecked((int)taskIndex), TimeUtc = now });

			HashSet<int> distinctTasks = new HashSet<int>();
			foreach(IndexedAction action in state.RecentTaskCompletions) distinctTasks.Add(action.Value);
			if(distinctTasks.Count < 5) return false;

			state.RecentTaskCompletions.Clear();
			// Delivery timestamps cannot distinguish automation from a backlog after
			// lag. Preserve legitimate task credits and use this only as a review alert.
			Flag($"Task burst observed for {SafeName(player)}: {distinctTasks.Count} distinct completions arrived within one second. Possible automation or network catch-up; no automatic penalty.");
			return false;
		}

		public static bool ObserveSabotageSweep(PlayerControl player, SystemTypes sabotageTarget)
		{
			if(!EnhancedMenuBehaviorChecks || player == null || player.Data == null || player == PlayerControl.LocalPlayer ||
				IsRoundStartGrace() || MeetingHud.Instance != null || HasMeetingTransitionGrace()) return false;

			ViolationState state = GetOrCreateState(player.OwnerId);
			DateTime now = DateTime.UtcNow;
			PruneIndexed(state.RecentSabotageRequests, now, 1.5);
			FloodSafety.Enqueue(state.RecentSabotageRequests, new IndexedAction { Value = (int)sabotageTarget, TimeUtc = now });

			HashSet<int> distinctTargets = new HashSet<int>();
			foreach(IndexedAction action in state.RecentSabotageRequests) distinctTargets.Add(action.Value);
			if(distinctTargets.Count < 2) return false;

			state.RecentSabotageRequests.Clear();
			Flag($"Multiple sabotage requests observed: {distinctTargets.Count} targets within 1.5 seconds. Claimed actor {SafeName(player)} is unverified; the game must validate cooldown and authority. No automatic penalty.");
			return false;
		}

		private static bool IsAmbiguousIdentityBurst(RpcCalls rpc)
		{
			return rpc == RpcCalls.CheckName || rpc == RpcCalls.SetName || rpc == RpcCalls.CheckColor || rpc == RpcCalls.SetColor ||
				rpc == RpcCalls.SetLevel || rpc == RpcCalls.SetHatStr || rpc == RpcCalls.SetSkinStr || rpc == RpcCalls.SetPetStr ||
				rpc == RpcCalls.SetVisorStr || rpc == RpcCalls.SetNamePlateStr;
		}

		private static void GrantAuthoritativeTransitionGrace(PlayerControl player, RpcCalls rpc)
		{
			switch(rpc)
			{
				case RpcCalls.BootFromVent:
				case RpcCalls.SetRole:
				case RpcCalls.Shapeshift:
				case RpcCalls.StartVanish:
				case RpcCalls.StartAppear:
					GrantMovementGrace(player, 4);
					break;
				case RpcCalls.StartMeeting:
				case RpcCalls.CloseMeeting:
				case RpcCalls.VotingComplete:
					GrantMeetingTransitionGrace(3);
					foreach(PlayerControl candidate in PlayerControl.AllPlayerControls)
					{
						GrantMovementGrace(candidate, 5);
					}
					break;
			}
		}

		private static bool IsMeetingBoundaryAction(RpcCalls rpc)
		{
			return rpc == RpcCalls.CheckMurder || rpc == RpcCalls.CheckProtect || rpc == RpcCalls.CheckShapeshift ||
				rpc == RpcCalls.CheckVanish || rpc == RpcCalls.CheckAppear || rpc == RpcCalls.EnterVent ||
				rpc == RpcCalls.ExitVent || rpc == RpcCalls.ClimbLadder || rpc == RpcCalls.UsePlatform ||
				rpc == RpcCalls.TriggerSpores;
		}

		public static int GetScore(int ownerId)
		{
			EnsureSession();
			return Violations.TryGetValue(ownerId, out ViolationState state) ? state.Score : 0;
		}

		public static bool IsRoundStartGrace()
		{
			if(GameManager.Instance == null || !GameManager.Instance.GameHasStarted) return true;
			if(roundStartedUtc == DateTime.MinValue) roundStartedUtc = DateTime.UtcNow;
			return (DateTime.UtcNow - roundStartedUtc).TotalSeconds <= 15;
		}

		public static bool IsEmergencyMeetingDuringCooldown(out double secondsRemaining)
		{
			secondsRemaining = 0;
			if(GameManager.Instance == null || !GameManager.Instance.GameHasStarted) return false;
			if(emergencyAvailableUtc == DateTime.MinValue) StartEmergencyCooldown();
			if(emergencyAvailableUtc == DateTime.MinValue) return false;

			secondsRemaining = (emergencyAvailableUtc - DateTime.UtcNow).TotalSeconds;
			// Allow a small replication/timer-display margin. We only punish a clearly
			// impossible emergency meeting, not a request racing the final countdown tick.
			return secondsRemaining > 1.5;
		}

		private static void StartEmergencyCooldown()
		{
			try
			{
				int cooldown = GameOptionsManager.Instance.CurrentGameOptions.GetInt(Int32OptionNames.EmergencyCooldown);
				emergencyAvailableUtc = DateTime.UtcNow.AddSeconds(Math.Max(0, cooldown));
			}
			catch(Exception exception)
			{
				emergencyAvailableUtc = DateTime.MinValue;
				AUAnticheatPlugin.Log.LogWarning($"Could not read the emergency-meeting cooldown: {exception.GetType().Name}");
			}
		}

		public static void GrantMeetingTransitionGrace(double seconds = 3)
		{
			SenderEvidence.PauseSequences(null, seconds);
			DateTime until = DateTime.UtcNow.AddSeconds(seconds);
			if(until > meetingTransitionGraceUntilUtc) meetingTransitionGraceUntilUtc = until;
		}

		public static bool HasMeetingTransitionGrace()
		{
			return meetingTransitionGraceUntilUtc >= DateTime.UtcNow;
		}

		public static PlayerControl GetHostPlayer()
		{
			if(AmongUsClient.Instance == null) return null;
			ClientData host = AmongUsClient.Instance.FindClientById(AmongUsClient.Instance.HostId);
			return host == null ? null : host.Character;
		}

		public static PlayerControl GetAuthoritativeActor(PlayerControl objectOwner)
		{
			return AmongUsClient.Instance != null && AmongUsClient.Instance.AmHost ? objectOwner : GetHostPlayer();
		}

		public static void GrantMovementGrace(PlayerControl player, double seconds = 2)
		{
			if(player == null || player.Data == null) return;
			SenderEvidence.PauseSequences(player.OwnerId, seconds);
			ViolationState state = GetOrCreateState(player.OwnerId);
			DateTime until = DateTime.UtcNow.AddSeconds(seconds);
			if(until > state.MovementGraceUntilUtc) state.MovementGraceUntilUtc = until;
		}

		public static bool HasMovementGrace(PlayerControl player)
		{
			if(player == null || player.Data == null) return true;
			return GetOrCreateState(player.OwnerId).MovementGraceUntilUtc >= DateTime.UtcNow;
		}

		public static void GrantGhostRoleGrace(PlayerControl player, double seconds = 8)
		{
			if(player == null || player.Data == null) return;
			SenderEvidence.PauseSequences(player.OwnerId, seconds);
			ViolationState state = GetOrCreateState(player.OwnerId);
			DateTime until = DateTime.UtcNow.AddSeconds(seconds);
			if(until > state.GhostRoleGraceUntilUtc) state.GhostRoleGraceUntilUtc = until;
		}

		public static bool HasGhostRoleGrace(PlayerControl player)
		{
			if(player == null || player.Data == null) return false;
			return GetOrCreateState(player.OwnerId).GhostRoleGraceUntilUtc >= DateTime.UtcNow;
		}

		public static void GrantVentCleaningGrace(PlayerControl player, int ventId, double seconds = 8)
		{
			if(player == null || player.Data == null) return;
			SenderEvidence.PauseSequences(player.OwnerId, seconds);
			ViolationState state = GetOrCreateState(player.OwnerId);
			state.VentCleaningVentId = ventId;
			state.VentCleaningGraceUntilUtc = DateTime.UtcNow.AddSeconds(seconds);
		}

		public static bool HasVentCleaningGrace(PlayerControl player, int ventId)
		{
			if(player == null || player.Data == null) return false;
			ViolationState state = GetOrCreateState(player.OwnerId);
			return state.VentCleaningVentId == ventId && state.VentCleaningGraceUntilUtc >= DateTime.UtcNow;
		}

		public static bool TryConsumeVentCleaningGrace(PlayerControl player, int ventId)
		{
			if(!HasVentCleaningGrace(player, ventId)) return false;
			ViolationState state = GetOrCreateState(player.OwnerId);
			state.VentCleaningVentId = -1;
			state.VentCleaningGraceUntilUtc = DateTime.MinValue;
			return true;
		}

		private static void ObserveDisconnect(string playerName, DisconnectReasons reason)
		{
			if(reason == DisconnectReasons.ExitGame || reason == DisconnectReasons.Kicked || reason == DisconnectReasons.Banned) return;

			EnsureSession();
			DateTime now = DateTime.UtcNow;
			Prune(RecentUnexpectedDisconnects, now, 2.5);
			FloodSafety.Enqueue(RecentUnexpectedDisconnects, now);
			if(RecentUnexpectedDisconnects.Count < 3 || (now - lastMassDisconnectAlertUtc).TotalSeconds < 8) return;

			lastMassDisconnectAlertUtc = now;
			RecentUnexpectedDisconnects.Clear();
			Flag($"Detected several unexpected disconnects in under three seconds (latest: {playerName}, {reason}). This can indicate a false-error-kick attempt or a server outage; no player was blamed without sender evidence.");
		}

		public static string GetRecentIncidentSummary(int maximum = 5)
		{
			EnsureSession();
			if(Incidents.Count == 0) return "No incidents recorded in this lobby.";

			int start = Math.Max(0, Incidents.Count - maximum);
			return string.Join("\n", Incidents.GetRange(start, Incidents.Count - start));
		}

		public static string GetSequenceSummary()
		{
			EnsureSession();
			if(!Enabled || !EnhancedMenuBehaviorChecks) return "Related-action analysis is off. Enable Cheat detection and Extra behaviour checks in Detection.";
			return SenderEvidence.SequenceSummary(id => SafeName(AmongUsClient.Instance?.FindClientById(id)?.Character));
		}

		public static void ResetSession()
		{
			SenderEvidence.Reset();
			InfluencerProtection.Reset();
			LobbyMovementGuard.Reset();
			LobbyLevelPolicy.Reset();
			VoteKickGuard.Reset();
			PacketPreflight.Reset();
			MovementMonitor.Reset();
			Violations.Clear();
			Incidents.Clear();
			roundStartedUtc = DateTime.MinValue;
			emergencyAvailableUtc = DateTime.MinValue;
			meetingTransitionGraceUntilUtc = DateTime.MinValue;
			lastMassDisconnectAlertUtc = DateTime.MinValue;
			RecentUnexpectedDisconnects.Clear();
			sessionKey = GetSessionKey();
		}

		private static void EnsureSession()
		{
			string current = GetSessionKey();
			if(current != sessionKey)
			{
				SenderEvidence.Reset();
				InfluencerProtection.Reset();
				VoteKickGuard.Reset();
				PacketPreflight.Reset();
				MovementMonitor.Reset();
				Violations.Clear();
				Incidents.Clear();
				roundStartedUtc = DateTime.MinValue;
				emergencyAvailableUtc = DateTime.MinValue;
				meetingTransitionGraceUntilUtc = DateTime.MinValue;
				lastMassDisconnectAlertUtc = DateTime.MinValue;
				RecentUnexpectedDisconnects.Clear();
				sessionKey = current;
			}
		}

		private static string GetSessionKey()
		{
			if(AmongUsClient.Instance == null) return "offline";
			return $"{AmongUsClient.Instance.GameId}:{AmongUsClient.Instance.HostId}";
		}

		private static ViolationState GetOrCreateState(int ownerId)
		{
			if(!Violations.TryGetValue(ownerId, out ViolationState state))
			{
				state = new ViolationState();
				Violations.Add(ownerId, state);
			}

			return state;
		}

		private static void AddIncident(string incident)
		{
			Incidents.Add(incident);
			if(Incidents.Count > 50) Incidents.RemoveAt(0);
		}

		private static void Prune(Queue<DateTime> events, DateTime now, double windowSeconds)
		{
			while(events.Count > 0 && (now - events.Peek()).TotalSeconds > windowSeconds)
			{
				events.Dequeue();
			}
		}

		private static void PruneIndexed(Queue<IndexedAction> events, DateTime now, double windowSeconds)
		{
			while(events.Count > 0 && (now - events.Peek().TimeUtc).TotalSeconds > windowSeconds)
			{
				events.Dequeue();
			}
		}

		private static (int MaxEvents, double WindowSeconds) GetRateLimit(RpcCalls rpc)
		{
			return rpc switch
			{
				RpcCalls.CompleteTask => (6, 5),
				RpcCalls.ReportDeadBody => (6, 5),
				RpcCalls.SetName => (6, 10),
				RpcCalls.CheckColor or RpcCalls.SetColor => (15, 10),
				RpcCalls.SetPetStr => (5, 10),
				RpcCalls.SetHatStr or RpcCalls.SetSkinStr or RpcCalls.SetVisorStr or RpcCalls.SetNamePlateStr => (8, 10),
				RpcCalls.SendChat => (4, 5),
				RpcCalls.SetLevel => (3, 30),
				RpcCalls.EnterVent or RpcCalls.ExitVent => (8, 3),
				RpcCalls.SnapTo => (6, 3),
				RpcCalls.CastVote => (3, 5),
				RpcCalls.AddVote => (10, 5),
				RpcCalls.ClimbLadder or RpcCalls.UsePlatform => (6, 3),
				RpcCalls.UpdateSystem => (35, 3),
				RpcCalls.MurderPlayer or RpcCalls.CheckMurder => (4, 2),
				RpcCalls.ProtectPlayer or RpcCalls.Shapeshift => (6, 5),
				RpcCalls.SetRole => (8, 15),
				RpcCalls.TriggerSpores => (20, 3),
				_ => (40, 3)
			};
		}

		private static (string Name, int MaxEvents, double WindowSeconds) GetCategoryRateLimit(RpcCalls rpc)
		{
			return rpc switch
			{
				RpcCalls.CheckName or RpcCalls.SetName or RpcCalls.CheckColor or RpcCalls.SetColor or RpcCalls.SetLevel or
				RpcCalls.SetHatStr or RpcCalls.SetSkinStr or RpcCalls.SetPetStr or RpcCalls.SetVisorStr or RpcCalls.SetNamePlateStr
					=> ("identity/cosmetic", 30, 10),
				RpcCalls.EnterVent or RpcCalls.ExitVent or RpcCalls.SnapTo or RpcCalls.ClimbLadder or RpcCalls.UsePlatform or RpcCalls.BootFromVent
					=> ("movement", 18, 3),
				RpcCalls.CompleteTask or RpcCalls.PlayAnimation or RpcCalls.SetScanner
					=> ("task/animation", 12, 5),
				RpcCalls.MurderPlayer or RpcCalls.CheckMurder or RpcCalls.ProtectPlayer or RpcCalls.CheckProtect or
				RpcCalls.Shapeshift or RpcCalls.CheckShapeshift or RpcCalls.CheckVanish or RpcCalls.StartVanish or RpcCalls.CheckAppear or RpcCalls.StartAppear
					=> ("combat/role", 10, 5),
				RpcCalls.ReportDeadBody or RpcCalls.StartMeeting or RpcCalls.CastVote or RpcCalls.AddVote or RpcCalls.ClearVote or RpcCalls.CloseMeeting or RpcCalls.VotingComplete
					=> ("meeting/vote", 12, 5),
				RpcCalls.UpdateSystem or RpcCalls.CloseDoorsOfType or RpcCalls.TriggerSpores
					=> ("ship/system", 42, 3),
				_ => (null, 0, 0)
			};
		}

		private static bool Punish(PlayerControl player)
		{
			if(Punishment == Punishments.None || AmongUsClient.Instance == null || !AmongUsClient.Instance.AmHost ||
				player == null || player.Data == null || player == PlayerControl.LocalPlayer || player.OwnerId == AmongUsClient.Instance.HostId) return false;

			bool ban = Punishment == Punishments.Ban;
			AUAnticheatPlugin.Log.LogMessage($"{SafeName(player)} was {(ban ? "banned" : "kicked")} by AU-Anticheat after reaching the evidence threshold.");
			AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Send("AU-Anticheat", $"{SafeName(player)} was {(ban ? "banned" : "kicked")} by anti-cheat.", 5);
			AmongUsClient.Instance.KickPlayer(player.OwnerId, ban);
			return true;
		}

		private static bool BanConfirmedExploit(PlayerControl player)
		{
			if(AmongUsClient.Instance == null || !AmongUsClient.Instance.AmHost || player == null || player.Data == null ||
				player == PlayerControl.LocalPlayer || player.OwnerId == AmongUsClient.Instance.HostId) return false;

			AUAnticheatPlugin.Log.LogMessage($"{SafeName(player)} was immediately banned by AU-Anticheat for a confirmed exploit signature.");
			AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Send("AU-Anticheat", $"{SafeName(player)} was banned by anti-cheat.", 5);
			AmongUsClient.Instance.KickPlayer(player.OwnerId, true);
			return true;
		}

		private static string SafeName(PlayerControl player)
		{
			return player != null && player.Data != null ? PublicText.Name(player.Data.PlayerName) : "Unknown player";
		}
	}
}

```

`source/anticheat/ChatContentRules.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
    internal static class ChatContentRules
    {
        private static readonly Regex Invite = new(@"\b(?:discord\s*[.]\s*gg|discord(?:app)?\s*[.]\s*com\s*/\s*invite|t\s*[.]\s*me)\s*/\s*[a-z0-9]", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase | RegexOptions.CultureInvariant, TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(25));
        private static readonly Regex Recruitment = new(@"\b(?:join|come\s+to|enter|unete|uníos|entra)\b.{0,35}\b(?:my|our|mi|nuestro|another|other|new)\b.{0,20}\b(?:lobb(?:y|ies)|room|sala|partida)\b", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase | RegexOptions.CultureInvariant, TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(25));
        private static readonly Regex Code = new(@"\b(?:code|codigo|código)\s*[:=]\s*[a-z]{6}\b", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase | RegexOptions.CultureInvariant, TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(25));
        internal static string Normalize(string value)
        {
            if(value == null || value.Length > 1600) return "";
            var output = new StringBuilder();
            foreach(char c in value.Normalize(NormalizationForm.FormKC))
                if(CharUnicodeInfo.GetUnicodeCategory(c) != UnicodeCategory.Format && !char.IsControl(c)) output.Append(c);
            return output.ToString();
        }
        public static bool IsAd(string value)
        {
            try
            {
                string text = Normalize(value);
                return Invite.IsMatch(text) || Recruitment.IsMatch(text) ||
                    (Code.IsMatch(text) && Regex.IsMatch(text, @"\b(join|come|lobby|room|sala|unete|entra)\b", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase | RegexOptions.CultureInvariant, TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(25)));
            }
            catch(ArgumentException) { return false; }
            catch(RegexMatchTimeoutException) { return false; }
        }
        public static bool MatchesLiteral(string value, IEnumerable<string> filters)
        {
            string text;
            try { text = Normalize(value); } catch(ArgumentException) { return false; }
            foreach(string filter in filters)
            {
                if(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(filter)) continue;
                int index = text.IndexOf(filter, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                while(index >= 0)
                {
                    int end = index + filter.Length;
                    if((index == 0 || !char.IsLetterOrDigit(text[index-1])) && (end == text.Length || !char.IsLetterOrDigit(text[end]))) return true;
                    index = text.IndexOf(filter, index + 1, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                }
            }
            return false;
        }
    }
}

```

`source/anticheat/ChatModeration.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using HarmonyLib;
using UnityEngine;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
    internal static class ChatModeration
    {
        public static bool Ads = true, Spam = true, WordFilter, NameFilter, AfkAlerts;
        public static int AfkSeconds = 120;
        public static long Hidden { get; private set; }
        public static string LastReason { get; private set; } = "None";
        private sealed class State { public uint NetId; public readonly Queue<float> Times = new(); }
        private static readonly Dictionary<int,State> States = new();
        private static string room = "";
        private static float noticeAt;
        public static string BoundCode(PlayerControl player)
        {
            if(player == null) return null;
            var peer = AmongUsClient.Instance?.FindClientById(player.OwnerId);
            if(peer == null || peer.IsBeingCreated || peer.Character != player || player.Data == null) return null;
            string code = FriendCodeExceptions.Normalize(peer.FriendCode);
            return code != null && code == FriendCodeExceptions.Normalize(player.Data.FriendCode) ? code : null;
        }
        [HarmonyPatch(typeof(ChatController), nameof(ChatController.AddChat))]
        private static class FilterDisplayedChat
        {
            private static bool Prefix(PlayerControl __0, string __1) => Allow(__0,__1);
        }
        internal static bool Allow(PlayerControl player, string message)
        {
            if(player == null || player == PlayerControl.LocalPlayer || player.Data == null || message == null) return true;
            LobbyIdleMonitor.Touch(player);
            string current = AmongUsClient.Instance == null ? "" : $"{AmongUsClient.Instance.GameId}:{AmongUsClient.Instance.HostId}";
            if(current != room) { room = current; States.Clear(); }
            string reason = null;
            if(ModerationStore.Contains(BoundCode(player), "block")) reason = "your local block list";
            else if(Ads && ChatContentRules.IsAd(message)) reason = "lobby recruitment or invite link";
            else if(WordFilter && ChatContentRules.MatchesLiteral(message, ModerationStore.Words)) reason = "your word filter";
            else if(NameFilter && ChatContentRules.MatchesLiteral(player.Data.PlayerName, ModerationStore.Names)) reason = "your name filter";
            float now = Time.realtimeSinceStartup;
            if(Spam)
            {
                if(!States.TryGetValue(player.OwnerId, out var state) || state.NetId != player.NetId)
                {
                    if(States.Count >= 64) States.Clear();
                    States[player.OwnerId] = state = new State { NetId = player.NetId };
                }
                while(state.Times.Count > 0 && now - state.Times.Peek() >= 10) state.Times.Dequeue();
                if(state.Times.Count >= 5) reason ??= "chat flooding";
                if(state.Times.Count < 6) state.Times.Enqueue(now);
            }
            if(reason == null) return true;
            Hidden++; LastReason = reason;
            if(now >= noticeAt)
            {
                noticeAt = now + 10;
                AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Send("Chat filter", $"Hidden chat displayed as {PublicText.Name(player.Data.PlayerName)}: {reason}. Sender identity is not authenticated. No ban or cheat points; other clients may receive it.");
            }
            return false;
        }
    }
}
```

`source/anticheat/ConfigMigration.cs`:

```cs
using System.IO;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
    internal static class ConfigMigration
    {
        internal const string CurrentFileName = "com.au.anticheat.cfg";
        // Compatibility only: this literal locates existing user preferences.
        // Do not rename it or delete the user's original configuration.
        internal const string LegacyFileName = "com.xav.anticheat.cfg";

        internal static bool ImportIfMissing(string directory)
        {
            string current = Path.Combine(directory, CurrentFileName);
            string legacy = Path.Combine(directory, LegacyFileName);
            if(File.Exists(current) || !File.Exists(legacy)) return false;
            if(new FileInfo(legacy).Length > 65536)
                throw new IOException("Previous configuration exceeds the import limit.");
            File.Copy(legacy, current, overwrite: false);
            return true;
        }
    }
}
```

`source/anticheat/FloodSafety.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
	internal static class FloodSafety
	{
		// Above all existing burst thresholds. Retain recent evidence, never an
		// attacker-controlled number of records in one observation window.
		public const int MaximumSamples = 256;
		public static void Enqueue<T>(Queue<T> samples, T sample)
		{
			while(samples.Count >= MaximumSamples) samples.Dequeue();
			samples.Enqueue(sample);
		}
	}

	internal sealed class VoteReplayWindow
	{
		public const int MaximumPairs = 256;
		public const double ReplaySeconds = 2;
		private readonly Dictionary<(int Source, int Target), double> accepted = new();
		public int Count => accepted.Count;

		// A claimed voter is NOT an authenticated sender. This is idempotency
		// protection only, and must never add score or trigger a ban.
		public bool Accept(int source, int target, double now)
		{
			if(!double.IsFinite(now)) return true;
			var key = (source, target);
			if(accepted.TryGetValue(key, out double previous) && now >= previous && now - previous < ReplaySeconds) return false;
			if(!accepted.ContainsKey(key) && accepted.Count >= MaximumPairs)
			{
				// Evict the oldest record, not a different voter's new legitimate vote.
				var oldest = key;
				double oldestTime = double.PositiveInfinity;
				foreach(var pair in accepted)
					if(pair.Value < oldestTime) { oldest = pair.Key; oldestTime = pair.Value; }
				accepted.Remove(oldest);
			}
			accepted[key] = now;
			return true;
		}

		public void Forget(int clientId)
		{
			var remove = new List<(int Source, int Target)>();
			foreach(var pair in accepted)
				if(pair.Key.Source == clientId || pair.Key.Target == clientId) remove.Add(pair.Key);
			foreach(var key in remove) accepted.Remove(key);
		}
		public void Reset() => accepted.Clear();
	}
}

```

`source/anticheat/FriendCodeExceptions.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
	internal static class FriendCodeExceptions
	{
		private static readonly SortedSet<string> Codes = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);
		private static Action<string> save;
		public static string[] Snapshot() => Codes.ToArray();
		public static void Clear() { Codes.Clear(); save?.Invoke(""); }
		public static string Normalize(string text)
		{
			string code = (text ?? "").Trim().ToLowerInvariant();
			return code.Length <= 36 && Regex.IsMatch(code, "^[a-z0-9]{1,24}#[0-9]{1,10}$") ? code : null;
		}
		public static void Initialize(string stored, Action<string> persist)
		{
			Codes.Clear(); save = persist;
			foreach(string part in (stored ?? "").Split(';'))
			{
				string normalized = Normalize(part);
				if(normalized != null && Codes.Count < 128) Codes.Add(normalized);
			}
		}
		public static bool Contains(string code) { string value = Normalize(code); return value != null && Codes.Contains(value); }
		public static bool Add(string code)
		{
			string value = Normalize(code);
			if(value == null || Codes.Count >= 128 || !Codes.Add(value)) return false;
			save?.Invoke(string.Join(";", Codes)); return true;
		}
		public static bool Remove(string code)
		{
			string value = Normalize(code);
			if(value == null || !Codes.Remove(value)) return false;
			save?.Invoke(string.Join(";", Codes)); return true;
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/GhostRoleTransitionMonitor.cs`:

```cs
using HarmonyLib;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
	internal static class GhostRoleTransitionMonitor
	{
		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(PlayerControl), nameof(PlayerControl.CoSetRole))]
		private static class OnGhostRoleAssigned
		{
			private static void Prefix(PlayerControl __instance, AmongUs.GameOptions.RoleTypes __0)
			{
				// The role RPC can precede IsDead replication. Use the requested role,
				// not the old role snapshot, and include new game-defined ghost roles.
				if(!RoleManager.IsGhostRole(__0)) return;
				Anticheat.GrantGhostRoleGrace(__instance);
				Anticheat.GrantMovementGrace(__instance, 4);
			}
		}

		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(PlayerControl), nameof(PlayerControl.Die))]
		private static class OnPlayerDie
		{
			private static void Prefix(PlayerControl __instance, bool assignGhostRole)
			{
				Anticheat.GrantMovementGrace(__instance, 4);
				if(assignGhostRole) Anticheat.GrantGhostRoleGrace(__instance);
			}
		}

		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(PlayerControl), nameof(PlayerControl.Exiled))]
		private static class OnPlayerExiled
		{
			private static void Prefix(PlayerControl __instance)
			{
				Anticheat.GrantMovementGrace(__instance, 4);
				Anticheat.GrantGhostRoleGrace(__instance);
			}
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/InfluencerProtection.cs`:

```cs
using HarmonyLib;
using Hazel;
using Il2CppInterop.Runtime.InteropTypes.Arrays;
using UnityEngine;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
	internal static class InfluencerProtection
	{
		public static bool Enabled = true;
		private static readonly InfluencerBurstWindow Window = new();
		private static int feedId;
		public static void Reset() { Window.Reset(); feedId = 0; }

		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(SocialMediumFeedSystem), nameof(SocialMediumFeedSystem.HandleSpiritGuideMessage))]
		private static class BeforeInfluencerDecode
		{
			[HarmonyPriority(Priority.First)]
			private static bool Prefix(MessageReader __0)
			{
				// Even direct entry must be checked BEFORE ReadBytesAndSize allocates.
				return !Enabled || PacketPreflight.ValidateRpc(RpcCalls.SpiritGuideMessage, __0);
			}
		}

		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(SocialMediumFeedSystem), nameof(SocialMediumFeedSystem.AddImageToFeed))]
		private static class BeforeInfluencerImages
		{
			[HarmonyPriority(Priority.First)]
			private static bool Prefix(SocialMediumFeedSystem __instance, Il2CppStructArray<byte> __0)
			{
				if(!Enabled) return true;
				if(__0 == null || !InfluencerRules.ValidImageCount(__0.Length))
					return PacketPreflight.Block("Influencer message must contain 1 to 3 images");
				// Use the shipped catalog, not a guessed asset count. This shared RPC
				// provides no trustworthy sender identity to punish.
				var sprites = __instance.PossibleSpriteList;
				if(sprites == null || sprites.Count == 0) return PacketPreflight.Block("Influencer image catalog is not ready");
				for(int i = 0; i < __0.Length; i++)
					if(!InfluencerRules.ValidImageIndex(__0[i], sprites.Count))
						return PacketPreflight.Block("Influencer image index is outside the current catalog");
				int currentId = __instance.GetInstanceID();
				if(feedId != currentId) { Window.Reset(); feedId = currentId; }
				if(!Window.TryAccept(Time.realtimeSinceStartup))
					return PacketPreflight.Block("Influencer image feed resource limit reached (not a player conviction)");
				return true;
			}
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/InfluencerRules.cs`:

```cs
using System;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
	internal static class InfluencerRules
	{
		public static bool ValidImageCount(int count) => count >= 1 && count <= 3;
		public static bool ValidImageIndex(int index, int spriteCount) => index >= 0 && index < spriteCount;
	}

	// Local resource ceiling, NOT a role cooldown or evidence against a player.
	// Fixed storage: rejected floods cannot grow a queue or attacker-keyed map.
	internal sealed class InfluencerBurstWindow
	{
		public const int MaximumMessages = 64;
		private double started = double.NaN;
		private int count;
		public void Reset() { started = double.NaN; count = 0; }
		public bool TryAccept(double now)
		{
			if(!double.IsFinite(now)) return false;
			if(double.IsNaN(started) || now < started || now - started >= 10)
			{
				started = now;
				count = 0;
			}
			if(count >= MaximumMessages) return false;
			count++;
			return true;
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/LevelSelectorRules.cs`:

```cs
using System.Globalization;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
	internal static class LevelSelectorRules
	{
		public static string Append(string input, int digit) => digit < 0 || digit > 9 || input.Length >= 10
			? input : input + digit.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
		public static string Backspace(string input) => input.Length == 0 ? input : input.Substring(0, input.Length - 1);
		public static bool TryMinimum(string input, out uint minimum) =>
			uint.TryParse(input, NumberStyles.None, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out minimum) && minimum > 0;
		public static uint Adjust(uint value, int delta) => (uint)System.Math.Clamp((long)value + delta, 1, uint.MaxValue);
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/LoadoutStore.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text.Json;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
    // Single bounded file; user names never become filesystem paths.
    internal sealed class LoadoutStore
    {
        public sealed class Entry
        {
            public string Build { get; set; }
            public string Payload { get; set; }
        }
        private readonly string path;
        private Dictionary<string,Entry> entries = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        public string Status { get; private set; } = "No saved loadouts.";
        public LoadoutStore(string directory)
        {
            path = Path.Combine(directory,"lobby-loadouts.json");
            try
            {
                if(!File.Exists(path)) return;
                if(new FileInfo(path).Length > 262144) throw new FormatException();
                var loaded = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<Dictionary<string,Entry>>(File.ReadAllText(path));
                if(loaded == null || loaded.Count > 20) throw new FormatException();
                foreach(var pair in loaded)
                {
                    if(!ValidName(pair.Key) || pair.Value == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(pair.Value.Build) || pair.Value.Build.Length > 100 ||
                       pair.Value.Payload == null || pair.Value.Payload.Length > 5464 || Convert.FromBase64String(pair.Value.Payload).Length is < 1 or > 4096)
                        throw new FormatException();
                    entries.Add(pair.Key,pair.Value);
                }
                Status = "Loadouts loaded from this device.";
            }
            catch(Exception) { entries.Clear(); Status = "Could not load saved loadouts. The existing file was not changed."; readFailed = true; }
        }
        private bool readFailed;
        public static bool ValidName(string name) => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(name) && name == name.Trim() && name.Length <= 32 &&
            name.All(c => char.IsLetterOrDigit(c) || c == ' ' || c == '-' || c == '_');
        public string[] Names => entries.Keys.OrderBy(x=>x,StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase).ToArray();
        public Entry Find(string name) => name != null && entries.TryGetValue(name,out var value) ? value : null;
        public bool Save(string name,string build,byte[] bytes)
        {
            if(!ValidName(name) || bytes == null || bytes.Length == 0 || bytes.Length > 4096 || string.IsNullOrEmpty(build) || build.Length > 100)
            { Status = "Use a name of 1–32 letters, numbers, spaces, hyphens or underscores."; return false; }
            if(entries.ContainsKey(name)) { Status = "That name exists. Use a different name or delete the old loadout first."; return false; }
            if(entries.Count >= 20) { Status = "Maximum 20 loadouts. Delete one before saving another."; return false; }
            var next = new Dictionary<string,Entry>(entries,StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase) { [name] = new Entry { Build=build,Payload=Convert.ToBase64String(bytes) } };
            return Write(next,"Loadout saved.");
        }
        public bool Delete(string name)
        {
            if(name == null || !entries.ContainsKey(name)) return false;
            var next = new Dictionary<string,Entry>(entries,StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase); next.Remove(name);
            return Write(next,"Loadout deleted.");
        }
        private bool Write(Dictionary<string,Entry> next,string status)
        {
            if(readFailed) { Status = "Saved file needs repair before changes can be saved; it has not been overwritten."; return false; }
            try
            {
                Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(path));
                File.WriteAllText(path+".tmp",JsonSerializer.Serialize(next,new JsonSerializerOptions { WriteIndented=true }));
                File.Move(path+".tmp",path,true); entries=next; Status=status; return true;
            }
            catch(Exception) { Status="Could not save loadouts. Previous file was kept."; return false; }
        }
    }
}
```

`source/anticheat/LobbyChat.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using HarmonyLib;
using UnityEngine;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
	// Ordinary chat from the host's own player. Never impersonates a server,
	// bypasses dead/quick-chat restrictions, or exposes roles during a round.
	internal static class LobbyChat
	{
		public static bool ShareAlerts = true;
		private const string Prefix = "[AU-Anticheat] ";
		internal const int MaximumPublicMessageLength = 100;
		internal static string CombinedMessage(IReadOnlyList<string> lines)
		{
			// Clean each rule without truncation; retain separators in one chat bubble.
			var cleaned = new List<string>();
			foreach(string line in lines)
			{
				string rule = PublicText.Clean(line, int.MaxValue);
				if(rule.Length > 0) cleaned.Add(rule);
			}
			return string.Join(" / ", cleaned);
		}
		private sealed class Pending { public string Text; public float Expires; public bool LobbyOnly, Rules; }
		private static readonly Queue<Pending> PendingMessages = new();
		private static string session = "";
		private static float nextSend;
		private static string sendingText;
		private static PlayerControl sendingPlayer;
		private static bool renderedDuringSend;
		private sealed class Echo { public string Text, Room; public uint NetId; public float Until; public bool Shown; }
		private static readonly List<Echo> Echoes = new();
		// Some game builds render RpcSendChat locally. Only add a fallback bubble
		// when that did not happen; never suppress ordinary incoming/manual chat.
		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(ChatController), nameof(ChatController.AddChat))]
		private static class ObserveChatRender
		{
			private static bool Prefix(PlayerControl __0, string __1)
			{
				if(sendingText != null && __0 == sendingPlayer && __1 == sendingText) renderedDuringSend = true;
				Echoes.RemoveAll(e => e.Until < Time.realtimeSinceStartup || e.Room != Key);
				if(__0 == null || __0 != PlayerControl.LocalPlayer) return true;
				foreach(var echo in Echoes)
				{
					if(echo.NetId != __0.NetId || echo.Text != __1) continue;
					if(echo.Shown) return false;
					echo.Shown = true; return true;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}
		private static string Key => AmongUsClient.Instance == null ? "offline" : $"{AmongUsClient.Instance.GameId}:{AmongUsClient.Instance.HostId}";
		// Clearing a queue or changing rooms must not shorten an outstanding send cooldown.
		public static void Clear() { PendingMessages.Clear(); session = Key; }
		private static bool Host => AUAnticheatPlugin.ProtectionsAttached && AmongUsClient.Instance != null && AmongUsClient.Instance.AmHost;
		private static float SendInterval => Math.Max(3f, ChatController.MAX_CHAT_SEND_RATE) + 0.25f;
		internal static bool LocalReady
		{
			get
			{
				var client = AmongUsClient.Instance;
				var local = PlayerControl.LocalPlayer;
				if(!Host || local == null || local.Data == null || local.Data.Disconnected || local.Data.IsDead ||
					client.ClientId != client.HostId || local.OwnerId != client.ClientId || HudManager.Instance?.Chat == null) return false;
				var peer = client.FindClientById(client.ClientId);
				return peer != null && !peer.IsBeingCreated && peer.Character == local;
			}
		}
		internal static bool WaitingLobby => LocalReady && LobbyBehaviour.Instance != null && MeetingHud.Instance == null &&
			AmongUsClient.Instance.GameState == InnerNet.InnerNetClient.GameStates.Joined &&
			(GameManager.Instance == null || !GameManager.Instance.GameHasStarted) &&
			GameStartManager.Instance != null && GameStartManager.Instance.startState == GameStartManager.StartingStates.NotStarting;
		// Successful ordinary host chat also postpones automation. Never change a rejected send.
		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(PlayerControl), nameof(PlayerControl.RpcSendChat))]
		private static class ObserveLocalChat
		{
			private static void Prefix(PlayerControl __instance, string __0)
			{
				// A new manual send is intentional, even when it repeats automated text.
				if(sendingText == null && __instance == PlayerControl.LocalPlayer) Echoes.RemoveAll(e => e.Text == __0);
			}
			private static void Postfix(PlayerControl __instance, bool __result)
			{
				if(__result && __instance == PlayerControl.LocalPlayer) RecordSend(false);
			}
		}
		private static void RecordSend(bool consumeCooldown)
		{
			float interval = SendInterval;
			if(float.IsFinite(interval)) nextSend = Math.Max(nextSend, Time.realtimeSinceStartup + interval);
			// Consume the normal cooldown, including for the player's next manual message.
			// Never advance this timer to make a send eligible sooner.
			if(consumeCooldown && HudManager.Instance?.Chat != null) HudManager.Instance.Chat.timeSinceLastMessage = 0;
		}
		private static void Ensure() { if(session != Key) Clear(); }
		public static void QueueNotice(string text)
		{
			Ensure(); if(!ShareAlerts || !Host || PendingMessages.Count >= 16) return;
			PendingMessages.Enqueue(new Pending { Text = Prefix + PublicText.Clean(text, 100-Prefix.Length), Expires = Time.realtimeSinceStartup + 20 });
		}
		public static void ProtectionNotice()
		{
			// Unattributed blocks stay in the local Alerts panel, never public chat.
		}
		public static void EvidenceNotice(string name, string action, string reason)
		{
			if(action != "banned" && action != "kicked" && action != "detected") return;
			string lead = PublicText.Clean(name,16) + " got " + action + " by anticheat for ";
			QueueNotice(lead + PublicText.Clean(reason,100-Prefix.Length-lead.Length));
		}
		public static bool QueueReport(IReadOnlyList<string> lines)
		{
			Ensure(); if(!Host || lines.Count == 0 || lines.Count > 16 || PendingMessages.Count >= 16) return false;
			string message = CombinedMessage(lines);
			if(message.Length == 0 || message.Length > MaximumPublicMessageLength) return false;
			foreach(var queued in PendingMessages) if(queued.LobbyOnly && !queued.Rules && queued.Text == message) return true;
			PendingMessages.Enqueue(new Pending { Text = message, Expires = Time.realtimeSinceStartup + 90, LobbyOnly = true });
			return true;
		}
		public static void CancelRules()
		{
			int count = PendingMessages.Count;
			while(count-- > 0) { var message = PendingMessages.Dequeue(); if(!message.Rules) PendingMessages.Enqueue(message); }
		}
		public static bool QueueRules(IReadOnlyList<string> lines)
		{
			Ensure(); if(!Host || !LobbyRules.CanSend || lines.Count == 0 || lines.Count > 8 || PendingMessages.Count >= 16) return false;
			string message = CombinedMessage(lines);
			if(message.Length == 0 || message.Length > MaximumPublicMessageLength) return false;
			foreach(var queued in PendingMessages) if(queued.Rules && queued.Text == message) return true;
			PendingMessages.Enqueue(new Pending { Text = message, Expires = Time.realtimeSinceStartup + 90, LobbyOnly = true, Rules = true });
			return true;
		}
		public static void Tick()
		{
			Ensure(); if(!Host) { PendingMessages.Clear(); return; }
			float now = Time.realtimeSinceStartup;
			while(PendingMessages.Count > 0 && (PendingMessages.Peek().Expires < now ||
				(PendingMessages.Peek().Rules ? !LobbyRules.CanSend : PendingMessages.Peek().LobbyOnly ? !RoundReports.Enabled : !ShareAlerts))) PendingMessages.Dequeue();
			if(PendingMessages.Count == 0 || now < nextSend) return;
			bool waiting = WaitingLobby;
			var local = PlayerControl.LocalPlayer;
			if(PendingMessages.Peek().LobbyOnly && (AmongUsClient.Instance.GameState == InnerNet.InnerNetClient.GameStates.Started ||
				(GameManager.Instance != null && GameManager.Instance.GameHasStarted))) { PendingMessages.Dequeue(); return; }
			if(!LocalReady || (!waiting && (MeetingHud.Instance == null ||
				AmongUsClient.Instance.GameState != InnerNet.InnerNetClient.GameStates.Started ||
				GameManager.Instance == null || !GameManager.Instance.GameHasStarted))) return;
			if(PendingMessages.Peek().LobbyOnly && !waiting) return;
			if(PendingMessages.Peek().LobbyOnly && !PendingMessages.Peek().Rules && !RoundReports.ReadyToSend) return;
			var chat = HudManager.Instance.Chat;
			float interval = SendInterval;
			if(!float.IsFinite(interval) || !float.IsFinite(chat.timeSinceLastMessage) || chat.timeSinceLastMessage < interval ||
				AmongUs.Data.DataManager.Settings.Multiplayer.ChatMode == InnerNet.QuickChatModes.QuickChatOnly) return;
			nextSend = now + interval;
			// A rejected send is not retried indefinitely. Ordinary chat restrictions win.
			var pending = PendingMessages.Dequeue();
			sendingText = pending.Text; sendingPlayer = local; renderedDuringSend = false;
			Echoes.RemoveAll(e => e.Until < now || e.Room != Key || (e.NetId == local.NetId && e.Text == pending.Text));
			if(Echoes.Count >= 16) Echoes.RemoveAt(0);
			var receipt = new Echo { Text = pending.Text, Room = Key, NetId = local.NetId, Until = now + 15 };
			Echoes.Add(receipt);
			try
			{
				if(local.RpcSendChat(pending.Text))
				{
					RecordSend(true);
					if(pending.Rules) LobbyRules.MarkSent();
					if(!renderedDuringSend) chat.AddChat(local, pending.Text);
				}
				else Echoes.Remove(receipt);
			}
			finally { sendingText = null; sendingPlayer = null; renderedDuringSend = false; }
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/LobbyExtension.cs`:

```cs
using HarmonyLib;
using UnityEngine;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
	internal static class LobbyExtension
	{
		public static bool Enabled = true;
		private static int lobbyInstance, offeredId, requestedId = -1, host, game;
		private static float expires;
		private static float nextAllowedRequest;
		private static bool available;
		private static bool awaitingConfirmation;
		private static float requestedAt;
		public static string Status { get; private set; } = "Waiting for the server to offer an extension. Inactivity closure is not overridden.";
		public static void Reset() { available = false; awaitingConfirmation = false; requestedId = -1; nextAllowedRequest = 0; Status = "Waiting for the server to offer an extension. Inactivity closure is not overridden."; }
		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(LobbyBehaviour), nameof(LobbyBehaviour.HandleLobbyTimerExtensionRequest))]
		private static class OnOffer
		{
			private static void Postfix(LobbyBehaviour __instance, int __0, bool __1, int __2, int __3)
			{
				int instance = __instance.GetInstanceID();
				bool changed = lobbyInstance != instance || game != AmongUsClient.Instance?.GameId || host != __2 || offeredId != __3 || available != (__1 && __0 > 0);
				if(lobbyInstance != instance || game != AmongUsClient.Instance?.GameId || host != __2) Reset();
				lobbyInstance = instance; offeredId = __3; host = __2;
				game = AmongUsClient.Instance == null ? 0 : AmongUsClient.Instance.GameId;
				available = __1 && __0 > 0; expires = Time.realtimeSinceStartup + __0;
				if(changed) AUAnticheatPlugin.Log.LogWarning($"Lobby extension offer: available={available}, seconds={__0}, offer={__3}, hostMatches={AmongUsClient.Instance?.HostId == __2}.");
				if(!available) Status = "The server has not offered another extension. Start the game before the lobby expires.";
			}
		}
		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(LobbyBehaviour), nameof(LobbyBehaviour.LobbyTimerExtended))]
		private static class OnExtended
		{
			private static void Postfix(LobbyBehaviour __instance)
			{
				var client = AmongUsClient.Instance;
				if(__instance.GetInstanceID() != lobbyInstance || client == null || client.GameId != game || client.HostId != host || requestedId < 0) return;
				available = false;
				awaitingConfirmation = false;
				Status = "The game confirmed a lobby extension. The server still controls the closing time.";
				AUAnticheatPlugin.Log.LogWarning("Lobby extension confirmation received.");
			}
		}
		public static void Tick()
		{
			var client = AmongUsClient.Instance; var lobby = LobbyBehaviour.Instance;
			if(client == null || lobby == null) { Reset(); return; }
			if(client.GameId != game || client.HostId != host || lobby.GetInstanceID() != lobbyInstance)
			{
				Reset(); game = client.GameId; host = client.HostId; lobbyInstance = lobby.GetInstanceID();
			}
			if(awaitingConfirmation && Time.realtimeSinceStartup - requestedAt >= 10)
			{
				awaitingConfirmation = false;
				Status = "No extension confirmation received. The lobby may still close; start before the countdown ends.";
				AUAnticheatPlugin.Log.LogWarning("Lobby extension request timed out without confirmation.");
			}
			if(!Enabled || !available || client == null || !client.AmHost || client.HostId != host || client.GameId != game || lobby == null ||
				lobby.GetInstanceID() != lobbyInstance || client.GameState != InnerNet.InnerNetClient.GameStates.Joined ||
				(GameManager.Instance != null && GameManager.Instance.GameHasStarted)) return;
			float remaining = expires - Time.realtimeSinceStartup;
			if(remaining <= 0) { available = false; Status = "Extension offer expired. No extension was confirmed."; return; }
			// Request as soon as offered: waiting until the last 30 seconds can
			// miss a short availability window. Still only one request per offer.
			if(requestedId == offeredId || Time.realtimeSinceStartup < nextAllowedRequest) return;
			requestedId = offeredId;
			// A fresh offer can expire within the old 30-second gate. Keep a paced
			// request interval and one request per offer, without missing short offers.
			nextAllowedRequest = Time.realtimeSinceStartup + 5;
			awaitingConfirmation = true; requestedAt = Time.realtimeSinceStartup;
			Status = "Extension requested; waiting for the game's confirmation. Do not assume the countdown stopped.";
			lobby.RpcExtendLobbyTimer(); // A synchronous confirmation must not be overwritten.
			AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Send("Lobby timer", "Requested the game's available lobby extension. Players stay in this lobby; the server decides whether to extend it.");
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/LobbyIdleMonitor.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
    internal static class LobbyIdleMonitor
    {
        private sealed class Idle { public uint NetId; public float X,Y,Since; public bool Warned; }
        private static readonly Dictionary<int,Idle> Players = new();
        private static string room = "";
        public static void Touch(PlayerControl player)
        {
            if(player != null && Players.TryGetValue(player.OwnerId,out var state) && state.NetId == player.NetId)
            { state.Since = Time.realtimeSinceStartup; state.Warned = false; }
        }
        public static void Tick()
        {
            if(!ChatModeration.AfkAlerts || !LobbyChat.WaitingLobby) { Players.Clear(); room = ""; return; }
            string current = $"{AmongUsClient.Instance.GameId}:{LobbyBehaviour.Instance.GetInstanceID()}";
            if(current != room) { Players.Clear(); room = current; }
            var present = new HashSet<int>();
            foreach(var player in PlayerControl.AllPlayerControls)
            {
                if(player == null || player.Data == null || player.Data.Disconnected || player == PlayerControl.LocalPlayer || player.OwnerId == AmongUsClient.Instance.HostId) continue;
                present.Add(player.OwnerId);
                var position = player.GetTruePosition();
                if(!float.IsFinite(position.x) || !float.IsFinite(position.y)) continue;
                if(!Players.TryGetValue(player.OwnerId,out var state) || state.NetId != player.NetId)
                    Players[player.OwnerId] = state = new Idle { NetId = player.NetId, X = position.x, Y = position.y, Since = Time.realtimeSinceStartup };
                float dx = position.x-state.X, dy = position.y-state.Y;
                if(dx*dx+dy*dy > 0.01f) { state.X=position.x; state.Y=position.y; Touch(player); }
                if(!state.Warned && Time.realtimeSinceStartup-state.Since >= ChatModeration.AfkSeconds)
                {
                    state.Warned=true;
                    AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Send("Possible AFK", $"{PublicText.Name(player.Data.PlayerName)} has no observed movement/chat for {ChatModeration.AfkSeconds}s. Review manually; menus or lag can look idle. No kick sent.");
                }
            }
            foreach(int id in new List<int>(Players.Keys)) if(!present.Contains(id)) Players.Remove(id);
        }
    }
}
```
