`source/anticheat/LobbyLevelPolicy.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using HarmonyLib;
using InnerNet;
using UnityEngine;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
	// Host admission preference, not a cheat detector. Levels are not proof of
	// account age or authenticity and this is not a reliable anti-raid boundary.
	internal static class LobbyLevelPolicy
	{
		public static bool Enabled { get; set; } = false;
		public static uint MinimumLevel { get; set; } = 10;
		private sealed class Observation
		{
			public uint NetId, Level;
			public bool RemovalRequested;
			public float StableSince;
		}
		private static readonly Dictionary<int, Observation> Players = new();
		private sealed class LevelRecord
		{
			public PlayerControl Player;
			public uint NetId;
			public uint? LastSet;
			public uint? LastLive;
			public bool SawNonzero;
			public string Diagnostic;
		}
		private static readonly Dictionary<int, LevelRecord> Levels = new();
		private static string levelRoom = "";
		private static LevelRecord Record(PlayerControl player)
		{
			string room = AmongUsClient.Instance == null ? "" : $"{AmongUsClient.Instance.GameId}:{AmongUsClient.Instance.HostId}";
			if(room != levelRoom) { Levels.Clear(); levelRoom = room; }
			if(!Levels.TryGetValue(player.OwnerId, out var record) || record.Player != player || record.NetId != player.NetId)
			{
				if(Levels.Count >= 64) Levels.Clear();
				Levels[player.OwnerId] = record = new LevelRecord { Player = player, NetId = player.NetId };
			}
			return record;
		}
		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(PlayerControl), nameof(PlayerControl.SetLevel))]
		private static class ObserveAppliedLevel
		{
			private static void Postfix(PlayerControl __instance, uint __0)
			{
				if(__instance == null || __0 == uint.MaxValue) return;
				var record = Record(__instance);
				record.LastSet = __0;
				if(__0 > 0) record.SawNonzero = true;
			}
		}
		internal static bool TryLevel(PlayerControl player, ClientData peer, out uint level, out string source)
		{
			level = uint.MaxValue; source = "waiting for complete player data";
			var record = Record(player);
			uint live = player.Data.PlayerLevel, joined = peer.PlayerLevel;
			if((live > 0 && live != uint.MaxValue) || (joined > 0 && joined != uint.MaxValue)) record.SawNonzero = true;
			if(player.Data.IsIncomplete) return false;
			// Live data is the same record used by the game's UI and other level
			// filters. A stale join snapshot must not permanently disable admission.
			if(live > 0 && live != uint.MaxValue) { record.LastLive = live; level = live; source = "live player record"; return true; }
			if(joined > 0 && joined != uint.MaxValue)
			{
				if((record.LastLive.HasValue && record.LastLive.Value != joined) ||
					(record.LastSet.HasValue && record.LastSet.Value > 0 && record.LastSet.Value != joined))
				{ source = "join snapshot conflicts with the last live level; waiting for live data"; return false; }
				level = joined; source = "current join record (live level unavailable)"; return true;
			}
			// Default zero and an explicit level-one update look identical in memory.
			// Require an observed SetLevel(0), complete data and no higher level seen
			// on this same player object. Never convert a timeout alone into evidence.
			if(live == 0 && joined == 0 && record.LastSet == 0 && !record.SawNonzero)
			{ level = 0; source = "observed level-one update"; return true; }
			source = record.SawNonzero ? "zero conflicts with an earlier level on this player object" : "level 1 is unconfirmed; waiting for a level update";
			if(record.Diagnostic != source)
			{
				record.Diagnostic = source;
				AUAnticheatPlugin.Log.LogWarning($"[Level filter] Lobby ID {player.OwnerId}: {source}. No level-based ban requested.");
			}
			return false;
		}
		private static string session = "";
		private static float nextScan;
		private static float nextRemoval, pendingSince;
		private static int pendingOwner = -1;
		private static uint pendingNetId;
		private static string pendingSession = "";
		public static void Forget(int ownerId) { Players.Remove(ownerId); Levels.Remove(ownerId); }
		// Keep the send cooldown across toggles/threshold edits; otherwise toggling
		// the rule could turn a paced policy back into a removal burst.
		public static void Reset() { ClearObservations(); Levels.Clear(); levelRoom = ""; pendingOwner = -1; pendingSession = ""; }
		private static void ClearObservations() { Players.Clear(); session = ""; nextScan = 0; }

		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(LobbyBehaviour), nameof(LobbyBehaviour.FixedUpdate))]
		private static class ObserveLobby
		{
			private static void Postfix()
			{
				var client = AmongUsClient.Instance;
				bool waiting = LobbyBehaviour.Instance != null && client != null &&
					client.GameState == InnerNetClient.GameStates.Joined &&
					(GameManager.Instance == null || !GameManager.Instance.GameHasStarted);
					if((!Enabled && !ModerationStore.EnforceBans) || !AUAnticheatPlugin.ProtectionsAttached || !waiting || !client.AmHost)
				{ ClearObservations(); return; }
				try
				{
					string current = $"{client.GameId}:{client.HostId}:{LobbyBehaviour.Instance.GetInstanceID()}:{MinimumLevel}";
					if(current != session) { ClearObservations(); session = current; nextRemoval = Math.Max(nextRemoval, Time.realtimeSinceStartup + 2); }
					float now = Time.realtimeSinceStartup;
					string room = $"{client.GameId}:{client.HostId}:{LobbyBehaviour.Instance.GetInstanceID()}";
					if(room != pendingSession) { pendingOwner = -1; pendingSession = room; }
					if(now < nextScan) return;
					nextScan = now + 0.1f;
					if(pendingOwner >= 0)
					{
						var pending = client.FindClientById(pendingOwner)?.Character;
						if(pending == null || pending.NetId != pendingNetId || pending.Data == null || pending.Data.Disconnected)
						{ pendingOwner = -1; nextRemoval = Math.Max(nextRemoval, now + 2); }
						else
						{
							if(now - pendingSince >= 15)
							{
								Enabled = false;
								ModerationStore.EnforceBans = false;
								AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Send("Lobby level rule", "Entry rule paused: a previous ban has not completed. No more bans will be sent. Check the lobby before enabling it again.");
							}
							return;
						}
					}
					if(now < nextRemoval) return;
					// Respect vanilla moderation availability. Never send a ban while
					// local host identity is unsettled or the game says banning is unavailable.
					if(client.ClientId != client.HostId || !client.CanBan()) return;
					foreach(var player in PlayerControl.AllPlayerControls)
					{
						if(player == null || player == PlayerControl.LocalPlayer || player.OwnerId == client.HostId || player.Data == null || player.Data.Disconnected ||
							client.FindClientById(player.OwnerId)?.Character != player) continue;
						uint level = player.Data.PlayerLevel;
						var peer = client.FindClientById(player.OwnerId);
						// Bind identity, but resolve the level separately: join snapshots
						// may lag behind the live record used by the game's UI.
						bool identityReady = peer != null && !peer.IsBeingCreated &&
							FriendCodeExceptions.Normalize(peer.FriendCode) != null &&
							FriendCodeExceptions.Normalize(peer.FriendCode) == FriendCodeExceptions.Normalize(player.Data.FriendCode);
						bool persistentBan = identityReady && ModerationStore.EnforceBans && ModerationStore.Contains(peer.FriendCode,"ban");
						string levelSource = "waiting for player identity";
						bool ready = identityReady && TryLevel(player, peer, out level, out levelSource);
						if(!persistentBan && !Enabled) continue;
						if(!ready && !persistentBan) { Players.Remove(player.OwnerId); continue; }
						if(!Players.TryGetValue(player.OwnerId, out var observed) || observed.NetId != player.NetId || observed.Level != level)
						{
							observed = new Observation { NetId = player.NetId, Level = level, StableSince = now };
							Players[player.OwnerId] = observed;
						}
						// TryLevel already requires an explicit level-one update and complete
						// data. Add a settling interval, not a timeout-based assumption.
						if(!persistentBan && level == 0 && now - observed.StableSince < 5f) continue;
						if(observed.RemovalRequested || (!persistentBan && !LobbyAdmissionRules.ShouldBan(Enabled, client.AmHost, waiting, false, ready,
							FriendCodeExceptions.Contains(peer.FriendCode), level, MinimumLevel))) continue;
						if(peer.Id != player.OwnerId || client.GetClientIdFromCharacter(player) != peer.Id || peer.Id == client.ClientId) continue;
						client.KickPlayer(player.OwnerId, true);
						if(!persistentBan) AUAnticheatPlugin.Log.LogWarning($"[Level filter] Ban requested: lobby ID {player.OwnerId}; displayed level {(ulong)level + 1}; minimum {MinimumLevel}; source={levelSource}; liveRaw={player.Data.PlayerLevel}; joinRaw={peer.PlayerLevel}. Entry policy, not cheating evidence.");
						pendingOwner = player.OwnerId; pendingNetId = player.NetId; pendingSince = now;
						nextRemoval = now + 2;
						observed.RemovalRequested = true;
						AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Send(persistentBan ? "Persistent ban" : "Lobby level rule",
							persistentBan ? $"Ban requested for {PublicText.Name(player.Data.PlayerName)}: matching your saved local ban list. Not a new cheating accusation." :
							$"Ban requested for {PublicText.Name(player.Data.PlayerName)}: level {(ulong)level + 1}, below this lobby's minimum of {MinimumLevel}. This is an entry rule, not a cheating accusation.");
						break; // One request, then wait for departure and cooldown. Never batch-ban a full lobby.
					}
				}
				catch(Exception exception)
				{
					Enabled = false;
					ModerationStore.EnforceBans = false;
					Reset();
					AUAnticheatPlugin.Log.LogWarning($"Disabled lobby level rule after {exception.GetType().Name}.");
					AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Send("Lobby level rule", "Level rule disabled after an error. Check the log before enabling it again.");
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/LobbyLoadouts.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using UnityEngine;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
    internal static class LobbyLoadouts
    {
        private static LoadoutStore store;
        private static float nextApply;
        public static string Status { get; private set; } = "Not initialized.";
        public static string[] Names => store?.Names ?? Array.Empty<string>();
        public static void Initialize(string directory) { store=new LoadoutStore(directory); Status=store.Status; }
        private static bool Ready()
        {
            var client=AmongUsClient.Instance;
            return client != null && client.AmHost && client.ClientId == client.HostId && LobbyBehaviour.Instance != null &&
                MeetingHud.Instance == null && client.GameState == InnerNet.InnerNetClient.GameStates.Joined &&
                GameManager.Instance != null && !GameManager.Instance.GameHasStarted && GameManager.Instance.LogicOptions != null &&
                GameOptionsManager.Instance?.CurrentGameOptions != null && PlayerControl.LocalPlayer != null &&
                (GameStartManager.Instance == null || GameStartManager.Instance.startState == GameStartManager.StartingStates.NotStarting);
        }
        public static bool Save(string name)
        {
            if(!Ready()) { Status="Host a waiting lobby first; stop the start countdown before saving."; return false; }
            try
            {
                byte[] bytes=GameManager.Instance.LogicOptions.gameOptionsFactory.ToBytes(GameOptionsManager.Instance.CurrentGameOptions,false);
                bool ok=store != null && store.Save(name,Application.version,bytes); Status=store?.Status ?? "Storage unavailable."; return ok;
            }
            catch(Exception e) { Status="Could not read the game's settings: "+e.GetType().Name; return false; }
        }
        public static void Delete(string name) { store?.Delete(name); Status=store?.Status ?? "Storage unavailable."; }
        public static bool Apply(string name)
        {
            if(!Ready()) { Status="Only the host can apply a loadout in a waiting lobby, before the start countdown."; return false; }
            if(Time.realtimeSinceStartup < nextApply) { Status="Wait a few seconds before applying another loadout."; return false; }
            var item=store?.Find(name);
            if(item == null || item.Build != Application.version) { Status="Loadout missing or saved on a different game build. Save a new loadout on this build."; return false; }
            var manager=GameOptionsManager.Instance; var logic=GameManager.Instance.LogicOptions;
            var previous=manager.CurrentGameOptions; var previousHost=manager.GameHostOptions;
            bool changed=false;
            try
            {
                var options=logic.gameOptionsFactory.FromBytes(Convert.FromBase64String(item.Payload));
                // Lobby mode/map/capacity are server session properties, not safe
                // to replace silently. Never evict players to fit a saved preset.
                if(options == null || options.Version != previous.Version || options.GameMode != previous.GameMode ||
                   options.MapId != previous.MapId || options.MaxPlayers != previous.MaxPlayers || options.AreInvalid(options.MaxPlayers))
                { Status="Select the same game mode, map and lobby size first. Invalid or incompatible settings were not applied."; return false; }
                nextApply=Time.realtimeSinceStartup+5;
                changed=true; manager.GameHostOptions=options; manager.CurrentGameOptions=options;
                logic.SetGameOptions(options); logic.SyncOptions();
                LobbyRules.Reset();
                Status="Loadout applied through normal game settings. Check the lobby settings before starting."; return true;
            }
            catch(Exception e)
            {
                if(changed) try { manager.GameHostOptions=previousHost; manager.CurrentGameOptions=previous; logic.SetGameOptions(previous); logic.SyncOptions(); } catch(Exception) { }
                Status="Apply failed ("+e.GetType().Name+"). Check settings before starting; a partial network update may have occurred."; return false;
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`source/anticheat/LobbyMovementGuard.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using HarmonyLib;
using Hazel;
using UnityEngine;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
	// Inspect explicit lobby teleport RPCs only. Interpolated walking is not a path trace.
	// This deliberately does not treat a wall crossing as evidence against an actor.
	internal static class LobbyMovementGuard
	{
		public static bool Enabled = true;
		public static int BlockedSnaps { get; private set; }
		private sealed class Sample
		{
			public uint NetId;
			public float ReadyAt, ObservedAt, SavedAt;
			public bool HasSafe;
			public Vector2 Feet;
		}
		private enum PathState { Clear, Touching, Crossed, Unknown }
		private static readonly Dictionary<int, Sample> Samples = new();
		private static string session = "";
		private static float lastNotice = -100;
		private static float nextScan;
		public static void Forget(int ownerId) => Samples.Remove(ownerId);
		public static void Reset()
		{
			Samples.Clear(); session = ""; lastNotice = -100; nextScan = 0;
			BlockedSnaps = 0;
		}
		private static bool Active()
		{
			return Enabled && AUAnticheatPlugin.ProtectionsAttached && LobbyBehaviour.Instance != null &&
				AmongUsClient.Instance != null && AmongUsClient.Instance.GameState == InnerNet.InnerNetClient.GameStates.Joined &&
				(GameManager.Instance == null || !GameManager.Instance.GameHasStarted);
		}
		private static void EnsureSession()
		{
			string key = $"{AmongUsClient.Instance.GameId}:{AmongUsClient.Instance.HostId}:{LobbyBehaviour.Instance.GetInstanceID()}";
			if(key != session) { Reset(); session = key; }
		}
		private static Sample GetSample(PlayerControl player, float now)
		{
			if(!Samples.TryGetValue(player.OwnerId, out Sample sample) || sample.NetId != player.NetId)
			{
				sample = new Sample { NetId = player.NetId, ReadyAt = now + MovementRecoveryRules.JoinGraceSeconds, ObservedAt = now };
				Samples[player.OwnerId] = sample;
			}
			return sample;
		}
		private static bool Stable(PlayerControl player)
		{
			return player != null && player.Data != null && !player.Data.IsDead && player.NetTransform != null &&
				player.MyPhysics != null && !player.MyPhysics.DoingCustomAnimation && !player.inVent && !player.onLadder && !player.inMovingPlat;
		}
		private static bool Solid(Collider2D collider)
		{
			return collider != null && collider.enabled && !collider.isTrigger &&
				(collider.attachedRigidbody == null || collider.attachedRigidbody.bodyType == RigidbodyType2D.Static);
		}
		private static bool ClearPoint(Vector2 point)
		{
			if(!MovementRecoveryRules.Finite(point.x, point.y) || Constants.ShipOnlyMask == 0) return false;
			var hits = Physics2D.OverlapCircleAll(point, 0.06f, Constants.ShipOnlyMask);
			if(hits.Length > 64) return false;
			foreach(var hit in hits) if(Solid(hit)) return false;
			return true;
		}
		private static PathState Trace(Vector2 start, Vector2 end)
		{
			if(!MovementRecoveryRules.Finite(end.x, end.y) || !ClearPoint(start) || !ClearPoint(end)) return PathState.Unknown;
			float distance = Vector2.Distance(start, end);
			if(distance < 0.01f) return PathState.Clear;
			var hits = Physics2D.LinecastAll(start, end, Constants.ShipOnlyMask);
			if(hits.Length > 64) return PathState.Unknown;
			bool touched = false;
			foreach(var hit in hits)
			{
				if(!Solid(hit.collider)) continue;
				if(hit.fraction <= 0) return PathState.Unknown;
				if(MovementRecoveryRules.Crossed(distance, hit.distance)) return PathState.Crossed;
				touched = true;
			}
			return touched ? PathState.Touching : PathState.Clear;
		}
		private static void Save(Sample sample, Vector2 feet, float now)
		{
			// Wall contact invalidates the old sample: retaining it turns walking
			// around furniture into an imaginary straight-line wall crossing.
			sample.HasSafe = ClearPoint(feet);
			if(!sample.HasSafe) return;
			sample.Feet = feet; sample.SavedAt = now;
		}
		private static void Notice(string text, float now)
		{
			if(now - lastNotice < 2) return;
			lastNotice = now;
			AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Send("Movement protection", text + " Sender unverified; no points or ban.");
		}
		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(LobbyBehaviour), nameof(LobbyBehaviour.FixedUpdate))]
		private static class ObserveLobby
		{
			private static void Postfix()
			{
				if(!Active()) { Samples.Clear(); return; }
				try
				{
					EnsureSession();
					float now = Time.realtimeSinceStartup;
					if(now < nextScan) return;
					nextScan = now + 0.05f;
					foreach(PlayerControl player in PlayerControl.AllPlayerControls)
					{
						if(player == null || player.Data == null) continue;
						Sample sample = GetSample(player, now);
						float elapsed = now - sample.ObservedAt;
						sample.ObservedAt = now;
						if(!Stable(player)) { sample.HasSafe = false; sample.ReadyAt = now + 1; continue; }
						if(now < sample.ReadyAt) continue;
						Vector2 feet = player.GetTruePosition();
						if(!MovementRecoveryRules.Fresh(elapsed)) { sample.HasSafe = false; sample.ReadyAt = now + 1; continue; }
						// Never SnapTo/RpcSnapTo based on ordinary observed motion. A chord
						// between network samples cannot reconstruct the player's real path.
						Save(sample, feet, now);
					}
				}
				catch(Exception e)
				{
					Enabled = false; Samples.Clear();
					AUAnticheatPlugin.Log.LogWarning($"Lobby movement guard disabled after {e.GetType().Name}: {e.Message}");
					AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Send("Movement protection", "Lobby movement guard disabled after an error. Other protections remain enabled.");
				}
			}
		}
		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(CustomNetworkTransform), nameof(CustomNetworkTransform.HandleRpc))]
		private static class BeforeLobbySnap
		{
			[HarmonyPriority(Priority.First)]
			private static bool Prefix(CustomNetworkTransform __instance, byte __0, MessageReader __1)
			{
				if(__0 != (byte)RpcCalls.SnapTo || !Active() || __instance == null) return true;
				PlayerControl player = __instance.myPlayer;
				if(!Stable(player)) return true;
				using var evidence = SenderEvidence.Begin("Lobby teleport", __0, player.NetId, player.OwnerId, PublicText.Name(player.Data.PlayerName));
				EnsureSession();
				float now = Time.realtimeSinceStartup;
				Sample sample = GetSample(player, now);
				if(!sample.HasSafe || now < sample.ReadyAt || !MovementRecoveryRules.Fresh(now - sample.SavedAt)) return true;
				int oldPosition = __1.Position;
				try
				{
					Vector2 destination = NetHelpers.ReadVector2(__1);
					__1.ReadUInt16();
					Vector2 feetOffset = player.GetTruePosition() - (Vector2)player.transform.position;
					try
					{
						if(!SceneBoundaryMonitor.OutsideLobby(destination+feetOffset) && Trace(sample.Feet, destination + feetOffset) != PathState.Crossed) return true;
					}
					catch(Exception) { sample.HasSafe = false; return true; } // Geometry failure is not a malformed packet.
					BlockedSnaps++;
					SenderEvidence.Record();
					Notice($"Affected: {PublicText.Name(player.Data.PlayerName)}. Blocked a lobby teleport across a wall or outside the loaded lobby bounds before it applied locally.", now);
					return false;
				}
				catch(Exception) { return PacketPreflight.Block("invalid lobby teleport payload"); }
				finally { __1.Position = oldPosition; }
			}
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/LobbyRules.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using UnityEngine;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
    // User-authored rules, stored locally. Only ordinary, paced host chat is used.
    internal static class LobbyRules
    {
        private static bool enabled;
        private static int delay = 10;
        private static Action<bool, int> saveSettings;
        private static string[] lines = Array.Empty<string>();
        private static string fullSession = "";
        private static float fullSince;
        private static bool posted;
        private static string sentFullSession = "";
        public static void NewRound() { sentFullSession = ""; Reset(); }
        public static void Disconnect() { sentFullSession = ""; Reset(); }
        public static void MarkSent() { sentFullSession = fullSession; Status = "Rules sent once for this full lobby. Waiting for the next refill."; }
        public static string FilePath { get; private set; } = "Not initialized";
        public static string Status { get; private set; } = "No rules loaded.";
        public static string Preview => string.Join("\n", lines);
        public static string MessageInfo
        {
            get
            {
                int length = LobbyChat.CombinedMessage(lines).Length;
                return length > LobbyChat.MaximumPublicMessageLength ?
                    $"Too long: {length}/{LobbyChat.MaximumPublicMessageLength} characters including separators. Shorten the rules; nothing will be posted. Your saved text is kept." :
                    $"One chat message: {length}/{LobbyChat.MaximumPublicMessageLength} characters including separators. No label is added.";
            }
        }
        public static string DraftMessageInfo(string text)
        {
            var draft = new List<string>();
            foreach(string raw in (text ?? "").Replace("\r", "").Split('\n'))
            {
                string line = raw.Trim();
                if(line.Length > 0 && !line.StartsWith("#")) draft.Add(line);
            }
            int length = LobbyChat.CombinedMessage(draft).Length;
            return $"One message: {length}/100 characters including separators. No label is added." +
                (length > 100 ? " Too long to save; shorten the rules." : "");
        }
        public static bool SaveText(string text)
        {
            if(text == null || System.Text.Encoding.UTF8.GetByteCount(text)>4096) { Status="Rules too large (maximum 4 KB). Nothing was saved."; return false; }
            int count=0;
            var accepted = new List<string>();
            foreach(string raw in text.Replace("\r","").Split('\n'))
            {
                string line=raw.Trim(); if(line.Length==0||line.StartsWith("#")) continue;
                if(line.Length>100||++count>8) { Status="Use up to 8 short rules fitting one 100-character message. Nothing was saved."; return false; }
                accepted.Add(line);
            }
            if(LobbyChat.CombinedMessage(accepted).Length > LobbyChat.MaximumPublicMessageLength)
            { Status="Rules exceed one 100-character message including separators. Nothing was saved."; return false; }
            try
            {
                File.WriteAllText(FilePath+".tmp",text.Replace("\r",""));
                File.Move(FilePath+".tmp",FilePath,true);
                Reload(); return true;
            }
            catch(Exception) { Status="Could not save rules. Your previous file was kept; check folder permissions."; return false; }
        }
        public static bool Enabled
        {
            get => enabled;
            set { if(enabled == value) return; enabled = value; Reset(); Save(); }
        }
        public static int DelaySeconds
        {
            get => delay;
            set { value = Math.Clamp(value, 1, 120); if(delay == value) return; delay = value; Reset(); Save(); }
        }
        private static void Save()
        {
            try { saveSettings?.Invoke(enabled, delay); }
            catch(Exception) { Status = "Could not save settings. Check folder permissions."; }
        }
        public static void Initialize(string directory, bool use, int seconds, Action<bool,int> persist)
        {
            enabled = use; delay = Math.Clamp(seconds,1,120); saveSettings = persist; Reset();
            FilePath = Path.Combine(directory, "lobby-rules.txt");
            try
            {
                Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
                // Never overwrite an existing rules list.
                if(!File.Exists(FilePath)) File.WriteAllText(FilePath,
                    "# Edit here or use the rules editor under Lobby in the menu.\n" +
                    "# All rules share one message: 100 characters including separators; no label added. Lines starting # are comments.\n" +
                    "Be respectful. No cheating or harassment.\n");
                Reload();
            }
            catch(Exception) { lines = Array.Empty<string>(); Status = "Rules file could not be opened. Check folder permissions."; }
        }
        public static void Reload()
        {
            Reset(); lines = Array.Empty<string>();
            try
            {
                using var file = new FileStream(FilePath,FileMode.Open,FileAccess.Read,FileShare.ReadWrite);
                if(file.Length > 4096) { Status = "Rules file is too large. Maximum: 4 KB."; return; }
                using var reader = new StreamReader(file);
                var accepted = new List<string>();
                int chars = 0;
                while(!reader.EndOfStream)
                {
                    string line = reader.ReadLine() ?? "";
                    chars += line.Length + 1;
                    if(chars > 4096) { Status = "Rules file is too large. Maximum: 4 KB."; return; }
                    line = line.Trim();
                    if(line.Length == 0 || line.StartsWith("#")) continue;
                    if(line.Length > 100 || accepted.Count == 8) { Status = "Use up to 8 short rules fitting one message. Nothing was queued. Existing file kept."; return; }
                    string safe = PublicText.Clean(line,int.MaxValue);
                    if(!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(safe)) accepted.Add(safe);
                }
                lines = accepted.ToArray();
                Status = lines.Length == 0 ? "No rules loaded. Edit the file and reload." : $"{lines.Length} rules loaded. Saved on this device.";
            }
            catch(Exception) { Status = "Could not read rules. Check the file and click Reload saved rules."; }
        }
        private static string FullLobbyKey()
        {
            var client = AmongUsClient.Instance;
            if(!enabled || !AUAnticheatPlugin.ProtectionsAttached || client == null || !client.AmHost ||
               LobbyBehaviour.Instance == null || MeetingHud.Instance != null || client.GameState != InnerNet.InnerNetClient.GameStates.Joined ||
               (GameManager.Instance != null && GameManager.Instance.GameHasStarted)) return "";
            var ids = new HashSet<int>(); var roster = new List<string>();
            foreach(var player in PlayerControl.AllPlayerControls)
            {
                if(player == null || player.Data == null || player.Data.Disconnected) continue;
                var peer = client.FindClientById(player.OwnerId);
                if(peer == null || peer.IsBeingCreated || peer.Character != player) continue;
                if(ids.Add(player.OwnerId)) roster.Add($"{player.OwnerId}:{player.NetId}");
            }
            int capacity = GameOptionsManager.Instance?.CurrentGameOptions?.MaxPlayers ?? 0;
            if(capacity < 4 || capacity > 15 || ids.Count != capacity) return "";
            roster.Sort(StringComparer.Ordinal);
            return $"{client.GameId}:{client.HostId}:{LobbyBehaviour.Instance.GetInstanceID()}:{capacity}:" + string.Join(",",roster);
        }
        public static bool CanSend => enabled && fullSession.Length > 0 && fullSession != sentFullSession && fullSession == FullLobbyKey();
        public static void Reset() { if(posted) Status = "Waiting for a stable full lobby."; fullSession = ""; posted = false; LobbyChat.CancelRules(); }
        public static void Tick()
        {
            string current = FullLobbyKey();
            if(current.Length == 0)
            {
                // Leaving a full lobby rearms even if the same player later returns.
                // Toggling automation off/on alone must not duplicate a sent post.
                if(enabled) sentFullSession = "";
                Reset(); return;
            }
            if(current != fullSession)
            {
                if(current != sentFullSession) sentFullSession = "";
                Reset(); fullSession = current; fullSince = Time.realtimeSinceStartup;
            }
            if(LobbyChat.CombinedMessage(lines).Length > LobbyChat.MaximumPublicMessageLength) { Status = MessageInfo; return; }
            if(posted || sentFullSession == current || lines.Length == 0 || Time.realtimeSinceStartup - fullSince < delay) return;
            if(LobbyChat.QueueRules(lines)) { posted = true; Status = "One rules message queued for this fill. Waiting for the normal chat cooldown."; }
        }
    }
}
```

`source/anticheat/LobbyServices.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using UnityEngine;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
	internal static class LobbyServices
	{
		private static float nextTick;
		private static bool activityFailed, extensionFailed, reportsFailed, chatFailed, rulesFailed, boundaryFailed, idleFailed;
		public static void Tick()
		{
			if(!AUAnticheatPlugin.ProtectionsAttached || Time.realtimeSinceStartup < nextTick) return;
			nextTick = Time.realtimeSinceStartup + 0.1f;
			Run(ActivityTracker.Tick, ref activityFailed, "Activity tracking");
			Run(LobbyIdleMonitor.Tick, ref idleFailed, "Lobby idle alerts");
			Run(SceneBoundaryMonitor.Tick, ref boundaryFailed, "Scene boundary observations");
			Run(LobbyExtension.Tick, ref extensionFailed, "Lobby extension");
			Run(RoundReports.Tick, ref reportsFailed, "Match reports");
			Run(LobbyRules.Tick, ref rulesFailed, "Lobby rules");
			Run(LobbyChat.Tick, ref chatFailed, "Shared chat alerts");
		}
		private static void Run(Action action, ref bool failed, string name)
		{
			if(failed) return;
			try { action(); }
			catch(Exception e)
			{
				failed = true;
				AUAnticheatPlugin.Log.LogWarning($"{name} disabled: {e}");
				AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Send(name, "This feature stopped after an error. Other protections remain active. Please check the log.");
			}
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/MeetingProtection.cs`:

```cs
using HarmonyLib;
using Il2CppInterop.Runtime.InteropTypes.Arrays;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
	internal static class MeetingProtection
	{
		public static bool Enabled = true;
		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(MeetingHud), nameof(MeetingHud.VotingComplete))]
		private static class BeforeResults
		{
			private static bool Prefix(Il2CppStructArray<MeetingHud.VoterState> __0)
			{
				if(!Enabled) return true;
				if(__0 == null || __0.Length > 252) return PacketPreflight.Block("invalid meeting result size");
				byte[] voters = new byte[__0.Length];
				for(int i = 0; i < voters.Length; i++) voters[i] = __0[i].VoterId;
				return MeetingResultRules.UniqueVoters(voters) || PacketPreflight.Block("meeting result lists an invalid or repeated voter");
			}
		}
		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(GameData), nameof(GameData.OnMeetingStart))]
		private static class MeetingStarted
		{
			private static void Prefix()
			{
				Anticheat.GrantMeetingTransitionGrace(3);
				foreach(var player in PlayerControl.AllPlayerControls) Anticheat.GrantMovementGrace(player, 4);
			}
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/MeetingResultRules.cs`:

```cs
using System;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
	internal static class MeetingResultRules
	{
		// Check only immutable shape, not stale role/alive/meeting snapshots.
		// Vote targets retain all native sentinel values (skip/dead/abstain).
		public static bool UniqueVoters(ReadOnlySpan<byte> voters)
		{
			if(voters.Length > 252) return false;
			Span<bool> seen = stackalloc bool[256];
			seen.Clear();
			foreach(byte id in voters)
			{
				if(id >= 253 || seen[id]) return false;
				seen[id] = true;
			}
			return true;
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/ModerationStore.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text.Json;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
    // Independent local-only policy store. No community lists, endpoints or tokens.
    internal static class ModerationStore
    {
        public sealed class Entry { public string FriendCode { get; set; } public string Kind { get; set; } }
        private static List<Entry> entries = new();
        private static string directory;
        private static bool loaded;
        public static bool EnforceBans = true;
        public static string Status = "Local moderation lists.";
        public static string[] Words = Array.Empty<string>(), Names = Array.Empty<string>();
        public static string DirectoryPath => directory ?? "Not initialized";
        public static Entry[] Snapshot() => entries.ToArray();
        public static bool Contains(string code, string kind)
        {
            code = FriendCodeExceptions.Normalize(code);
            return code != null && entries.Exists(e => e.FriendCode == code && e.Kind == kind);
        }
        public static void Initialize(string path) { directory = path; entries = new(); Words = Names = Array.Empty<string>(); loaded = false; Reload(); }
        public static void Reload()
        {
            if(directory == null) return;
            try
            {
                Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
                string path = Path.Combine(directory, "moderation-list.json");
                var read = new List<Entry>();
                if(File.Exists(path))
                {
                    if(new FileInfo(path).Length > 131072) throw new IOException();
                    read = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<List<Entry>>(File.ReadAllText(path)) ?? new();
                }
                if(read.Count > 512 || read.Any(e => e == null || FriendCodeExceptions.Normalize(e.FriendCode) == null || !ValidKind(e.Kind))) throw new FormatException();
                foreach(var entry in read) entry.FriendCode = FriendCodeExceptions.Normalize(entry.FriendCode);
                var words = ReadFilters("filtered-words.txt"); var names = ReadFilters("filtered-names.txt");
                entries = read; Words = words; Names = names;
                loaded = true;
                Status = "Lists loaded locally. Watchlist entries are not proof of cheating.";
            }
            catch(Exception) { loaded = false; Status = "Could not load moderation files. Previous lists kept; fix files and reload before saving."; }
        }
        private static string[] ReadFilters(string name)
        {
            string path = Path.Combine(directory, name);
            if(!File.Exists(path)) File.WriteAllText(path, "# One literal word or phrase per line. No regular expressions.\n");
            if(new FileInfo(path).Length > 16384) throw new IOException();
            var lines = File.ReadAllLines(path).Select(s => s.Trim()).Where(s => s.Length > 0 && !s.StartsWith("#")).ToArray();
            if(lines.Length > 128 || lines.Any(s => s.Length > 100)) throw new FormatException();
            return lines;
        }
        private static bool ValidKind(string kind) => kind == "ban" || kind == "block" || kind == "watch";
        public static bool Set(string code, string kind, bool enabled)
        {
            code = FriendCodeExceptions.Normalize(code);
            if(!loaded) { Status = "Fix moderation files and reload before saving; damaged files will not be overwritten."; return false; }
            if(directory == null || code == null || !ValidKind(kind)) { Status = "A matching current friend code is required."; return false; }
            var next = entries.Where(e => !(e.FriendCode == code && e.Kind == kind)).ToList();
            if(enabled) next.Add(new Entry { FriendCode = code, Kind = kind });
            if(next.Count > 512) { Status = "List limit reached (512 entries)."; return false; }
            try
            {
                string path = Path.Combine(directory, "moderation-list.json");
                File.WriteAllText(path + ".tmp", JsonSerializer.Serialize(next, new JsonSerializerOptions { WriteIndented = true }));
                File.Move(path + ".tmp", path, true); entries = next;
                Status = "Saved on this device. No data was uploaded."; return true;
            }
            catch(Exception) { Status = "Save failed. Previous list kept."; return false; }
        }
    }
}
```

`source/anticheat/MovementContext.cs`:

```cs
namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
	internal static class MovementContext
	{
		public static bool StableLivingPlayer(PlayerControl player)
		{
			if(player == null || player.Data == null) return false;
			return MovementSafetyRules.CanInspectLivingMovement(
				GameManager.Instance != null && GameManager.Instance.GameHasStarted && LobbyBehaviour.Instance == null && ShipStatus.Instance != null,
				player.roleAssigned && player.Data.Role != null && player.MyPhysics != null,
				player.Data.IsDead || RoleManager.IsGhostRole(player.Data.RoleType),
				Anticheat.IsRoundStartGrace() || Anticheat.HasMovementGrace(player) || Anticheat.HasGhostRoleGrace(player) || Anticheat.HasMeetingTransitionGrace(),
				MeetingHud.Instance != null,
				player.inVent || player.onLadder || player.inMovingPlat || (player.MyPhysics != null && player.MyPhysics.DoingCustomAnimation));
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/MovementMonitor.cs`:

```cs
using HarmonyLib;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
	internal static class MovementMonitor
	{
		private sealed class MovementSample
		{
			public Vector2 Position;
			public DateTime TimeUtc;
			public DateTime SpeedWindowStartedUtc;
			public float PathDistance;
			public int ConsecutiveSpeedWindows;
			public int ConsecutiveLargeJumps;
			public DateTime LargeJumpSequenceStartedUtc;
		}

		private static readonly Dictionary<int, MovementSample> Samples = new Dictionary<int, MovementSample>();
		private static string sessionKey = string.Empty;
		public static void Reset() { Samples.Clear(); sessionKey = string.Empty; }
		public static void Forget(int ownerId) { Samples.Remove(ownerId); }

		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(CustomNetworkTransform), nameof(CustomNetworkTransform.Deserialize))]
		private static class OnMovementData
		{
			private static void Postfix(CustomNetworkTransform __instance)
			{
				if(!Anticheat.Enabled || __instance == null) return;

				PlayerControl player = __instance.myPlayer;
				if(player == null || player == PlayerControl.LocalPlayer || player.Data == null) return;
				if(GameManager.Instance == null || !GameManager.Instance.GameHasStarted || LobbyBehaviour.Instance != null) return;

				ResetForNewSession();
				using var evidence = SenderEvidence.Begin("Movement observation", null, player.NetId, player.OwnerId, PublicText.Name(player.Data.PlayerName));
				Vector2 position = player.GetTruePosition();
				DateTime now = DateTime.UtcNow;

				if(!Samples.TryGetValue(player.OwnerId, out MovementSample sample))
				{
					Samples[player.OwnerId] = new MovementSample { Position = position, TimeUtc = now, SpeedWindowStartedUtc = now };
					return;
				}

				Vector2 previousPosition = sample.Position;
				double elapsed = (now - sample.TimeUtc).TotalSeconds;
				float distance = Vector2.Distance(previousPosition, position);
				sample.Position = position;
				sample.TimeUtc = now;

				if(elapsed < 0.03 || elapsed > 2 || !MovementContext.StableLivingPlayer(player))
				{
					ResetSpeedWindow(sample, now);
					sample.ConsecutiveLargeJumps = 0;
					return;
				}

				float expectedSpeed = player.MyPhysics != null ? player.MyPhysics.TrueSpeed : 3.0f;
				float allowedDistance = Math.Max(3.5f, expectedSpeed * (float)elapsed * 5.0f + 2.0f);
				if(distance > allowedDistance)
				{
					// One correction is ambiguous: kills, transitions and network catch-up can
					// all create a single large deserialize step. Exclude it from sustained
					// speed accounting and require correlation unless it is a huge teleport.
					if(sample.ConsecutiveLargeJumps == 0) sample.LargeJumpSequenceStartedUtc = now;
					sample.ConsecutiveLargeJumps++;
					if(sample.ConsecutiveLargeJumps >= 3)
					{
						double sequenceSeconds = (now - sample.LargeJumpSequenceStartedUtc).TotalSeconds;
						// A burst shorter than this is normally buffered network state being
						// replayed after lag, not three independently observed movements.
						if(sequenceSeconds >= 0.35)
						{
							bool extremeSequence = distance > 18.0f && sequenceSeconds >= 0.75;
							Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} produced repeated abnormal movement over {sequenceSeconds:F2}s ({distance:F1} units in the latest step; safe step {allowedDistance:F1}).", extremeSequence);
						}
						sample.ConsecutiveLargeJumps = 0;
						sample.LargeJumpSequenceStartedUtc = DateTime.MinValue;
					}

					ResetSpeedWindow(sample, now, false);
					return;
				}

				sample.ConsecutiveLargeJumps = 0;
				sample.LargeJumpSequenceStartedUtc = DateTime.MinValue;
				sample.PathDistance += distance;
				CheckSustainedSpeed(player, sample, now, expectedSpeed);
			}
		}

		private static void CheckSustainedSpeed(PlayerControl player, MovementSample sample, DateTime now, float expectedSpeed)
		{
			double windowSeconds = (now - sample.SpeedWindowStartedUtc).TotalSeconds;
			if(windowSeconds < 1.0) return;
			if(windowSeconds > 3.0)
			{
				ResetSpeedWindow(sample, now);
				return;
			}

			float allowedPath = expectedSpeed * (float)windowSeconds * 1.45f + 0.75f;
			if(sample.PathDistance > allowedPath)
			{
				float ratio = allowedPath > 0 ? sample.PathDistance / allowedPath : float.MaxValue;
				sample.ConsecutiveSpeedWindows++;
				bool extreme = ratio >= 2.4f;
				if(extreme || sample.ConsecutiveSpeedWindows >= 2)
				{
					Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} sustained {sample.PathDistance / (float)windowSeconds:F1} units/s (safe path limit {allowedPath / (float)windowSeconds:F1}).", extreme);
					sample.ConsecutiveSpeedWindows = 0;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				sample.ConsecutiveSpeedWindows = 0;
			}

			ResetSpeedWindow(sample, now, false);
		}

		private static void ResetSpeedWindow(MovementSample sample, DateTime now, bool resetConsecutive = true)
		{
			sample.SpeedWindowStartedUtc = now;
			sample.PathDistance = 0;
			if(resetConsecutive) sample.ConsecutiveSpeedWindows = 0;
		}

		private static void ResetForNewSession()
		{
			string current = AmongUsClient.Instance == null ? "offline" : $"{AmongUsClient.Instance.GameId}:{AmongUsClient.Instance.HostId}";
			if(current == sessionKey) return;

			Samples.Clear();
			sessionKey = current;
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/MovementRecoveryRules.cs`:

```cs
using System;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
	internal static class MovementRecoveryRules
	{
		public const float JoinGraceSeconds = 3;
		public const float MaximumSampleGap = 0.5f;
		public const float WallMargin = 0.2f;
		public static bool Finite(float x, float y) => float.IsFinite(x) && float.IsFinite(y);
		public static bool Fresh(float elapsed) => float.IsFinite(elapsed) && elapsed >= 0 && elapsed <= MaximumSampleGap;
		public static bool Crossed(float distance, float hitDistance)
			=> float.IsFinite(distance) && float.IsFinite(hitDistance) && hitDistance > 0.01f && hitDistance <= distance && distance - hitDistance > WallMargin;
		public static bool CanBroadcast(bool isLocalPlayer, bool isHost, bool serverAuthority)
			// Owning a player does not authorize corrective jumps on official servers.
			=> isHost && !serverAuthority;
		public static bool ConfirmedMotion(int observations, float duration)
			=> observations >= 2 && float.IsFinite(duration) && duration >= 0.12f && duration <= 1;
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/PacketPreflight.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using Hazel;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
	internal static class PacketPreflight
	{
		public static long BlockedPackets { get; private set; }
		public static string LastBlockReason { get; private set; } = "None";
		private static DateTime lastNoticeUtc;

		public static bool ValidateBatch(MessageReader reader, bool rejectUnknownTags)
		{
			try
			{
				if(!PacketRules.ValidateBatch(CopyRemaining(reader), rejectUnknownTags, out string reason)) return Block(reason);
				return true;
			}
			catch(Exception) { return Block("invalid packet buffer bounds"); }
		}

		public static bool ValidateRpc(RpcCalls rpc, MessageReader reader)
		{
			try
			{
				if(!PacketRules.ValidateRpc((byte)rpc, CopyRemaining(reader), out string reason)) return Block(reason);
				return true;
			}
			catch(Exception) { return Block("invalid RPC buffer bounds"); }
		}

		private static byte[] CopyRemaining(MessageReader reader)
		{
			if(reader == null || reader.Buffer == null) throw new FormatException();
			int remaining = reader.BytesRemaining;
			long start = (long)reader.Offset + reader.Position;
			if(remaining < 0 || remaining > 65535 || start < 0 || start + remaining > reader.Buffer.Length) throw new FormatException();
			byte[] copy = new byte[remaining];
			for(int i = 0; i < remaining; i++) copy[i] = reader.Buffer[(int)start + i];
			return copy;
		}

		public static bool Block(string reason)
		{
			LobbyChat.ProtectionNotice();
			SenderEvidence.Record();
			BlockedPackets++;
			LastBlockReason = reason;
			DateTime now = DateTime.UtcNow;
			if((now - lastNoticeUtc).TotalSeconds >= 2)
			{
				lastNoticeUtc = now;
				AUAnticheatPlugin.Log.LogWarning($"Rejected packet before dispatch: {reason}");
				AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Send("Protection", $"Rejected packet before processing: {reason}. Sender unknown; no player penalized.");
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static void Reset()
		{
			BlockedPackets = 0;
			LastBlockReason = "None";
			lastNoticeUtc = DateTime.MinValue;
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/PacketRules.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Text;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
	// Pure managed rules, shared verbatim with the regression harness. Numeric IDs
	// match RpcCalls/GameDataTypes in the inspected v19s (build 7489) assemblies.
	internal static class PacketRules
	{
		private static readonly UTF8Encoding StrictUtf8 = new UTF8Encoding(false, true);

		public static bool TryPackedUInt(ReadOnlySpan<byte> bytes, ref int position, out uint value)
		{
			value = 0;
			for(int index = 0; index < 5; index++)
			{
				if(position < 0 || position >= bytes.Length) return false;
				byte next = bytes[position++];
				if(index == 4 && (next & 0xf0) != 0) return false;
				value |= (uint)(next & 0x7f) << (7 * index);
				if((next & 0x80) == 0) return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static bool ValidString(ReadOnlySpan<byte> bytes, ref int position, int maximumBytes)
		{
			if(!TryPackedUInt(bytes, ref position, out uint length) || length > maximumBytes || length > bytes.Length - position) return false;
			try { StrictUtf8.GetCharCount(bytes.Slice(position, (int)length)); }
			catch(DecoderFallbackException) { return false; }
			position += (int)length;
			return true;
		}

		public static bool ValidateBatch(ReadOnlySpan<byte> bytes, bool rejectUnknownTags, out string reason)
		{
			int cursor = 0;
			int messages = 0;
			while(cursor < bytes.Length)
			{
				if(++messages > 96) return Fail("excessive nested-message count", out reason);
				if(bytes.Length - cursor < 3) return Fail("truncated nested-message header", out reason);
				int length = bytes[cursor] | (bytes[cursor + 1] << 8);
				byte tag = bytes[cursor + 2];
				cursor += 3;
				if(length > bytes.Length - cursor) return Fail("nested message extends past its packet", out reason);
				ReadOnlySpan<byte> child = bytes.Slice(cursor, length);
				int field = 0;
				if(rejectUnknownTags && !KnownGameDataTag(tag)) return Fail("unsupported nested-message tag", out reason);
				if(tag == 1 || tag == 2 || tag == 5 || tag == 6 || tag == 7)
				{
					if(!TryPackedUInt(child, ref field, out _)) return Fail("invalid nested object/client identifier", out reason);
				}
				if(tag == 2)
				{
					if(field >= child.Length) return Fail("missing RPC identifier", out reason);
					byte rpc = child[field++];
					if(!ValidateRpc(rpc, child.Slice(field), out reason)) return false;
				}
				if(tag == 6 && !ValidString(child, ref field, 256)) return Fail("invalid scene-name string", out reason);
				cursor += length;
			}
			reason = null;
			return true;
		}

		public static bool ValidateRpc(byte rpc, ReadOnlySpan<byte> bytes, out string reason)
		{
			int position = 0;
			bool valid;
			// Only verified required fields are checked. Unreviewed/custom RPCs and
			// trailing extension fields are left to the game, never called cheats.
				switch(rpc)
			{
				case 66: valid = bytes.Length >= 4; break; // Judge: actor, target, ushort nonce
				case 67:
					// Influencer: packed length followed by 1..3 image indices. Check
					// before the native array reader allocates or the feed renders.
					valid = TryPackedUInt(bytes, ref position, out uint images) &&
						images >= 1 && images <= 3 && images <= bytes.Length - position;
					break;
				case 1: case 19: case 20: case 34: case 38: case 47: case 48:
					valid = TryPackedUInt(bytes, ref position, out _); break;
				case 5: case 13: case 39: case 40: case 41: case 42: case 43:
					valid = ValidString(bytes, ref position, rpc == 13 ? 1600 : 512); break;
				case 6:
					position = 4;
					valid = bytes.Length >= 4 && ValidString(bytes, ref position, 512); break;
				case 0: case 7: case 11: case 14: case 27:
					valid = bytes.Length >= 1; break;
				case 8: valid = bytes.Length >= 5; break;
				case 12:
					valid = TryPackedUInt(bytes, ref position, out _) && bytes.Length - position >= 4; break;
				case 15: valid = bytes.Length >= 2 && bytes[0] <= 1; break;
				case 18:
					valid = TryPackedUInt(bytes, ref position, out _) && bytes.Length - position >= 1; break;
				case 21: valid = bytes.Length >= 6; break; // two encoded ushort coordinates + sequence
				case 24: case 31: valid = bytes.Length >= 2; break;
				case 26: valid = bytes.Length >= 8; break;
				case 35:
					position = 1;
					valid = bytes.Length >= 1 && TryPackedUInt(bytes, ref position, out _) && bytes.Length - position >= 1; break;
				case 44: valid = bytes.Length >= 3 && bytes[2] <= 1; break;
				case 46:
					valid = TryPackedUInt(bytes, ref position, out _) && position < bytes.Length && bytes[position] <= 1; break;
				case 55:
					valid = TryPackedUInt(bytes, ref position, out _) && (position == bytes.Length || bytes[position] <= 1); break;
				case 23:
					// At minimum every vote entry consumes one byte. Keep the existing
					// generous cap until the exact result layout is verified in-game.
					valid = TryPackedUInt(bytes, ref position, out uint count) && count <= 1024 && count <= bytes.Length - position; break;
				default: valid = true; break;
			}
			reason = valid ? null : $"malformed RPC {rpc} required fields";
			return valid;
		}

		public static bool KnownGameDataTag(byte tag) => tag == 1 || tag == 2 ||
			tag == 4 || tag == 5 || tag == 6 || tag == 7 || tag == 8 || tag == 207;

		private static bool Fail(string message, out string reason)
		{
			reason = message;
			return false;
		}
	}
}

```

`source/anticheat/PlatformIntegrity.cs`:

```cs
using HarmonyLib;
using InnerNet;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
	// Detection only. No device identifiers are logged, persisted, or displayed.
	internal static class PlatformIntegrity
	{
		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(PlayerControl), nameof(PlayerControl.Start))]
		private static class OnPlayerStart
		{
			private static void Postfix(PlayerControl __instance)
			{
				if(!Anticheat.Enabled || !Anticheat.CheckSpoofedPlatforms) return;

				ClientData client = AmongUsClient.Instance.GetClientFromCharacter(__instance);
				if(client == null || IsConsistent(client.PlatformData)) return;

				Anticheat.Flag(__instance, $"{client.PlayerName} sent inconsistent platform information ({client.PlatformData.Platform}).", false);
			}
		}

		public static bool IsConsistent(PlatformSpecificData platform)
		{
			string name = platform.PlatformName;
			if(string.IsNullOrEmpty(name)) return false;
			bool hasXboxId = platform.XboxPlatformId != 0;
			bool hasPlayStationId = platform.PsnPlatformId != 0;

			switch(platform.Platform)
			{
				case Platforms.StandaloneEpicPC:
				case Platforms.StandaloneSteamPC:
				case Platforms.StandaloneMac:
				case Platforms.StandaloneItch:
				case Platforms.IPhone:
				case Platforms.Android:
				case (Platforms)112:
					return IsGenericName(name) && !hasXboxId && !hasPlayStationId;

				case Platforms.StandaloneWin10:
					return IsGenericName(name) && hasXboxId && !hasPlayStationId;

				case Platforms.Xbox:
					return !IsGenericName(name) && name.Length >= 3 && name.Length <= 16 && hasXboxId && !hasPlayStationId;

				case Platforms.Playstation:
					return !IsGenericName(name) && !hasXboxId && hasPlayStationId;

				case Platforms.Switch:
					return !IsGenericName(name) && !hasXboxId && !hasPlayStationId;

				case (Platforms)255:
					return AmongUsClient.Instance.NetworkMode == NetworkModes.LocalGame;

				default:
					return false;
			}
		}

		private static bool IsGenericName(string name)
		{
			return name == "TESTNAME";
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/PublicText.cs`:

```cs
using System.Text;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
	internal static class PublicText
	{
		public static string Clean(string text, int limit)
		{
			var result = new StringBuilder();
			foreach(char c in text ?? "")
			{
				if(result.Length >= limit) break;
				if(!char.IsControl(c) && !char.IsSurrogate(c) && c != '<' && c != '>' && c != '[' && c != ']') result.Append(c);
			}
			return result.ToString().Trim();
		}
		public static string Name(string text) { string name = Clean(text, 22); return name.Length == 0 ? "Unnamed player" : name; }
		public static string Simple(string title, string message)
		{
			if(message.Contains("several unexpected disconnects"))
				return "Several players disconnected together. This could be an attack or a connection problem. No attacker has been identified.";
			if(title.StartsWith("Anti-cheat"))
			{
				string lower = message.ToLowerInvariant();
				string category = lower.Contains("scan") ? "scan animation" :
					lower.Contains("vent") ? "vent action" :
					lower.Contains("movement") || lower.Contains("units/s") ? "movement" :
					lower.Contains("murder") || lower.Contains("kill") ? "kill action" :
					lower.Contains("meeting") || lower.Contains("vote") || lower.Contains("report") ? "meeting action" :
					lower.Contains("task") ? "task action" : lower.Contains("role") ? "role action" :
					lower.Contains("sabotage") || lower.Contains("system") ? "ship system action" : "game action";
				return $"Unusual {category}. Review the details; a named target is not proof of who sent it.";
			}
			if(title == "Protection" && message.StartsWith("Rejected packet before processing:"))
			{
				string action = message.Contains("vote-kick") ? "a vote-kick request" :
					message.Contains("Influencer") ? "an invalid or excessive Influencer message" :
					message.Contains("teleport") ? "an unexpected teleport" :
					message.Contains("vot") || message.Contains("meeting") ? "invalid meeting data" : "invalid game data";
				return $"Blocked {action} on this client. The sender is not verified, so nobody was automatically banned.";
			}
			return message;
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/RemovalRequestGuard.cs`:

```cs
using HarmonyLib;
using InnerNet;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
    // Guards requests made by THIS client, not server decisions or other clients'
    // outgoing traffic. Does not suppress OnPlayerLeft or fake a connected state.
    internal static class RemovalRequestGuard
    {
        public static bool Enabled = true;
        public static long Blocked { get; private set; }
        private static bool Deny(string reason, int id)
        {
            Blocked++;
            if(Blocked <= 20) AUAnticheatPlugin.Log.LogWarning($"Local removal request stopped: {reason}; affected lobby ID {id}. Caller is not authenticated attacker evidence.");
            return false;
        }
        [HarmonyPatch(typeof(InnerNetClient), nameof(InnerNetClient.KickPlayer))]
        private static class BeforeKickOrBan
        {
            private static bool Prefix(InnerNetClient __instance, int __0)
            {
                if(!Enabled || __instance == null) return true;
                if(!__instance.AmHost || __instance.ClientId != __instance.HostId) return Deny("client has no settled host authority",__0);
                if(__0 == __instance.ClientId || __0 == __instance.HostId) return Deny("self/host targeted",__0);
                return true; // Ordinary authorized moderation remains available.
            }
        }
        [HarmonyPatch(typeof(InnerNetClient), nameof(InnerNetClient.SendLateRejection))]
        private static class BeforeLateRejection
        {
            private static bool Prefix(InnerNetClient __instance, int __0)
            {
                if(!Enabled || __instance == null) return true;
                if(!__instance.AmHost || __instance.ClientId != __instance.HostId || __0 == __instance.ClientId || __0 == __instance.HostId) return Deny("unauthorized late rejection",__0);
                var target = __instance.FindClientById(__0);
                if(target != null && target.IsReady && !target.IsBeingCreated)
                    return Deny("late startup rejection aimed at an already-ready client",__0);
                return true; // Preserve legitimate startup timeout/failed-ready handling.
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`source/anticheat/RoundLedger.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
	internal sealed class RoundLedger
	{
		private sealed class Player { public string Name, Role; public bool Impostor; public int Kills; }
		private readonly Dictionary<uint, Player> players = new();
		private readonly HashSet<uint> killed = new();
		private double started, duration;
		private int reason;
		public bool Running { get; private set; }
		public bool Completed { get; private set; }
		public void Begin(double now) { players.Clear(); killed.Clear(); started = now; Running = true; Completed = false; }
		public void Observe(uint id, string name, string role, bool impostor)
		{
			if(!Running) return;
			if(!players.TryGetValue(id, out var player))
			{
				if(players.Count >= 64) return;
				players[id] = player = new Player();
			}
			player.Name = PublicText.Name(name);
			// Default crew records during transitions must not erase observed roles.
			if(!player.Impostor || impostor) player.Role = PublicText.Clean(role, 24);
			player.Impostor |= impostor;
		}
		public void Kill(uint killer, uint victim)
		{
			if(!Running || killer == victim || killed.Count >= 64 || !players.TryGetValue(killer, out var source) || !source.Impostor || !killed.Add(victim)) return;
			source.Kills++;
		}
		public void End(double now, int outcome)
		{
			if(!Running) return;
			duration = double.IsFinite(now - started) ? Math.Clamp(now - started, 0, 86400) : 0;
			reason = outcome; Running = false; Completed = true;
		}
		public static string Winner(int outcome) => outcome switch { 0 or 1 or 5 or 7 => "Crewmates", 2 or 3 or 4 or 6 or 8 => "Impostors", _ => "Unknown" };
        public string ChatSummary(int budget)
        {
            if(!Completed || budget <= 0) return null;
            string header = $"{Winner(reason)} won {(int)duration / 60}:{(int)duration % 60:00}";
            var impostors = new List<Player>();
            foreach(var player in players.Values) if(player.Impostor) impostors.Add(player);
            if(impostors.Count == 0)
            {
                return null; // Keep incomplete results local; never invent missing roles.
            }
            // Keep every impostor and kill count. Only abbreviate displayed names
            // when needed; ~ marks shortened names. Full details stay in Alerts.
            for(int nameLimit = 22; nameLimit >= 1; nameLimit--)
            {
                var parts = new List<string>();
                foreach(var player in impostors)
                {
                    string name = player.Name.Length <= nameLimit ? player.Name : player.Name.Substring(0,nameLimit) + "~";
                    string role = player.Role switch { "Impostor" => "I", "Shapeshifter" => "S", "Phantom" => "P", "Viper" => "V", _ => "?" };
                    parts.Add($"{name}({role}):{player.Kills}k");
                }
                string message = header + " | " + string.Join("; ",parts);
                if(message.Length <= budget) return message;
            }
            return null; // Cannot represent an unusual roster without dropping stats.
        }
		public List<string> Lines()
		{
			var lines = new List<string>();
			if(!Completed) return lines; // Never leak roles from an active game.
			lines.Add($"{Winner(reason)} won | {(int)duration / 60}m {(int)duration % 60}s");
			foreach(var player in players.Values)
				if(player.Impostor && lines.Count < 16) lines.Add($"{player.Name} ({player.Role}): {player.Kills} kill(s)");
			if(lines.Count == 1) lines.Add("No impostor role data was available to this client.");
			return lines;
		}
	}
}

```

`source/anticheat/RoundReports.cs`:

```cs
using HarmonyLib;
using AmongUs.GameOptions;
using UnityEngine;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
	internal static class RoundReports
	{
		public static bool Enabled = true;
		private static RoundLedger ledger = new();
		private static int gameId, hostId;
		private static bool queued;
		private static float waitingSince = -1;
		private static string readyLobby = "";
		internal const float ReturnDelay = 5f;
		private static string ReadyKey => LobbyChat.WaitingLobby ?
			$"{AmongUsClient.Instance.GameId}:{AmongUsClient.Instance.HostId}:{LobbyBehaviour.Instance.GetInstanceID()}:{PlayerControl.LocalPlayer.NetId}" : "";
		internal static bool ReadyToSend => Enabled && ledger.Completed && readyLobby.Length > 0 && readyLobby == ReadyKey &&
			waitingSince >= 0 && Time.realtimeSinceStartup - waitingSince >= ReturnDelay;
		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(AmongUsClient), nameof(AmongUsClient.OnDisconnected))]
		private static class ClearLobbyServices
		{
			private static void Postfix()
			{
				ledger = new(); queued = false; waitingSince = -1; readyLobby = "";
				LobbyChat.Clear(); LobbyRules.Disconnect(); ActivityTracker.Reset(); LobbyExtension.Reset(); LobbyLevelPolicy.Reset();
			}
		}
		public static void Begin()
		{
			if(AmongUsClient.Instance == null) return;
			if(ledger.Running && gameId == AmongUsClient.Instance.GameId && hostId == AmongUsClient.Instance.HostId) return;
			LobbyRules.NewRound();
			gameId = AmongUsClient.Instance.GameId; hostId = AmongUsClient.Instance.HostId;
			queued = false; waitingSince = -1; readyLobby = ""; LobbyChat.Clear();
			ledger.Begin(Time.realtimeSinceStartup); Snapshot();
		}
		private static void Observe(PlayerControl player)
		{
			if(player == null || player.Data == null) return;
			var data = player.Data;
			var role = data.RoleType;
			if(RoleManager.IsGhostRole(role) && data.RoleWhenAlive.HasValue) role = data.RoleWhenAlive.Value;
			if(RoleManager.IsGhostRole(role)) return;
			ledger.Observe(player.NetId, data.PlayerName, role.ToString(), RoleManager.IsImpostorRole(role));
		}
		private static void Snapshot() { foreach(var player in PlayerControl.AllPlayerControls) Observe(player); }
		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(RoleManager), nameof(RoleManager.SetRole))]
		private static class CaptureAssignedRole
		{
			private static void Postfix(PlayerControl __0, RoleTypes __1)
			{
				if(!ledger.Running || __0?.Data == null || RoleManager.IsGhostRole(__1)) return;
				ledger.Observe(__0.NetId, __0.Data.PlayerName, __1.ToString(), RoleManager.IsImpostorRole(__1));
			}
		}
		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(AmongUsClient), nameof(AmongUsClient.OnPlayerLeft))]
		private static class PreserveLeavingRole
		{
			private static void Prefix(InnerNet.ClientData __0) { Observe(__0?.Character); }
		}
		public static void Tick()
		{
			var client = AmongUsClient.Instance;
			if(client == null || client.GameId != gameId || client.HostId != hostId) { ledger = new(); waitingSince = -1; readyLobby = ""; return; }
			if(ledger.Running) { Snapshot(); return; }
			string current = Enabled && ledger.Completed ? ReadyKey : "";
			if(current.Length == 0) { waitingSince = -1; readyLobby = ""; return; }
			if(current != readyLobby) { readyLobby = current; waitingSince = Time.realtimeSinceStartup; }
			if(queued || !ReadyToSend) return;
			var lines = ledger.Lines();
            int bodyBudget = LobbyChat.MaximumPublicMessageLength - LobbyChat.CombinedMessage(new[]{"x"}).Length + 1;
            string summary = ledger.ChatSummary(bodyBudget);
            if(summary == null)
			{
				queued = true; // One local notice, never repeated attempts or a split public report.
                AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Send("Match results", "Results kept locally: impostor data was unavailable or the full roster could not fit one message. No roles were guessed. Kills are observed by this client.\n" + string.Join("\n", lines));
				return;
			}
            queued = LobbyChat.QueueReport(new[]{summary});
            if(queued) AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Send("Match results", "Full results (observed kills):\n" + string.Join("\n", lines));
		}
		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(PlayerControl), nameof(PlayerControl.MurderPlayer))]
		private static class OnKillResult
		{
			private static void Prefix(PlayerControl __0, MurderResultFlags __1, out bool __state)
			{
				__state = __0 != null && __0.Data != null && !__0.Data.IsDead && (__1 & MurderResultFlags.Succeeded) != 0;
			}
			private static void Postfix(PlayerControl __instance, PlayerControl __0, bool __state)
			{
				if(!__state || __instance == null || __0 == null) return;
				Observe(__instance); ledger.Kill(__instance.NetId, __0.NetId);
			}
		}
		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(AmongUsClient), nameof(AmongUsClient.OnGameEnd))]
		private static class OnEnd
		{
			private static void Prefix(EndGameResult __0)
			{
				if(__0 == null) return;
				Snapshot(); ledger.End(Time.realtimeSinceStartup, (int)__0.GameOverReason);
			}
		}
	}
}
```

```cs
using Hazel;
using System;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
	internal abstract class RpcCheck : ICheck
	{
		public virtual bool Enabled { get; set; } = true;

		public virtual bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			return true;
		}

		public abstract RpcCalls GetId();

		public virtual bool IsHostOnly()
		{
			return false;
		}

		public virtual Type GetExpectedNetObject()
		{
			// There are more RPCs for the PlayerControl net object than for any other net object
			// To make it easier for us, each instance of RpcCheck will be for the PlayerControl net object unless stated otherwise
			return typeof(PlayerControl);
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/SavedSettings.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.IO;
using System.Text.Json;
using UnityEngine;
using AUAnticheat.ui;
using AUAnticheat.features;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
 internal static class SavedSettings
 {
  private sealed class Setting { public Func<string> Read; public Action<string> Apply; public string Default; }
  private static readonly Dictionary<string,Setting> Items=new();
  private static string path, last;
  private static float next;
  public static string Status { get; private set; }="Settings not initialized.";
  private static string Text<T>(T value)=>Convert.ToString(value,CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
  private static void Bind<T>(string key,Func<T> read,Action<T> write,T fallback)
  {
   Items[key]=new Setting { Read=()=>Text(read()),Default=Text(fallback),Apply=text=>
   {
    object value=typeof(T).IsEnum?Enum.Parse(typeof(T),text):Convert.ChangeType(text,typeof(T),CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
    if(typeof(T).IsEnum&&!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(T),value)) return;
    if(value is float f&&(float.IsInfinity(f)||float.IsNaN(f))) return;
    write((T)value);
   }};
  }
  public static void Initialize(string directory)
  {
   Items.Clear(); path=Path.Combine(directory,"settings.json"); next=0;
   Bind("Detection.Enabled",()=>Anticheat.Enabled,v=>Anticheat.Enabled=v,true);
   Bind("Detection.Alerts",()=>Anticheat.SendNotification,v=>Anticheat.SendNotification=v,true);
   Bind("Detection.Block",()=>Anticheat.DiscardInvalidMessages,v=>Anticheat.DiscardInvalidMessages=v,true);
   Bind("Detection.Platforms",()=>Anticheat.CheckSpoofedPlatforms,v=>Anticheat.CheckSpoofedPlatforms=v,true);
   Bind("Detection.Behavior",()=>Anticheat.EnhancedMenuBehaviorChecks,v=>Anticheat.EnhancedMenuBehaviorChecks=v,true);
   Bind("Detection.Response",()=>Anticheat.Punishment,v=>Anticheat.Punishment=v,Anticheat.Punishments.Ban);
   Bind("Detection.Threshold",()=>Anticheat.EnforcementThreshold,v=>Anticheat.EnforcementThreshold=Math.Clamp(v,50,200),100);
   Bind("Detection.AlertSeconds",()=>Anticheat.NotificationDuration,v=>Anticheat.NotificationDuration=Math.Clamp(v,1,60),10f);
   foreach(var pair in Anticheat.RpcHandlers) { var item=pair.Value; Bind("RPC."+pair.Key,()=>item.Enabled,v=>item.Enabled=v,true); }
   foreach(var pair in Anticheat.GameDataHandlers) { var item=pair.Value; Bind("GameData."+pair.Key,()=>item.Enabled,v=>item.Enabled=v,true); }
   Bind("Guard.Removal",()=>Protections.ProtectAgainstNonHostKickExploit,v=>Protections.ProtectAgainstNonHostKickExploit=v,true);
   Bind("Guard.LocalRemovalRequests",()=>RemovalRequestGuard.Enabled,v=>RemovalRequestGuard.Enabled=v,true);
   Bind("Guard.VoteKick",()=>Protections.FilterVoteKickReplays,v=>Protections.FilterVoteKickReplays=v,true);
   Bind("Guard.Teleport",()=>Protections.BlockUnexpectedLocalTeleport.Enabled,v=>Protections.BlockUnexpectedLocalTeleport.Enabled=v,true);
   Bind("Guard.Meetings",()=>MeetingProtection.Enabled,v=>MeetingProtection.Enabled=v,true);
   Bind("Guard.Influencer",()=>InfluencerProtection.Enabled,v=>InfluencerProtection.Enabled=v,true);
   Bind("Guard.LobbyWalls",()=>LobbyMovementGuard.Enabled,v=>LobbyMovementGuard.Enabled=v,true);
   Bind("Guard.Encryption",()=>Protections.ForceDtls.Enabled,v=>Protections.ForceDtls.Enabled=v,true);
   Bind("Guard.Preflight",()=>Protections.PreflightPackets,v=>Protections.PreflightPackets=v,true);
   Bind("Guard.LargeMessages",()=>Protections.BlockLargeGameMessages,v=>Protections.BlockLargeGameMessages=v,true);
   Bind("Guard.MessageTypes",()=>Protections.BlockInvalidGameDataMessages,v=>Protections.BlockInvalidGameDataMessages=v,true);
   Bind("Guard.Numbers",()=>Protections.HardenedReadPackedUInt.Enabled,v=>Protections.HardenedReadPackedUInt.Enabled=v,true);
   Bind("Guard.VotingMemory",()=>Protections.MemoryAllocationOverload.Enabled,v=>Protections.MemoryAllocationOverload.Enabled=v,true);
   Bind("Lobby.ShareDetections",()=>LobbyChat.ShareAlerts,v=>LobbyChat.ShareAlerts=v,true);
   Bind("Moderation.PersistentBans",()=>ModerationStore.EnforceBans,v=>ModerationStore.EnforceBans=v,true);
   Bind("Chat.HideAds",()=>ChatModeration.Ads,v=>ChatModeration.Ads=v,true);
   Bind("Chat.HideSpam",()=>ChatModeration.Spam,v=>ChatModeration.Spam=v,true);
   Bind("Chat.WordFilter",()=>ChatModeration.WordFilter,v=>ChatModeration.WordFilter=v,false);
   Bind("Chat.NameFilter",()=>ChatModeration.NameFilter,v=>ChatModeration.NameFilter=v,false);
   Bind("Moderation.AfkAlerts",()=>ChatModeration.AfkAlerts,v=>ChatModeration.AfkAlerts=v,false);
   Bind("Moderation.AfkSeconds",()=>ChatModeration.AfkSeconds,v=>ChatModeration.AfkSeconds=Math.Clamp(v,60,600),120);
   Bind("Lobby.Reports",()=>RoundReports.Enabled,v=>RoundReports.Enabled=v,true);
   Bind("Lobby.Extensions",()=>LobbyExtension.Enabled,v=>LobbyExtension.Enabled=v,true);
   Bind("Lobby.LevelRule",()=>LobbyLevelPolicy.Enabled,v=>LobbyLevelPolicy.Enabled=v,false);
   Bind("Lobby.MinimumLevel",()=>LobbyLevelPolicy.MinimumLevel,v=>LobbyLevelPolicy.MinimumLevel=Math.Max(1u,v),10u);
   Bind("Lobby.Rules",()=>LobbyRules.Enabled,v=>LobbyRules.Enabled=v,false);
   Bind("Lobby.RulesDelay",()=>LobbyRules.DelaySeconds,v=>LobbyRules.DelaySeconds=v,10);
   Bind("UI.Scale",()=>MainUI.scale,v=>MainUI.scale=Math.Clamp(v,.75f,1.5f),1f);
   Bind("UI.Opacity",()=>Styles.menuOpacity,v=>Styles.menuOpacity=Math.Clamp(v,.35f,1f),.75f);
   Bind("UI.X",()=>MainUI.windowPosition.x,v=>MainUI.windowPosition=new Vector2(Math.Clamp(v,0,100000),MainUI.windowPosition.y),90f);
   Bind("UI.Y",()=>MainUI.windowPosition.y,v=>MainUI.windowPosition=new Vector2(MainUI.windowPosition.x,Math.Clamp(v,0,100000)),70f);
   Bind("Alerts.HidePopups",()=>AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.DisableNotifications,v=>AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.DisableNotifications=v,false);
   Bind("Alerts.Technical",()=>AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.ShowTechnicalDetails,v=>AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.ShowTechnicalDetails=v,false);
   try
   {
    Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
    if(File.Exists(path))
    {
     if(new FileInfo(path).Length>65536) throw new IOException("Settings file too large");
     var values=JsonSerializer.Deserialize<Dictionary<string,string>>(File.ReadAllText(path));
     if(values==null) throw new FormatException();
     foreach(var pair in values) if(Items.TryGetValue(pair.Key,out var setting))
      try { setting.Apply(pair.Value); } catch(Exception) { /* Bad entries leave that setting at its existing default. */ }
    }
    last=Snapshot(); Status="Settings save automatically on change.";
   }
   catch(Exception) { last=Snapshot(); Status="Could not load settings. Existing values kept; check settings.json."; }
  }
  private static string Snapshot()
  {
   var values=new Dictionary<string,string>(); foreach(var item in Items) values[item.Key]=item.Value.Read();
   return JsonSerializer.Serialize(values,new JsonSerializerOptions { WriteIndented=true });
  }
  public static void Tick()
  {
   if(path==null||Time.realtimeSinceStartup<next) return;
   next=Time.realtimeSinceStartup+1; Save();
  }
  public static void Save()
  {
   if(path==null) return;
   string data=Snapshot(); if(data==last&&File.Exists(path)) return;
   try { File.WriteAllText(path+".tmp",data); File.Move(path+".tmp",path,true); last=data; Status="Settings saved automatically."; }
   catch(Exception error) { Status=$"Settings could not be saved ({error.GetType().Name}). Check folder permissions."; }
  }
  public static void ResetDefaults()
  {
   foreach(var item in Items.Values) item.Apply(item.Default);
   FriendCodeExceptions.Clear();
   Styles.primaryColor=Styles.UIColors.Cardinal;
   Save();
  }
 }
}
```

`source/anticheat/ScannerReplayWindow.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
    internal sealed class ScannerReplayWindow
    {
        private sealed class Sample { public byte Sequence; public bool Scanning; public double At; }
        private readonly Dictionary<uint,Sample> samples=new();
        public void Reset() => samples.Clear();
        public bool Accept(uint objectId,bool scanning,byte sequence,double now)
        {
            if(!double.IsFinite(now)) return true;
            bool duplicate=samples.TryGetValue(objectId,out var old) && old.Scanning && scanning &&
                old.Sequence==sequence && now>=old.At && now-old.At<2;
            // Always allow scan-stop cleanup (including death/meeting transitions).
            if(duplicate) return false;
            if(samples.Count>=64 && !samples.ContainsKey(objectId)) samples.Clear();
            samples[objectId]=new Sample { Sequence=sequence,Scanning=scanning,At=now };
            return true;
        }
    }
}
```
