`source/anticheat/SceneBoundaryMonitor.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;
namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
    internal static class SceneBoundaryMonitor
    {
        private sealed class Observation { public uint NetId; public float Ready,OutsideSince,LastNotice=-100; public int Count; }
        private static readonly Dictionary<int,Observation> players=new();
        private static string session="";
        private static float nextGeometry;
        private static Bounds bounds;
        private static bool valid;
        private static bool Refresh()
        {
            var client=AmongUsClient.Instance;
            Component root=LobbyBehaviour.Instance != null ? LobbyBehaviour.Instance : ShipStatus.Instance;
            if(client==null || root==null) { session=""; players.Clear(); valid=false; return false; }
            string key=$"{client.GameId}:{client.HostId}:{root.GetInstanceID()}";
            if(key!=session) { session=key; players.Clear(); valid=false; nextGeometry=0; }
            if(Time.realtimeSinceStartup<nextGeometry) return valid;
            nextGeometry=Time.realtimeSinceStartup+1; valid=false;
            var colliders=root.GetComponentsInChildren<Collider2D>();
            if(colliders==null || colliders.Length>2048 || Constants.ShipOnlyMask==0) return false;
            int count=0;
            foreach(var collider in colliders)
            {
                if(collider==null || !collider.enabled || collider.isTrigger ||
                   (Constants.ShipOnlyMask & (1<<collider.gameObject.layer))==0 ||
                   (collider.attachedRigidbody!=null && collider.attachedRigidbody.bodyType!=RigidbodyType2D.Static)) continue;
                if(count++==0) bounds=collider.bounds; else bounds.Encapsulate(collider.bounds);
            }
            valid=SceneBoundaryRules.Usable(bounds.min.x,bounds.min.y,bounds.max.x,bounds.max.y,count);
            return valid;
        }
        public static bool OutsideLobby(Vector2 point)
        {
            try { return LobbyBehaviour.Instance != null && Refresh() && Outside(point); }
            catch(Exception) { valid=false; return false; } // Missing geometry must not convict.
        }
        private static bool Outside(Vector2 point) => SceneBoundaryRules.Outside(point.x,point.y,bounds.min.x,bounds.min.y,bounds.max.x,bounds.max.y);
        public static void Tick()
        {
            bool lobby=LobbyBehaviour.Instance!=null;
            if(!(lobby?LobbyMovementGuard.Enabled:Anticheat.Enabled) || !Refresh()) return;
            float now=Time.realtimeSinceStartup;
            var present=new HashSet<int>();
            foreach(var player in PlayerControl.AllPlayerControls)
            {
                if(player?.Data==null || player.Data.Disconnected) continue;
                present.Add(player.OwnerId);
                if(!players.TryGetValue(player.OwnerId,out var sample) || sample.NetId!=player.NetId)
                {
                    if(players.Count>=64) continue;
                    players[player.OwnerId]=sample=new Observation { NetId=player.NetId,Ready=now+5 };
                }
                bool stable=lobby ? !player.Data.IsDead && player.MyPhysics!=null && !player.MyPhysics.DoingCustomAnimation : MovementContext.StableLivingPlayer(player);
                if(!stable || now<sample.Ready || !Outside(player.GetTruePosition())) { sample.Count=0; continue; }
                if(sample.Count++==0) sample.OutsideSince=now;
                if(!SceneBoundaryRules.Sustained(sample.Count,now-sample.OutsideSince) || now-sample.LastNotice<15) continue;
                sample.LastNotice=now;
                using var evidence=SenderEvidence.Begin("Scene boundary observation",null,player.NetId,player.OwnerId,PublicText.Name(player.Data.PlayerName));
                SenderEvidence.Record();
                var position=player.GetTruePosition();
                AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Send("Protection",$"Position ({position.x:F1}, {position.y:F1}) stayed beyond the loaded scene's outer bounds. Possible invalid movement or desync; sender unknown, no ban.");
            }
            foreach(int id in new List<int>(players.Keys)) if(!present.Contains(id)) players.Remove(id);
        }
    }
}
```

`source/anticheat/SceneBoundaryRules.cs`:

```cs
using System;
namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
    internal static class SceneBoundaryRules
    {
        public static bool Usable(float minX,float minY,float maxX,float maxY,int count) =>
            count>=2 && float.IsFinite(minX) && float.IsFinite(minY) && float.IsFinite(maxX) && float.IsFinite(maxY) &&
            maxX-minX>=3 && maxY-minY>=2 && maxX-minX<1000 && maxY-minY<1000;
        // Intentionally generous. This is an outer envelope, not a navigation mesh.
        public static bool Outside(float x,float y,float minX,float minY,float maxX,float maxY) =>
            float.IsFinite(x) && float.IsFinite(y) && (x<minX-2 || x>maxX+2 || y<minY-2 || y>maxY+2);
        public static bool Sustained(int samples,float seconds) => samples>=4 && float.IsFinite(seconds) && seconds>=2;
    }
}

```

`source/anticheat/SenderEvidence.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Text;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
	// Neither an object owner nor a payload claim authenticates the sender.
	// Repetition cannot upgrade these observations into transport evidence.
	internal static class SenderEvidence
	{
		internal sealed class Context : IDisposable
		{
			internal readonly string Channel;
			internal readonly byte? Rpc;
			internal readonly uint? ObjectId;
			internal readonly int? ObjectOwner;
			internal readonly string Procedure;
			internal bool SequenceRecorded;
			internal int? ClaimedActor;
			internal int ProposedPoints;
			internal bool ConflictingClaims;
			internal string Affected;
			private readonly Context previous;
			internal readonly int generation;
			private bool disposed;
			internal Context(string channel, byte? rpc, uint? objectId, int? owner, string affected, string procedure)
			{
				Procedure = procedure;
				Channel = channel; Rpc = rpc; ObjectId = objectId; ObjectOwner = owner; Affected = affected;
				previous = Current; generation = sessionGeneration; Current = this;
			}
			public void Dispose()
			{
				if(disposed) return;
				disposed = true;
				if(generation == sessionGeneration && ReferenceEquals(Current, this)) Current = previous;
			}
		}
		internal sealed class Entry
		{
			internal DateTime TimeUtc;
			internal string Channel;
			internal byte? Rpc;
			internal uint? ObjectId;
			internal int? ObjectOwner, ClaimedActor;
			internal int ProposedPoints;
			internal bool ConflictingClaims;
			internal int Repeats = 1;
			internal string Affected;
		}
		[ThreadStatic] internal static Context Current;
		private static volatile int sessionGeneration;
		private static readonly object Sync = new();
		private static readonly Queue<Entry> Entries = new();
		private static Entry latest;
		private static readonly SequenceEvidence Sequences = new();
		public static int Count { get { lock(Sync) return Entries.Count; } }
		public static Context Begin(string channel, byte? rpc, uint? objectId, int? owner, string affected = null, string procedure = null)
			=> new Context(channel, rpc, objectId, owner, affected, procedure);
		public static void PauseSequences(int? owner, double seconds) { lock(Sync) Sequences.Pause(owner, seconds); }
		public static void ForgetSequences(int owner) { lock(Sync) Sequences.Forget(owner); }
		public static string SequenceSummary(Func<int, string> name = null) { lock(Sync) return Sequences.Summary(name); }
		public static void NoteTarget(string affected) { if(Current != null) Current.Affected = affected; }
		public static string TargetSummary => "Affected: " + (Current?.Affected ?? "unknown / not identifiable from this message") + ".";
		public static void NoteCandidate(int? claimedActor, int points)
		{
			if(Current == null) return;
			Current.ClaimedActor = claimedActor;
			Current.ProposedPoints = Math.Clamp(points, 0, 100);
			if(Current.ObjectOwner.HasValue && claimedActor.HasValue && Current.ObjectOwner != claimedActor)
				Current.ConflictingClaims = true;
		}
		public static void NoteObjectClaim(uint claimed, uint expected)
		{
			if(Current != null && claimed != expected) Current.ConflictingClaims = true;
		}
		public static void Record()
		{
			lock(Sync) RecordCore();
		}
		private static void RecordCore()
		{
			var context = Current != null && Current.generation == sessionGeneration ? Current : null;
			if(context != null && !context.SequenceRecorded)
			{
				context.SequenceRecorded = true;
				Sequences.Observe(context.ObjectOwner, context.ObjectId, context.ClaimedActor, context.ConflictingClaims, context.Procedure);
			}
			DateTime now = DateTime.UtcNow;
			string channel = context?.Channel ?? "Packet/unknown";
			if(latest != null && now >= latest.TimeUtc && (now - latest.TimeUtc).TotalSeconds < 2 &&
				latest.Channel == channel && latest.Rpc == context?.Rpc && latest.ObjectId == context?.ObjectId &&
				latest.ObjectOwner == context?.ObjectOwner && latest.ClaimedActor == context?.ClaimedActor &&
				latest.Affected == context?.Affected && latest.ConflictingClaims == (context?.ConflictingClaims ?? false) && latest.ProposedPoints == (context?.ProposedPoints ?? 0))
			{
				latest.Repeats = Math.Min(1000000, latest.Repeats + 1);
				return;
			}
			latest = new Entry { TimeUtc = now, Channel = channel, Rpc = context?.Rpc, ObjectId = context?.ObjectId,
				ObjectOwner = context?.ObjectOwner, ClaimedActor = context?.ClaimedActor,
				ConflictingClaims = context?.ConflictingClaims ?? false, ProposedPoints = context?.ProposedPoints ?? 0, Affected = context?.Affected };
			while(Entries.Count >= 100) Entries.Dequeue();
			Entries.Enqueue(latest);
		}
		public static void Reset()
		{
			lock(Sync) { sessionGeneration++; Current = null; Entries.Clear(); latest = null; Sequences.Clear(); }
		}
		public static string Summary(int maximum = 10)
		{
			lock(Sync) return SummaryCore(maximum);
		}
		private static string SummaryCore(int maximum)
		{
			var output = new StringBuilder();
			var rows = Entries.ToArray();
			for(int i = Math.Max(0, rows.Length - Math.Clamp(maximum, 0, 100)); i < rows.Length; i++)
			{
				var row = rows[i];
				output.AppendLine($"{row.TimeUtc:HH:mm:ss} UTC | {row.Channel} RPC {row.Rpc?.ToString() ?? "?"} | object {row.ObjectId?.ToString() ?? "?"}");
				output.AppendLine($"Sender UNKNOWN; object owner {row.ObjectOwner?.ToString() ?? "?"}; claimed actor {row.ClaimedActor?.ToString() ?? "?"}. These are not verified senders.");
				output.AppendLine("Affected: " + (row.Affected ?? "unknown / not identifiable from this message") + ".");
				output.AppendLine($"{(row.ConflictingClaims ? "Conflicting claims; " : "")}{row.Repeats} observation(s); {row.ProposedPoints} candidate points, NOT applied.");
			}
			return output.ToString();
		}
	}
}

```

`source/anticheat/SequenceEvidence.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
    // An explainable, bounded correlation tool, NOT sender authentication.
    // No dependency on punishment, networking, persistent IDs, or cheat scores.
    internal sealed class SequenceEvidence
    {
        private sealed class Observation
        {
            internal double At;
            internal int Owner;
            internal uint Object;
            internal string Family, Action;
        }

        private readonly Func<double> clock;
        private readonly List<Observation> observations = new();
        private readonly Dictionary<int, double> grace = new();
        private double globalGrace, lastTime = double.NegativeInfinity;
        internal const int Capacity = 256;
        internal const double Window = 45;
        internal SequenceEvidence(Func<double> clock = null)
        {
            this.clock = clock ?? (() => (double)Stopwatch.GetTimestamp() / Stopwatch.Frequency);
        }

        private double Now()
        {
            double now = clock();
            if(!double.IsFinite(now)) return double.NaN;
            if(now < lastTime) Clear(); // Never merge evidence across a clock discontinuity.
            lastTime = now;
            observations.RemoveAll(e => now - e.At > Window);
            foreach(int id in grace.Where(p => p.Value <= now).Select(p => p.Key).ToArray()) grace.Remove(id);
            return now;
        }

        internal int Count { get { Now(); return observations.Count; } }
        internal void Clear()
        {
            observations.Clear(); grace.Clear(); globalGrace = 0; lastTime = double.NegativeInfinity;
        }

        internal void Forget(int owner)
        {
            observations.RemoveAll(e => e.Owner == owner);
            grace.Remove(owner);
        }

        internal void Pause(int? owner, double seconds)
        {
            double now = Now();
            if(!double.IsFinite(now) || !double.IsFinite(seconds) || seconds <= 0) return;
            double until = now + Math.Min(seconds, 30);
            if(owner.HasValue)
            {
                observations.RemoveAll(e => e.Owner == owner.Value);
                // Bound state even if bogus owners are supplied. Ignore excess new owners.
                if(grace.ContainsKey(owner.Value) || grace.Count < 64)
                    grace[owner.Value] = Math.Max(grace.GetValueOrDefault(owner.Value), until);
            }
            else
            {
                observations.Clear(); globalGrace = Math.Max(globalGrace, until);
            }
        }

        internal void Observe(int? owner, uint? objectId, int? claim, bool conflict, string action)
        {
            double now = Now();
            if(!double.IsFinite(now) || now < globalGrace || !owner.HasValue || owner < 0 || !objectId.HasValue ||
                conflict || (claim.HasValue && claim != owner) || grace.ContainsKey(owner.Value)) return;
            string family = Family(action);
            if(family == null) return; // Never derive identities from shared targets, names, or unknown RPCs.
            // Different checks and duplicate packets from one short burst are not independent evidence.
            if(observations.Any(e => e.Owner == owner && e.Object == objectId && e.Family == family && now - e.At < 2)) return;
            if(observations.Count >= Capacity) observations.RemoveAt(0);
            observations.Add(new Observation { At = now, Owner = owner.Value, Object = objectId.Value, Family = family, Action = action });
        }

        private static string Family(string action) => action switch
        {
            "EnterVent" or "ExitVent" or "SnapTo" or "ClimbLadder" or "UsePlatform" => "movement",
            "CheckMurder" or "MurderPlayer" or "CheckProtect" or "ProtectPlayer" or "SetRole" or
                "Shapeshift" or "CheckShapeshift" or "CheckVanish" or "StartVanish" or "CheckAppear" or "StartAppear" => "role/combat",
            "ReportDeadBody" or "StartMeeting" or "CastVote" or "ClearVote" or "VotingComplete" => "meeting",
            "CompleteTask" or "PlayAnimation" or "SetScanner" => "task/animation",
            "SetName" or "CheckName" or "SetColor" or "CheckColor" or "SetLevel" or
                "SetHatStr" or "SetSkinStr" or "SetPetStr" or "SetVisorStr" or "SetNamePlateStr" => "appearance/identity",
            _ => null
        };

        internal string Summary(Func<int, string> name = null)
        {
            Now();
            var output = new StringBuilder();
            var groups = observations.GroupBy(e => (e.Owner, e.Object))
                .Where(g => g.Count() >= 3 && g.Select(e => e.Family).Distinct().Count() >= 2 && g.Last().At - g.First().At >= 4)
                .OrderByDescending(g => g.Last().At).Take(8);
            foreach(var group in groups)
            {
                string label = name?.Invoke(group.Key.Owner);
                output.AppendLine($"Review messages referencing {label ?? "player"} (lobby ID {group.Key.Owner}, object {group.Key.Object}).");
                output.AppendLine($"{group.Count()} spaced observations across {group.Select(e => e.Family).Distinct().Count()} action types in the last 45 seconds.");
                output.AppendLine("Recent sequence: " + string.Join(" -> ", group.TakeLast(4).Select(e => e.Action)) + ".");
                output.AppendLine("Sender UNKNOWN. This player may be the victim of forged messages. No points or automatic removal from this pattern.");
            }
            return output.Length == 0 ? "No sustained mixed-action patterns outside normal transition grace periods." : output.ToString();
        }
    }
}

```

`source/anticheat/VentilationRules.cs`:

```cs
using System;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
	internal static class VentilationRules
	{
		// AFTER system and player fields: ushort sequence, byte operation, byte vent ID.
		public static bool TryRead(ReadOnlySpan<byte> payload, out ushort sequence, out byte operation, out byte vent)
		{
			sequence = 0; operation = 0; vent = 0;
			if(payload.Length < 4) return false;
			sequence = (ushort)(payload[0] | (payload[1] << 8));
			operation = payload[2]; vent = payload[3];
			return operation <= 5;
		}
		public static bool IsMovement(byte operation) => operation >= 2 && operation <= 4;
		public static bool ShouldCheckEntryRole(bool authority, bool initialized, bool transition, bool dead)
			=> authority && initialized && !transition && !dead;
		public static bool RoleAllowsEntry(bool canVent, bool engineer) => canVent || engineer;
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/VoteKickGuard.cs`:

```cs
using System.Diagnostics;
using Hazel;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat
{
	internal static class VoteKickGuard
	{
		private static readonly VoteReplayWindow RecentVotes = new();
		public static long RejectedVotes { get; private set; }
		public static void Reset() { RecentVotes.Reset(); RejectedVotes = 0; }
		public static void Forget(int clientId) => RecentVotes.Forget(clientId);

		public static bool Validate(MessageReader reader, System.Func<int,string> resolveName = null)
		{
			if(reader == null) return Reject("missing vote-kick payload");
			int oldPosition = reader.Position;
			try
			{
				if(reader.BytesRemaining < 8) return Reject("truncated vote-kick pair");
				int source = reader.ReadInt32(), target = reader.ReadInt32();
				string targetName = resolveName?.Invoke(target);
				SenderEvidence.NoteTarget($"vote-kick target {targetName ?? "unknown player"} (lobby ID {target})");
				SenderEvidence.NoteCandidate(source, 0);
				var client = AmongUsClient.Instance;
				if(client == null) return true;
				if(source == target) return Reject("self-targeted vote-kick");
				if(client.FindClientById(source) == null || client.FindClientById(target) == null)
					return Reject("vote-kick references a client absent from this lobby");
				double now = (double)Stopwatch.GetTimestamp() / Stopwatch.Frequency;
				return RecentVotes.Accept(source, target, now) || Reject("duplicate vote-kick pair within two seconds");
			}
			catch(System.Exception) { return Reject("invalid vote-kick payload or unavailable client state"); }
			finally { reader.Position = oldPosition; }
		}

		private static bool Reject(string reason)
		{
			RejectedVotes++;
			return PacketPreflight.Block(reason);
		}

	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/gamedata/ClientReady.cs`:

```cs
using AmongUs.InnerNet.GameDataMessages;
using Hazel;
using InnerNet;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat.gamedata
{
	internal class ClientReady : GameDataCheck
	{
		public override bool Validate(MessageReader reader)
		{
			int clientId = reader.ReadPackedInt32();

			ClientData client = AmongUsClient.Instance.FindClientById(clientId);
			if(client == null)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag($"Received ClientReady message for unknown client: {clientId}.");
				return false;
			}

			// Ready messages are idempotent and may legitimately be repeated while a
			// client reconnects or retransmits during scene loading.
			return true;
		}

		public override GameDataTypes GetId()
		{
			return GameDataTypes.ReadyFlag;
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/gamedata/SceneChange.cs`:

```cs
using AmongUs.InnerNet.GameDataMessages;
using Hazel;
using InnerNet;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat.gamedata
{
	internal class SceneChange : GameDataCheck
	{
		public override bool Validate(MessageReader reader)
		{
			int clientId = reader.ReadPackedInt32();
			string scene = reader.ReadString();

			ClientData client = AmongUsClient.Instance.FindClientById(clientId);
			if(client == null)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag($"Received SceneChange message for unknown client: {clientId}.");
				return false;
			}

			// If the host receives a scene change of Tutorial, it will spawn in an instance of The Skeld map
			if(scene == "Tutorial" && AmongUsClient.Instance.NetworkMode != NetworkModes.LocalGame)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(client.Character, $"{client.Character.Data.PlayerName} sent a scene change of Tutorial.");
				return false;
			}

			return true;
		}

		public override GameDataTypes GetId()
		{
			return GameDataTypes.SceneChangeFlag;
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/rpc/AddVote.cs`:

```cs
using Hazel;
using InnerNet;
using System;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat.rpc
{
	internal class AddVote : RpcCheck
	{
		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			int source = reader.ReadInt32();
			int target = reader.ReadInt32();

			ClientData client = AmongUsClient.Instance.FindClientById(source);
			ClientData targetClient = AmongUsClient.Instance.FindClientById(target);
			if(client == null || client.Character == null || client.Character.Data == null)
			{
				AUAnticheatPlugin.Log.LogInfo($"An unknown client id ({source}) attempted to votekick {target}");
				return false;
			}

			if(targetClient == null)
			{
				AUAnticheatPlugin.Log.LogInfo($"{client.Character.Data.PlayerName} attempted to votekick an unknown client id ({target})");
				return false;
			}

			// VoteBanSystem is the normal lobby-wide vote-kick mechanism. It is valid
			// outside meetings and remains available to dead players, so neither state
			// is evidence of cheating.
			return true;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId()
		{
			return RpcCalls.AddVote;
		}

		public override Type GetExpectedNetObject()
		{
			return typeof(VoteBanSystem);
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/rpc/AdvancedRpcChecks.cs`:

```cs
using AmongUs.GameOptions;
using Hazel;
using InnerNet;
using System;
using UnityEngine;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat.rpc
{
	internal static class AdvancedRuleHelpers
	{
		public static string Name(PlayerControl player)
		{
			return player != null && player.Data != null ? player.Data.PlayerName : "Unknown player";
		}

		public static bool IsActiveRound()
		{
			return GameManager.Instance != null && GameManager.Instance.GameHasStarted && ShipStatus.Instance != null;
		}

		public static bool IsRole(PlayerControl player, string roleName)
		{
			return player != null && player.Data != null && player.Data.RoleType.ToString() == roleName;
		}

		public static void FlagAuthority(PlayerControl objectOwner, string reason)
		{
			Anticheat.Flag(Anticheat.GetAuthoritativeActor(objectOwner), reason);
		}

		public static bool IsWithinAbilityDistance(PlayerControl source, PlayerControl target, float configuredDistance, float tolerance = 1.25f)
		{
			if(source == null || target == null) return false;
			return Vector2.Distance(source.GetTruePosition(), target.GetTruePosition()) <= configuredDistance + tolerance;
		}

		public static void GrantAllMovementGrace(double seconds = 5)
		{
			foreach(PlayerControl candidate in PlayerControl.AllPlayerControls)
			{
				Anticheat.GrantMovementGrace(candidate, seconds);
			}
		}
	}

	internal class SyncSettings : RpcCheck
	{
		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			if(ShipStatus.Instance == null || Anticheat.IsRoundStartGrace()) return true;

			AdvancedRuleHelpers.FlagAuthority(player, $"The lobby host changed game settings after the round-start window.");
			return false;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId() => RpcCalls.SyncSettings;
		public override bool IsHostOnly() => true;
	}

	internal class MurderPlayer : RpcCheck
	{
		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			PlayerControl target = reader.ReadNetObject<PlayerControl>();
			MurderResultFlags result = (MurderResultFlags)reader.ReadInt32();
			if((result & MurderResultFlags.Succeeded) == 0) return true;

			// This is the host-authorized result. Observer role/death/meeting snapshots
			// may already have advanced by the time it arrives, so they cannot be used
			// to accuse the host or suppress an ordinary kill. The defending host checks
			// the original CheckMurder request below.
			if(player == null || player.Data == null || target == null || target.Data == null || target == player)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag("Blocked a structurally invalid host-authorized murder result.");
				return false;
			}

			return true;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId() => RpcCalls.MurderPlayer;
		public override bool IsHostOnly() => true;
	}

	internal class StartMeeting : RpcCheck
	{
		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			if(AdvancedRuleHelpers.IsActiveRound() && MeetingHud.Instance == null && !GameManager.Instance.IsHideAndSeek())
			{
				AdvancedRuleHelpers.GrantAllMovementGrace();
				return true;
			}

			PlayerControl actor = Anticheat.GetAuthoritativeActor(player);
			Anticheat.Flag(actor, $"{AdvancedRuleHelpers.Name(actor)} attempted to force a meeting in an invalid game state.");
			return false;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId() => RpcCalls.StartMeeting;
		public override bool IsHostOnly() => true;
	}

	internal abstract class MeetingAuthorityCheck : RpcCheck
	{
		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			if(MeetingHud.Instance != null)
			{
				AdvancedRuleHelpers.GrantAllMovementGrace();
				return true;
			}

			PlayerControl host = Anticheat.GetHostPlayer();
			Anticheat.Flag(host, $"{AdvancedRuleHelpers.Name(host)} sent {GetId()} without an active meeting.");
			return false;
		}

		public override bool IsHostOnly() => true;
		public override Type GetExpectedNetObject() => typeof(MeetingHud);
	}

	internal class CloseMeeting : MeetingAuthorityCheck
	{
		public override RpcCalls GetId() => RpcCalls.CloseMeeting;
	}

	internal class VotingComplete : MeetingAuthorityCheck
	{
		public override RpcCalls GetId() => RpcCalls.VotingComplete;
	}

	internal class ClearVote : MeetingAuthorityCheck
	{
		public override RpcCalls GetId() => RpcCalls.ClearVote;
	}

	internal class BootFromVent : RpcCheck
	{
		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			if(AdvancedRuleHelpers.IsActiveRound())
			{
				Anticheat.GrantMovementGrace(player);
				return true;
			}

			PlayerControl actor = Anticheat.GetAuthoritativeActor(player);
			Anticheat.Flag(actor, $"{AdvancedRuleHelpers.Name(actor)} attempted to boot a player from a vent outside active gameplay.");
			return false;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId() => RpcCalls.BootFromVent;
		public override bool IsHostOnly() => true;
		public override Type GetExpectedNetObject() => typeof(PlayerPhysics);
	}

	internal class SetRole : RpcCheck
	{
		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			RoleTypes requestedRole = (RoleTypes)reader.ReadUInt16();
			reader.ReadBoolean();

			if(Utilities.IsAnticheatPresent() && RoleManager.IsGhostRole(requestedRole))
			{
				Anticheat.GrantGhostRoleGrace(player);
				Anticheat.GrantMovementGrace(player, 4);
				return true;
			}

			if(LobbyBehaviour.Instance == null && Anticheat.IsRoundStartGrace()) return true;
			if(LobbyBehaviour.Instance == null && player != null && !player.roleAssigned) return true;
			if(player != null && player.Data != null && player.Data.RoleType == requestedRole) return true;
			if(IsLegitimateGhostTransition(player, requestedRole)) return true;

			PlayerControl actor = Anticheat.GetAuthoritativeActor(player);
			string location = LobbyBehaviour.Instance != null ? "in the lobby" : "mid-game";
			Anticheat.Flag(actor, $"{AdvancedRuleHelpers.Name(actor)} changed {AdvancedRuleHelpers.Name(player)}'s role to {requestedRole} {location}.");
			return false;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId() => RpcCalls.SetRole;
		public override bool IsHostOnly() => true;

		private static bool IsLegitimateGhostTransition(PlayerControl player, RoleTypes requestedRole)
		{
			if(player == null || player.Data == null || !RoleManager.IsGhostRole(requestedRole)) return false;
			if(!player.Data.IsDead && !Anticheat.HasGhostRoleGrace(player)) return false;

			RoleTypes livingRole = player.Data.RoleWhenAlive.HasValue ? player.Data.RoleWhenAlive.Value : player.Data.RoleType;
			bool wasImpostor = RoleManager.IsImpostorRole(livingRole);
			return RoleManager.IsImpostorRole(requestedRole) == wasImpostor;
		}
	}

	internal class ProtectPlayer : RpcCheck
	{
		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			PlayerControl target = reader.ReadNetObject<PlayerControl>();
			PlayerControl actor = Anticheat.GetAuthoritativeActor(player);
			if(AdvancedRuleHelpers.IsActiveRound() && AdvancedRuleHelpers.IsRole(player, "GuardianAngel") && target != null && target.Data != null) return true;

			Anticheat.Flag(actor, $"{AdvancedRuleHelpers.Name(actor)} issued an invalid Guardian Angel protection.");
			return false;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId() => RpcCalls.ProtectPlayer;
		public override bool IsHostOnly() => true;
	}

	internal class Shapeshift : RpcCheck
	{
		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			PlayerControl target = reader.ReadNetObject<PlayerControl>();
			reader.ReadBoolean();
			PlayerControl actor = Anticheat.GetAuthoritativeActor(player);
			// Official servers have already validated this result. The local role snapshot
			// can arrive a few frames before or after the shapeshift RPC, especially for a
			// lagging client, so do not reinterpret a temporary duplicate appearance as a
			// cheat. The request-side CheckShapeshift validation remains active for hosts
			// that are the actual network authority.
			if(Utilities.IsAnticheatPresent() && AdvancedRuleHelpers.IsActiveRound() && player != null && target != null)
			{
				Anticheat.GrantMovementGrace(player, 3);
				return true;
			}

			// Host-authored revert/cleanup results may arrive after the death state.
			if(AdvancedRuleHelpers.IsActiveRound() && AdvancedRuleHelpers.IsRole(player, "Shapeshifter") && target != null)
			{
				Anticheat.GrantMovementGrace(player, 3);
				return true;
			}

			Anticheat.Flag(actor, $"{AdvancedRuleHelpers.Name(actor)} issued an invalid shapeshift for {AdvancedRuleHelpers.Name(player)}.");
			return false;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId() => RpcCalls.Shapeshift;
		public override bool IsHostOnly() => true;
	}

	internal class CheckMurder : RpcCheck
	{
		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			PlayerControl target = reader.ReadNetObject<PlayerControl>();
			if(Utilities.IsAnticheatPresent()) return !Anticheat.ObserveDistinctTargetSpray(player, target, GetId());
			if(AdvancedRuleHelpers.IsActiveRound() && MeetingHud.Instance == null && player != null && player.Data != null && !player.Data.IsDead &&
				player.Data.Role != null && player.Data.Role.CanUseKillButton && target != null && target != player && target.Data != null && !target.Data.IsDead)
			{
				return !Anticheat.ObserveDistinctTargetSpray(player, target, GetId());
			}

			Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{AdvancedRuleHelpers.Name(player)} requested an impossible murder.");
			return false;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId() => RpcCalls.CheckMurder;
	}

	internal class CheckProtect : RpcCheck
	{
		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			PlayerControl target = reader.ReadNetObject<PlayerControl>();
			if(Utilities.IsAnticheatPresent()) return !Anticheat.ObserveDistinctTargetSpray(player, target, GetId());
			if(AdvancedRuleHelpers.IsActiveRound() && AdvancedRuleHelpers.IsRole(player, "GuardianAngel") && target != null && target.Data != null && !target.Data.IsDead &&
				AdvancedRuleHelpers.IsWithinAbilityDistance(player, target, player.Data.Role.GetAbilityDistance(), 1.5f))
			{
				return !Anticheat.ObserveDistinctTargetSpray(player, target, GetId());
			}

			Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{AdvancedRuleHelpers.Name(player)} requested an impossible Guardian Angel protection.");
			return false;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId() => RpcCalls.CheckProtect;
	}

	internal class CheckShapeshift : RpcCheck
	{
		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			PlayerControl target = reader.ReadNetObject<PlayerControl>();
			if(reader.BytesRemaining > 0) reader.ReadBoolean();
			if(Utilities.IsAnticheatPresent()) return !Anticheat.ObserveDistinctTargetSpray(player, target, GetId());
			if(AdvancedRuleHelpers.IsActiveRound() && AdvancedRuleHelpers.IsRole(player, "Shapeshifter") && !player.Data.IsDead && target != null)
			{
				return !Anticheat.ObserveDistinctTargetSpray(player, target, GetId());
			}

			Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{AdvancedRuleHelpers.Name(player)} requested an impossible shapeshift.");
			return false;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId() => RpcCalls.CheckShapeshift;
	}

	internal class TriggerSpores : RpcCheck
	{
		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			if(Utilities.IsAnticheatPresent()) return true;
			if(AdvancedRuleHelpers.IsActiveRound() && Utilities.GetCurrentMap() == MapNames.Fungle && player != null && player.Data != null && !player.Data.IsDead) return true;

			Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{AdvancedRuleHelpers.Name(player)} triggered spores in an invalid state.");
			return false;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId() => RpcCalls.TriggerSpores;
	}

	internal abstract class PhantomCheck : RpcCheck
	{
		private readonly bool hostOnly;

		protected PhantomCheck(bool hostOnly)
		{
			this.hostOnly = hostOnly;
		}

		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			if(Utilities.IsAnticheatPresent()) return true;
			bool validLiveRequest = !hostOnly && AdvancedRuleHelpers.IsRole(player, "Phantom") && player != null && player.Data != null && !player.Data.IsDead;
			bool validHostResult = hostOnly && (AdvancedRuleHelpers.IsRole(player, "Phantom") || Anticheat.HasGhostRoleGrace(player));
			if(AdvancedRuleHelpers.IsActiveRound() && (validLiveRequest || validHostResult)) return true;

			PlayerControl actor = hostOnly ? Anticheat.GetAuthoritativeActor(player) : player;
			Anticheat.Flag(actor, $"{AdvancedRuleHelpers.Name(actor)} used {GetId()} for a player who is not an active Phantom.");
			return false;
		}

		public override bool IsHostOnly() => hostOnly;
	}

	internal class CheckVanish : PhantomCheck
	{
		public CheckVanish() : base(false) { }
		public override RpcCalls GetId() => RpcCalls.CheckVanish;
	}

	internal class StartVanish : PhantomCheck
	{
		public StartVanish() : base(true) { }
		public override RpcCalls GetId() => RpcCalls.StartVanish;
	}

	internal class CheckAppear : PhantomCheck
	{
		public CheckAppear() : base(false) { }
		public override RpcCalls GetId() => RpcCalls.CheckAppear;
	}

	internal class StartAppear : PhantomCheck
	{
		public StartAppear() : base(true) { }
		public override RpcCalls GetId() => RpcCalls.StartAppear;
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/rpc/CheckName.cs`:

```cs
using Hazel;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat.rpc
{
	internal class CheckName : RpcCheck
	{
		public readonly int MAX_NAME_LENGTH = 10;

		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			string requestedName = reader.ReadString();

			/*
			ClientData clientData = AmongUsClient.Instance.GetClient(player.OwnerId);
			if(AmongUsClient.Instance.NetworkMode != NetworkModes.LocalGame && requestedName != clientData.PlayerName)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{clientData.PlayerName} requested a name that does not match their name in the login handshake.");
				player.SetName(clientData.PlayerName);
				return false;
			}
			*/

			if(requestedName.Length > MAX_NAME_LENGTH)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag($"Rejected an overlong claimed name ({requestedName.Length} characters). Affected object {player.NetId}; sender unverified, no player penalized.");
				return false;
			}

			if(requestedName.Contains('<'))
			{
				Anticheat.Flag($"Rejected a claimed name containing markup. Affected object {player.NetId}; sender unverified, no player penalized.");
				return false;
			}

			return true;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId()
		{
			return RpcCalls.CheckName;
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/rpc/ClimbLadder.cs`:

```cs
using Hazel;
using System;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat.rpc
{
	internal class ClimbLadder : RpcCheck
	{
		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			byte ladderId = reader.ReadByte();
			reader.ReadByte(); // animation sequence, including wrap-around
			if(ShipStatus.Instance != null && ShipStatus.Instance.Ladders != null && !Anticheat.IsRoundStartGrace())
			{
				bool found = false;
				foreach(Ladder ladder in ShipStatus.Instance.Ladders)
				{
					if(ladder != null && ladder.Id == ladderId) { found = true; break; }
				}
				if(!found)
				{
					Anticheat.Flag("Blocked a ladder action for an identifier absent from the loaded map. Sender unverified.");
					return false;
				}
			}
			if(Utilities.IsAnticheatPresent())
			{
				Anticheat.GrantMovementGrace(player, 4);
				return true;
			}
			if(ShipStatus.Instance == null)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} tried to climb a ladder when there is no instance of ShipStatus.");
				return false;
			}

			MapNames map = Utilities.GetCurrentMap();
			if(map != MapNames.Airship && map != MapNames.Fungle)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} tried to climb a ladder outside of the proper map.");
				return false;
			}

			// A ladder animation can cross a death-state update on another client. It is
			// harmless once dead, so packet ordering is not treated as evidence.
			if(player.Data.IsDead)
			{
				Anticheat.GrantMovementGrace(player, 2);
				return true;
			}

			Anticheat.GrantMovementGrace(player, 4);
			return true;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId()
		{
			return RpcCalls.ClimbLadder;
		}

		public override Type GetExpectedNetObject()
		{
			return typeof(PlayerPhysics);
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/rpc/CloseDoorsOfType.cs`:

```cs
			return true;
```

`source/anticheat/rpc/CompleteTask.cs`:

```cs
using Hazel;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat.rpc
{
	internal class CompleteTask : RpcCheck
	{
		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			uint taskIndex = reader.ReadPackedUInt32();
			if(player == null || player.Data == null)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag("Blocked CompleteTask without a valid player object.");
				return false;
			}

			// Arrival bursts are observational: lag can batch real completions.
			if(Anticheat.ObserveTaskCompletionSweep(player, taskIndex)) return false;

			// The official service is authoritative and may deliver the completion before
			// this client's role/task snapshot catches up.
			if(Utilities.IsAnticheatPresent()) return true;

			// If there is no instance of ShipStatus (such as if the game has not started yet or the map was despawned), then it is not possible to complete tasks (
			// Technically we don't need this to detect if someone completes a task in the lobby, as the task ID being greater than the total amount of tasks check should detect it
			if(ShipStatus.Instance == null)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} tried completing task {taskIndex} when there was no valid instance of ShipStatus.");
				return false;
			}

			if(RoleManager.IsImpostorRole(player.Data.RoleType))
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} tried completing task {taskIndex} while being an imposter.");
				return false;
			}

			// Task IDs are zero-indexed
			if(player.Data.Tasks == null) return true;
			if(taskIndex >= (uint)player.Data.Tasks.Count)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} tried completing task {taskIndex} when they only have {player.Data.Tasks.Count} tasks.");
				return false;
			}

			if(player.Data.Tasks[(int)taskIndex].Complete)
			{
				// Completion is idempotent, so a duplicate delivery cannot grant credit.
				AUAnticheatPlugin.Log.LogInfo($"Ignored duplicate task completion {taskIndex} from {player.Data.PlayerName}.");
				return false;
			}

			return true;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId()
		{
			return RpcCalls.CompleteTask;
		}
	}
}

```

`source/anticheat/rpc/EnterVent.cs`:

```cs
using Hazel;
using System;
using UnityEngine;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat.rpc
{
	internal class EnterVent : RpcCheck
	{
		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			int ventId = reader.ReadPackedInt32();
			if(!VentRuleHelpers.ValidateLoadedVent(ventId)) return false;
			bool initialized = ShipStatus.Instance != null && player != null && player.Data != null && player.Data.Role != null;
			if(!initialized) return true;
			bool authority = AmongUsClient.Instance != null && AmongUsClient.Instance.AmHost && !Utilities.IsAnticheatPresent();
			bool transition = Anticheat.IsRoundStartGrace() || Anticheat.HasMeetingTransitionGrace() ||
				Anticheat.HasGhostRoleGrace(player) || Anticheat.HasMovementGrace(player) ||
				(player.MyPhysics != null && player.MyPhysics.DoingCustomAnimation);
			if(!VentilationRules.ShouldCheckEntryRole(authority, initialized, transition, player.Data.IsDead))
			{
				Anticheat.GrantMovementGrace(player);
				return true;
			}
			if(!VentilationRules.RoleAllowsEntry(player.Data.Role.CanVent, player.Data.RoleType == AmongUs.GameOptions.RoleTypes.Engineer))
			{
				return PacketPreflight.Block("vent entry inconsistent with current role; affected object is not sender proof");
			}
			// Arrival position is not request position; lagged distance is not proof.
			if(GameManager.Instance != null && GameManager.Instance.IsHideAndSeek() && RoleManager.IsImpostorRole(player.Data.RoleType))
			{
				return PacketPreflight.Block("seeker vent entry disallowed in current game mode");
			}

			Anticheat.GrantMovementGrace(player);
			return true;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId()
		{
			return RpcCalls.EnterVent;
		}

		public override Type GetExpectedNetObject()
		{
			return typeof(PlayerPhysics);
		}
	}

	internal static class VentRuleHelpers
	{
		public const float MaximumEntryDistance = 5.0f;

		public static bool ValidateLoadedVent(int ventId)
		{
			if(ShipStatus.Instance == null || ShipStatus.Instance.AllVents == null || ShipStatus.Instance.AllVents.Length == 0 || Anticheat.IsRoundStartGrace()) return true;
			if(FindVent(ventId) != null) return true;
			return PacketPreflight.Block("vent identifier absent from loaded map");
		}

		public static Vent FindVent(int ventId)
		{
			if(ShipStatus.Instance == null || ShipStatus.Instance.AllVents == null) return null;
			foreach(Vent vent in ShipStatus.Instance.AllVents)
			{
				if(vent != null && vent.Id == ventId) return vent;
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/rpc/ExitVent.cs`:

```cs
using Hazel;
using System;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat.rpc
{
	internal class ExitVent : RpcCheck
	{
		// Sending ExitVent RPCs can be used to make the player teleport to areas without having to send SnapTo RPCs
		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			int ventId = reader.ReadPackedInt32();
			if(!VentRuleHelpers.ValidateLoadedVent(ventId)) return false;
			// Engineer timeout, ejection, death and role changes can precede exit
			// locally. !inVent/current CanVent cannot prove a forged cleanup packet.
			Anticheat.GrantMovementGrace(player);
			return true;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId()
		{
			return RpcCalls.ExitVent;
		}

		public override Type GetExpectedNetObject()
		{
			return typeof(PlayerPhysics);
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/rpc/MobileMenuRpcChecks.cs`:

```cs
using Hazel;
using System;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat.rpc
{
	internal sealed class CheckColor : RpcCheck
	{
		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			byte color = reader.ReadByte();
			if(color >= Palette.ColorNames.Length)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{SafeName(player)} requested invalid color {color}.");
				return false;
			}

			if(GameManager.Instance != null && GameManager.Instance.GameHasStarted && LobbyBehaviour.Instance == null)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{SafeName(player)} attempted to change color after the match started.");
				return false;
			}

			if(Anticheat.ObserveRapidColorCycling(player, color)) return false;

			return true;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId() => RpcCalls.CheckColor;

		private static string SafeName(PlayerControl player)
		{
			return player != null && player.Data != null ? player.Data.PlayerName : "A player";
		}
	}

	internal sealed class CosmeticChange : RpcCheck
	{
		private readonly RpcCalls id;
		private readonly string cosmeticName;

		public CosmeticChange(RpcCalls id, string cosmeticName)
		{
			this.id = id;
			this.cosmeticName = cosmeticName;
		}

		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			string cosmeticId = reader.ReadString();
			if(cosmeticId == null || cosmeticId.Length > 128 || ContainsControlCharacters(cosmeticId))
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{SafeName(player)} sent a malformed {cosmeticName} identifier.");
				return false;
			}

			if(GameManager.Instance != null && GameManager.Instance.GameHasStarted && LobbyBehaviour.Instance == null)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{SafeName(player)} attempted a mid-game {cosmeticName} change.");
				return false;
			}

			return true;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId() => id;

		private static bool ContainsControlCharacters(string value)
		{
			foreach(char character in value)
			{
				if(char.IsControl(character)) return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private static string SafeName(PlayerControl player)
		{
			return player != null && player.Data != null ? player.Data.PlayerName : "A player";
		}
	}

	internal sealed class SendChat : RpcCheck
	{
		private const int MaximumChatLength = 400;

		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			string message = reader.ReadString();
			if(message == null || message.Length > MaximumChatLength)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"Blocked an oversized chat message attributed to {SafeName(player)}.", false);
				return false;
			}

			foreach(char character in message)
			{
				if(character == '\0' || (char.IsControl(character) && character != '\n'))
				{
					Anticheat.Flag(player, $"Blocked a chat message with unsafe control characters attributed to {SafeName(player)}.", false);
					return false;
				}
			}

			return true;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId() => RpcCalls.SendChat;

		private static string SafeName(PlayerControl player)
		{
			return player != null && player.Data != null ? player.Data.PlayerName : "an unknown player";
		}
	}

	internal sealed class CastVoteCheck : RpcCheck
	{
		private const byte SkipVoteId = 253;

		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			byte sourcePlayerId = reader.ReadByte();
			byte suspectPlayerId = reader.ReadByte();
			PlayerControl claimedSource = FindPlayer(sourcePlayerId);

			if(MeetingHud.Instance == null)
			{
				if(Anticheat.HasMeetingTransitionGrace()) return false;
				Anticheat.Flag($"Blocked a vote outside a meeting claiming player id {sourcePlayerId}.");
				return false;
			}

			if(Utilities.IsAnticheatPresent()) return true;

			if(claimedSource == null || claimedSource.Data == null || claimedSource.Data.IsDead || claimedSource.Data.Disconnected)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag($"Blocked a vote with an invalid or inactive claimed voter ({sourcePlayerId}).");
				return false;
			}

			if(suspectPlayerId != SkipVoteId && FindPlayer(suspectPlayerId) == null)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag($"Blocked a vote for invalid player id {suspectPlayerId}.");
				return false;
			}

			if(AmongUsClient.Instance != null && AmongUsClient.Instance.AmHost && MeetingHud.Instance.DidVote(sourcePlayerId))
			{
				AUAnticheatPlugin.Log.LogInfo($"Ignored a duplicate vote claiming to be from {claimedSource.Data.PlayerName}.");
				return false;
			}

			return true;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId() => RpcCalls.CastVote;

		public override Type GetExpectedNetObject() => typeof(MeetingHud);

		private static PlayerControl FindPlayer(byte playerId)
		{
			foreach(PlayerControl candidate in PlayerControl.AllPlayerControls)
			{
				if(candidate != null && candidate.PlayerId == playerId) return candidate;
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/rpc/PlayAnimation.cs`:

```cs
using Hazel;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat.rpc
{
	internal class PlayAnimation : RpcCheck
	{
		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			TaskTypes animation = (TaskTypes)reader.ReadByte();
			if(Utilities.IsAnticheatPresent()) return true;

			if(LobbyBehaviour.Instance)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} sent the PlayAnimation RPC for task {animation} inside the lobby.", false);
				return false;
			}

			if(RoleManager.IsImpostorRole(player.Data.RoleType))
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} sent the PlayAnimation RPC for task {animation} when they are an imposter.", false);
				return false;
			}

			if(!GameManager.Instance.LogicOptions.GetVisualTasks())
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} sent the PlayAnimation RPC for task {animation} when visual tasks are off.", false);
				return false;
			}

			return true;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId()
		{
			return RpcCalls.PlayAnimation;
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/rpc/ReportDeadBody.cs`:

```cs
using Hazel;

using InnerNet;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat.rpc
{
	internal class ReportDeadBody : RpcCheck
	{
		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			// Current Among Us writes a player ID byte, not a NetworkedPlayerInfo net ID.
			// byte.MaxValue represents an emergency-button report with no body target.
			byte targetPlayerId = reader.ReadByte();
			if(player == null || player.Data == null)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag("Blocked ReportDeadBody without a valid player object.");
				return false;
			}

			// Another report may have started the meeting while this reliable request was
			// in flight. It is harmless and must not become evidence or a second meeting.
			if(MeetingHud.Instance != null || Anticheat.HasMeetingTransitionGrace())
			{
				AUAnticheatPlugin.Log.LogInfo($"Ignored a report from {player.Data.PlayerName} during the meeting transition.");
				return false;
			}

			if(targetPlayerId == byte.MaxValue && (GameManager.Instance == null || !GameManager.Instance.GameHasStarted || LobbyBehaviour.Instance != null))
			{
				// A report delayed across a round/lobby transition is ambiguous. Block it,
				// but do not use the confirmed-signature path.
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} attempted to create an emergency meeting outside an active round.", false);
				return false;
			}

			if(targetPlayerId == byte.MaxValue && Anticheat.IsEmergencyMeetingDuringCooldown(out double secondsRemaining))
			{
				Anticheat.FlagConfirmedExploit(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} attempted an emergency meeting about {System.Math.Ceiling(secondsRemaining)} seconds before the configured cooldown expired.");
				return false;
			}

			// Official servers validate report authority. A lobby host's local body/death
			// snapshot can be behind the server, so accepting the result avoids dropping
			// legitimate reports without weakening genuinely host-authoritative lobbies.
			if(Utilities.IsAnticheatPresent())
			{
				Anticheat.GrantMeetingTransitionGrace(3);
				return true;
			}

			NetworkedPlayerInfo target = null;
			if(targetPlayerId != byte.MaxValue)
			{
				foreach(PlayerControl candidate in PlayerControl.AllPlayerControls)
				{
					if(candidate != null && candidate.Data != null && candidate.PlayerId == targetPlayerId)
					{
						target = candidate.Data;
						break;
					}
				}

				if(target == null)
				{
					Anticheat.Flag(player, $"Blocked a report for unknown player ID {targetPlayerId}.", false);
					return false;
				}
			}
			if(GameManager.Instance == null || !GameManager.Instance.GameHasStarted || LobbyBehaviour.Instance != null)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} attempted to report outside an active round.");
				return false;
			}

			if(player.Data.IsDead || player.Data.Disconnected)
			{
				if(Anticheat.HasGhostRoleGrace(player)) return false;
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} attempted to report while dead or disconnected.");
				return false;
			}

			if(GameManager.Instance.IsHideAndSeek())
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} attempted to call a meeting in Hide and Seek");
				return false;
			}

			// A null target is a legitimate emergency-button meeting. Body reports must identify a dead player.
			if(target != null && (!target.IsDead || target.Disconnected))
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} attempted to report a player who has no valid body.");
				return false;
			}

			Anticheat.GrantMeetingTransitionGrace(3);
			return true;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId()
		{
			return RpcCalls.ReportDeadBody;
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/rpc/SetColor.cs`:

```cs
using Hazel;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat.rpc
{
	internal class SetColor : RpcCheck
	{
		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			uint netId = reader.ReadUInt32();
			byte color = reader.ReadByte();

			// This net id field written in the RPC is seemingly useless as the client RPC handler does not do anything with this value
			if(netId != player.Data.NetId)
			{
				SenderEvidence.NoteObjectClaim(netId, player.Data.NetId);
				Anticheat.Flag($"Rejected a SetColor identity mismatch: claimed object {netId}, expected {player.Data.NetId}. Possible spoofing or desynchronization; sender unverified, no player penalized.");
				return false;
			}

			if(color >= Palette.ColorNames.Length)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag($"Rejected invalid color {color} for object {player.NetId}; sender unverified, no player penalized.");
				return false;
			}

			return true;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId()
		{
			return RpcCalls.SetColor;
		}

		public override bool IsHostOnly()
		{
			return true;
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/rpc/SetLevel.cs`:

```cs
using Hazel;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat.rpc
{
	internal class SetLevel : RpcCheck
	{
		public readonly uint MAX_PLAYER_LEVEL = 10000;

		// We should not block SetLevel RPCs
		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			uint level = reader.ReadPackedUInt32();
			if(Utilities.IsAnticheatPresent()) return true;

			// The vanilla Among Us anticheat bans players if they send a SetLevel RPC with a lever greater than 100k
			// This is rather generous, we just check if the requested player level is greater than 10k
			if(level > MAX_PLAYER_LEVEL)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} sent SetLevel RPC with a level that is too high ({level}).");
				// A forged payload must not replace the last observed level with a
				// made-up value (which could also bypass the host's entry preference).
				return false;
			}

			// The SetLevel RPC should only be sent when a player joins the game in the lobby
			if(GameManager.Instance != null && GameManager.Instance.GameHasStarted)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} sent SetLevel RPC when the game has already started.");
				return false;
			}

			return true;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId()
		{
			return RpcCalls.SetLevel;
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/rpc/SetName.cs`:

```cs
using Hazel;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat.rpc
{
	internal class SetName : RpcCheck
	{
		// We increase the max name length to 12 instead of 10 as we need to account for the numbers at the end of player names when there are multiple players with the same name
		public readonly int MAX_NAME_LENGTH = 12;

		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			// On modded lobbies, it is common for players to have custom names
			if(!Utilities.IsAnticheatPresent()) return true;

			uint netId = reader.ReadUInt32();
			string requestedName = reader.ReadString();

			if(netId != GetExpectedNetId(player))
			{
				SenderEvidence.NoteObjectClaim(netId, GetExpectedNetId(player));
				Anticheat.Flag($"Rejected a SetName identity mismatch: claimed object {netId}, expected {GetExpectedNetId(player)}. Possible spoofing or desynchronization; sender unverified, no player penalized.");
				return false;
			}

			if(requestedName.Length > MAX_NAME_LENGTH)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag($"Rejected an overlong claimed name ({requestedName.Length} characters). Affected object {player.NetId}; sender unverified, no player penalized.");
				return false;
			}

			if(requestedName.Contains('<'))
			{
				Anticheat.Flag($"Rejected a claimed name containing markup. Affected object {player.NetId}; sender unverified, no player penalized.");
				return false;
			}

			return true;
		}

		private uint GetExpectedNetId(PlayerControl player)
		{
			// In host authority, the host sends the SetName RPC with the net id of the player's NetworkedPlayerInfo net object
			// In server authority, the server sends the SetName RPC with the net id of the player's PlayerControl net object
			// I don't know why this discrepancy exists, or why the SetName RPC even includes the net id field
			return Utilities.IsAnticheatPresent() ? player.NetId : player.Data.NetId;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId()
		{
			return RpcCalls.SetName;
		}

		// On vanilla servers, the host is able to receive the SetName RPC due to how server-authority works
		// On modded servers, the host should never receive the SetName RPC
		public override bool IsHostOnly()
		{
			return !Utilities.IsAnticheatPresent();
		}
	}
}

```

`source/anticheat/rpc/SetScanner.cs`:

```cs
using Hazel;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat.rpc
{
	internal class SetScanner : RpcCheck
	{
		private readonly ScannerReplayWindow replays = new();
		private string session = "";
		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			bool scanning = reader.ReadBoolean();
			byte sequence = reader.ReadByte();
			string key = $"{AmongUsClient.Instance?.GameId}:{AmongUsClient.Instance?.HostId}:{ShipStatus.Instance?.GetInstanceID()}";
			if(key != session) { replays.Reset(); session=key; }
			if(player != null && !replays.Accept(player.NetId,scanning,sequence,UnityEngine.Time.realtimeSinceStartup))
				return PacketPreflight.Block("repeated scan-start animation sequence; duplicate suppressed, not proof of a cheat menu");
			if(Utilities.IsAnticheatPresent()) return true;

			// The Medbay scan task can only be done if the map itself exists
			// If the game has not started yet (which the vanilla anticheat should already check), or if we're in the lobby to actual game transition, or the map was despawned,
			// then we know that SetScanner RPC was sent illegitimately
			if(ShipStatus.Instance == null && scanning)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} sent the SetScanner RPC while the map has not spawned in yet.", false);
				return false;
			}

			// When a player gets killed, a SetScanner RPC with the scanning value sent to false is sent
			// This applies to Imposters too if they were to somehow die, so we need to account for this false flag
			if(RoleManager.IsImpostorRole(player.Data.RoleType) && scanning)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} sent the SetScanner RPC when they are an imposter.", false);
				return false;
			}

			if(scanning && (GameManager.Instance == null || !GameManager.Instance.LogicOptions.GetVisualTasks()))
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} sent the SetScanner RPC while visual tasks were disabled.", false);
				return false;
			}

			// Do not infer task ownership from the replicated task list. Task assignment
			// and SetScanner can be observed in different orders, and scanner animation
			// itself grants no gameplay advantage. Structural, role, visual-setting, and
			// flood validation remain active.

			return true;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId()
		{
			return RpcCalls.SetScanner;
		}
	}
}

```

`source/anticheat/rpc/SetStartCounter.cs`:

```cs
using Hazel;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat.rpc
{
	internal class SetStartCounter : RpcCheck
	{
		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			reader.ReadPackedInt32();
			sbyte counter = reader.ReadSByte();
			if(Utilities.IsAnticheatPresent()) return true;

			// When a non-host player sends the SetStartCounter RPC, the counter value must always be -1
			// I'm not sure why non-host players even need to send this RPC, it's more something only the host should be sending
			if(player.OwnerId != AmongUsClient.Instance.HostId && counter != -1)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} sent a SetStartCounter RPC with an invalid value: {counter}.");

				// Revert the invalid start counter
				if(AmongUsClient.Instance.AmHost)
				{
					PlayerControl.LocalPlayer.RpcSetStartCounter(-1);
				}

				return false;
			}

			/*
			if(PlayerControl.LocalPlayer != null && LobbyBehaviour.Instance == null)
			{
				// The vanilla game already ignores SetStartCounter RPCs when the game has started, so we do not need to revert it
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} sent a SetStartCounter RPC when the lobby has despawned.");
				return false;
			}
			*/

			return true;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId()
		{
			return RpcCalls.SetStartCounter;
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/rpc/SnapTo.cs`:

```cs
using Hazel;
using System;
using UnityEngine;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat.rpc
{
	internal class SnapTo : RpcCheck
	{
		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			Vector2 position = NetHelpers.ReadVector2(reader);
			reader.ReadUInt16();

			// Lobby spawn/position corrections legitimately use SnapTo. Only active-round jumps are judged.
			if(LobbyBehaviour.Instance != null)
			{
				Anticheat.GrantMovementGrace(player, 1);
				return true;
			}

			// The official server is the movement authority and sends legitimate map,
			// decontamination, role and correction snaps through remote-owned objects.
			if(Utilities.IsAnticheatPresent())
			{
				Anticheat.GrantMovementGrace(player, 2);
				return true;
			}

			// Ghosts (including Influencer), vent travel and transitions are not
			// living-player teleports. Observers cannot override host state.
			if(AmongUsClient.Instance != null && AmongUsClient.Instance.AmHost && MovementContext.StableLivingPlayer(player))
			{
				float distance = Vector2.Distance(player.transform.position, position);
				if(distance > 8.0f)
				{
					// A forged target ID may belong to a victim. Block before applying
					// the change without awarding points to the affected player.
					return PacketPreflight.Block($"unexpected {distance:F1}-unit living-player teleport");
				}
			}

			Anticheat.GrantMovementGrace(player, 1);

			return true;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId()
		{
			return RpcCalls.SnapTo;
		}

		public override Type GetExpectedNetObject()
		{
			return typeof(CustomNetworkTransform);
		}
	}
}
```

`source/anticheat/rpc/UpdateSystem.cs`:

```cs
using Hazel;
using Il2CppInterop.Runtime;
using InnerNet;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using AUAnticheat.features;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat.rpc
{
	internal class UpdateSystem : RpcCheck
	{
		// TODO: Maybe change the variable name to something shorter lol?
		private static readonly SystemTypes[] SystemsThatCanBeUpdatedWhenDead = {
			SystemTypes.MedBay,
			SystemTypes.Sabotage,
			// Ghosts update the Security system when closing cameras, but not opening them
			SystemTypes.Security,
			SystemTypes.Ventilation
		};

		private static readonly Dictionary<Il2CppSystem.Type, Func<PlayerControl, MessageReader, bool>> systemHandlers = new Dictionary<Il2CppSystem.Type, Func<PlayerControl, MessageReader, bool>>()
		{
			{ Il2CppType.From(typeof(SwitchSystem)), ValidateSwitchSystem },
			{ Il2CppType.From(typeof(MushroomMixupSabotageSystem)), ValidateMushroomMixupSystem },
			{ Il2CppType.From(typeof(SabotageSystemType)), ValidateSabotageSystem }
		};

		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			SystemTypes system = (SystemTypes)reader.ReadByte();
			player = reader.ReadNetObject<PlayerControl>();
			if(ShipStatus.Instance == null || player == null || player.Data == null)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag("Blocked a system update with an unavailable map/player. No sender attribution.");
				return false;
			}

			ShipStatus.Instance.Systems.TryGetValue(system, out ISystemType systemInterface);
			if(systemInterface == null)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} tried to update system {system} when the current map has no such system.");
				return false;
			}

			if(system == SystemTypes.Ventilation && IsSuspiciousVentilationSequence(player, reader))
			{
				return false;
			}

			if(system == SystemTypes.Sabotage && ObserveSabotageTargetSweep(player, reader))
			{
				return false;
			}

			// On non-host clients this is already a host-relayed result. The embedded
			// player identifies the original actor, not the network sender, and local
			// state may be one update ahead or behind. Only the defending host performs
			// role/state/operation enforcement.
			if(AmongUsClient.Instance != null && (!AmongUsClient.Instance.AmHost || Utilities.IsAnticheatPresent())) return true;

			if(player.Data.IsDead && !SystemsThatCanBeUpdatedWhenDead.Contains(system))
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} tried to update system {system} while dead.");
				return false;
			}

			// thanks https://discord.com/channels/623153565053222947/754333645199900723/1292612730418757785
			Il2CppSystem.Type systemType = Il2CppType.TypeFromPointer(systemInterface.ObjectClass);

			systemHandlers.TryGetValue(systemType, out Func<PlayerControl, MessageReader, bool> handler);
			if(handler == null) return true;

			return handler(player, reader);
		}

		private static bool ObserveSabotageTargetSweep(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			int oldPosition = reader.Position;
			try
			{
				if(reader.BytesRemaining < 1) return false;
				SystemTypes target = (SystemTypes)reader.ReadByte();
				if(!Utilities.IsValidSabotage(target)) return false;
				return Anticheat.ObserveSabotageSweep(player, target);
			}
			finally
			{
				reader.Position = oldPosition;
			}
		}

		private static bool IsSuspiciousVentilationSequence(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			int oldPosition = reader.Position;
			try
			{
				if(reader.BytesRemaining < 4)
				{
					PacketPreflight.Block("truncated ventilation sequence/operation/vent payload");
					return true;
				}
				byte[] header = { reader.ReadByte(), reader.ReadByte(), reader.ReadByte(), reader.ReadByte() };
				if(!VentilationRules.TryRead(header, out _, out byte op, out byte ventId))
				{
					PacketPreflight.Block("unknown ventilation operation");
					return true;
				}
				VentilationSystem.Operation operation = (VentilationSystem.Operation)op;
				if(!VentRuleHelpers.ValidateLoadedVent(ventId)) return true;
				if(VentilationRules.IsMovement(op))
				{
					// Newer builds move through this system instead of legacy vent RPCs.
					// Replicated inVent/position flags can arrive after the operation.
					Anticheat.GrantMovementGrace(player);
					return false;
				}
				if(!Protections.ProtectAgainstNonHostKickExploit) return false;
				if(operation == VentilationSystem.Operation.StartCleaning)
				{
					if(HasIncompleteVentCleaningTask(player))
					{
						Anticheat.GrantVentCleaningGrace(player, ventId);
						return false;
					}

					FlagSuspiciousVentilation(player, operation, ventId);
					return true;
				}

				if(operation == VentilationSystem.Operation.BootImpostors)
				{
					// BootImpostors is also emitted by the legitimate Vent Cleaning task.
					// It is accepted exactly once and only after the matching, task-backed
					// StartCleaning operation. Merely owning the task is not sufficient:
					// exploit menus can forge the embedded player and send BootImpostors alone.
					if(Anticheat.TryConsumeVentCleaningGrace(player, ventId)) return false;

					FlagSuspiciousVentilation(player, operation, ventId);
					return true;
				}

				return false;
			}
			finally
			{
				reader.Position = oldPosition;
			}
		}

		private static bool HasIncompleteVentCleaningTask(PlayerControl player)
		{
			if(player == null || player.Data == null || player.Data.Tasks == null) return false;

			foreach(NetworkedPlayerInfo.TaskInfo task in player.Data.Tasks)
			{
				if(task.TypeId == (byte)TaskTypes.VentCleaning && !task.Complete) return true;
			}

			return false;
		}

		private static void FlagSuspiciousVentilation(PlayerControl player, VentilationSystem.Operation operation, ushort ventId)
		{
			string name = player != null && player.Data != null ? player.Data.PlayerName : "Unknown player";
			Anticheat.FlagConfirmedExploit(player, $"{name} sent an unexplained ventilation {operation} operation for vent {ventId}.");
		}

		// The Mushroom Mixup system is only updated in the SabotageSystemType::Update function by the host. It should never be sent by a player
		private static bool ValidateMushroomMixupSystem(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			MushroomMixupSabotageSystem.Operation operation = (MushroomMixupSabotageSystem.Operation)reader.ReadByte();
			if(AmongUsClient.Instance != null && !AmongUsClient.Instance.AmHost)
			{
				// Observers see the result after host processing; the embedded player can
				// still be the original sabotager, so it is not sender attribution.
				return true;
			}

			Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} attempted to update Mushroom Mixup system with operation {operation}.");
			return false;
		}

		private static bool ValidateSabotageSystem(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			SystemTypes system = (SystemTypes)reader.ReadByte();

			if(!Utilities.IsValidSabotage(system))
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} attempted to sabotage an invalid system: {system}.");
				return false;
			}

			if(!RoleManager.IsImpostorRole(player.Data.RoleType))
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} attempted to sabotage {system} when they are not an imposter.");
				return false;
			}

			if(GameManager.Instance.IsHideAndSeek())
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} attempted to sabotage {system} while in Hide and Seek.");
				return false;
			}

			return true;
		}

		private static bool ValidateSwitchSystem(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			byte switches = reader.ReadByte();

			if(switches.HasBit(128))
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} attempted to bulk-update switches: {switches}.");
				return false;
			}

			if(switches > 5)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} attempted to toggle an invalid switch: {switches}.");
				return false;
			}

			// Block light switch updates when lights are currently not sabotaged
			// It is possible for this check to false flag if a player is attempting to fix lights when they have not received the message about the sabotage being fixed
			// This is also why you may experience the bug where lights get unfixed right after they get fixed
			// So to avoid wrongly banning players, we just silent flag and block the RPC to prevent hackers from being able to force sabotage lights
			SwitchSystem system = ShipStatus.Instance.Systems[SystemTypes.Electrical].Cast<SwitchSystem>();
			if(system.ExpectedSwitches == system.ActualSwitches)
			{
				AUAnticheatPlugin.Log.LogInfo($"Blocked switch update from {player.Data.PlayerName} as lights are not currently sabotaged");
				return false;
			}

			// False positives may be possible if a player is toggling light switches before their client receives the StartMeeting RPC so we silent flag
			// Maybe we can check to see what state the meeting is in, and if it is after the meeting was animated then flag the player?
			if(MeetingHud.Instance)
			{
				AUAnticheatPlugin.Log.LogInfo($"Blocked switch update from {player.Data.PlayerName} as there is a currently active meeting");
				return false;
			}

			return true;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId()
		{
			return RpcCalls.UpdateSystem;
		}

		public override Type GetExpectedNetObject()
		{
			return typeof(ShipStatus);
		}
	}
}
```

```cs
using Hazel;

namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat.rpc
{
	internal class UsePlatform : RpcCheck
	{
		public override bool Validate(PlayerControl player, MessageReader reader)
		{
			if(Utilities.IsAnticheatPresent())
			{
				Anticheat.GrantMovementGrace(player, 5);
				return true;
			}
			MapNames map = Utilities.GetCurrentMap();
			if(map != MapNames.Airship)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} tried to use a platform outside of the proper map.");
				return false;
			}

			if(ShipStatus.Instance == null)
			{
				Anticheat.Flag(player, $"{player.Data.PlayerName} tried to use a platform when there is no instance of ShipStatus.");
				return false;
			}

			Anticheat.GrantMovementGrace(player, 5);
			return true;
		}

		public override RpcCalls GetId()
		{
			return RpcCalls.UsePlatform;
		}
	}
}
```

`source/features/Protections.cs`:

```cs
using HarmonyLib;
using Hazel;
using InnerNet;

namespace AUAnticheat.features
{
	internal static class Protections
	{
		public static bool BlockLargeGameMessages { get; set; } = true;
		public static bool BlockInvalidGameDataMessages { get; set; } = true;
		public static bool ProtectAgainstNonHostKickExploit { get; set; } = true;
		public static bool PreflightPackets { get; set; } = true;
		public static bool FilterVoteKickReplays { get; set; } = true;

		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(InnerNetClient), nameof(InnerNetClient.SetEndpoint))]
		public static class ForceDtls
		{
			public static bool Enabled { get; set; } = true;

			private static void Prefix(ref bool dtls)
			{
				if(Enabled) dtls = true;
			}
		}

		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(CustomNetworkTransform), nameof(CustomNetworkTransform.HandleRpc))]
		public static class BlockUnexpectedLocalTeleport
		{
			public static bool Enabled { get; set; } = true;

			private static bool Prefix(CustomNetworkTransform __instance, byte callId)
			{
				if(!Enabled || __instance == null || callId != (byte)RpcCalls.SnapTo || __instance.myPlayer != PlayerControl.LocalPlayer) return true;
				if(Utilities.IsAnticheatPresent()) return true;
				PlayerControl local = PlayerControl.LocalPlayer;
				if(AmongUsClient.Instance == null || !AmongUsClient.Instance.AmHost || !anticheat.MovementContext.StableLivingPlayer(local)) return true;

				using var evidence = anticheat.SenderEvidence.Begin("Local teleport", callId, local.NetId, local.OwnerId, anticheat.PublicText.Name(local.Data.PlayerName));
				anticheat.SenderEvidence.Record();
				AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Send("Protection", "Blocked an unsolicited position change targeting your player.");
				return false;
			}
		}

		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(MessageReader), nameof(MessageReader.ReadPackedUInt32))]
		public static class HardenedReadPackedUInt
		{
			public static bool Enabled { get; set; } = true;

			private static bool Prefix(MessageReader __instance, ref uint __result)
			{
				if(!Enabled) return true;

				uint output = 0;
				for(int index = 0; index < 5; index++)
				{
					if(__instance.BytesRemaining < 1)
					{
						throw new System.FormatException("Truncated packed integer.");
					}

					byte value = __instance.ReadByte();
					if(index == 4 && (value & 0xf0) != 0) throw new System.FormatException("Packed integer overflow.");
					output |= (uint)(value & 0x7f) << (index * 7);
					if((value & 0x80) == 0)
					{
						__result = output;
						return false;
					}
				}

				throw new System.FormatException("Overlong packed integer.");
			}
		}

		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(MeetingHud), nameof(MeetingHud.HandleRpc))]
		public static class MemoryAllocationOverload
		{
			public static bool Enabled { get; set; } = true;

			private static bool Prefix(byte callId, MessageReader reader)
			{
				if(!Enabled || callId != (byte)RpcCalls.VotingComplete) return true;

				int oldPosition = reader.Position;
				try
				{
					int arrayLength = reader.ReadPackedInt32();
					if(arrayLength < 0 || arrayLength > 1024 || arrayLength > reader.BytesRemaining)
					{
						AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Send("Protection", "Blocked a malformed voting result designed to exhaust memory.");
						return false;
					}
				}
				catch(System.Exception)
				{
					return anticheat.PacketPreflight.Block("malformed voting-result length");
				}
				finally
				{
					reader.Position = oldPosition;
				}

				return true;
			}
		}

		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(VoteBanSystem), nameof(VoteBanSystem.AddVote))]
		private static class VoteKickNotice
		{
			private static void Prefix(int srcClient, int clientId)
			{
				if(PlayerControl.LocalPlayer == null || clientId != PlayerControl.LocalPlayer.OwnerId) return;

				ClientData voter = AmongUsClient.Instance.FindClientById(srcClient);
				string name = voter == null ? "A player" : voter.PlayerName;
				AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Send("Vote-kick", $"A vote to remove you claims voter {name} (lobby ID {srcClient}). Sender unverified; this is not proof against that player.");
			}
		}
	}
}
```
