`source/network/DataHandler.cs`:

```cs
using AmongUs.InnerNet.GameDataMessages;
using HarmonyLib;
using Hazel;
using AUAnticheat.anticheat;
using AUAnticheat.features;
using InnerNet;

namespace AUAnticheat.network
{
	internal class DataHandler
	{
		// On Vanilla servers, messages are limited to at most 1201 bytes
		// On Modded Nikocat servers, messages are limited to at most 1400 bytes
		// Messages shouldn't greater than 1500 bytes anyway, as at that point messages will likely not be received by some clients due to exceeding MTU size
		// 1400 bytes seems to be the sweet spot, as it is enough to support modded servers, and also enough to prevent attacks with large messages
		public static readonly int MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH = 1400;
		public static readonly int MAX_NESTED_MESSAGES = 96;

		[HarmonyPatch(typeof(InnerNetClient), nameof(InnerNetClient.HandleGameData))]
		class HandleGameData
		{
			static bool Prefix(InnerNetClient __instance, MessageReader parentReader)
			{
				if(Protections.BlockLargeGameMessages && parentReader.Length > MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH)
				{
					PacketPreflight.Block("oversized game-data message");
					parentReader.Recycle();
					return false;
				}

				try
				{
					// Validate the complete envelope and supported RPC structures before
					// scheduling ANY child. A bad tail must not follow an executed prefix.
					if(Protections.PreflightPackets && !PacketPreflight.ValidateBatch(parentReader, Protections.BlockInvalidGameDataMessages)) return false;
					int nestedMessageCount = 0;
					while(parentReader.BytesRemaining > 0)
					{
						if(++nestedMessageCount > MAX_NESTED_MESSAGES)
						{
							PacketPreflight.Block("excessive nested message count");
							break;
						}

						MessageReader reader = parentReader.ReadMessageAsNewBuffer();
						HandleGameDataInner(__instance, reader, ++__instance.msgNum);
					}
				}
				catch(System.Exception)
				{
					PacketPreflight.Block("malformed nested game data");
				}
				finally
				{
					parentReader.Recycle();
				}

				return false;
			}
		}

		public static void HandleGameDataInner(InnerNetClient innerNetClient, MessageReader reader, int msgNum)
		{
			GameDataTypes type = (GameDataTypes)reader.Tag;

			if(Protections.BlockInvalidGameDataMessages && !PacketRules.KnownGameDataTag((byte)type))
			{
				PacketPreflight.Block("invalid game-data type");
				reader.Recycle();
				return;
			}

			bool isValid = Anticheat.HandleGameData(type, reader);
			if(!isValid)
			{
				reader.Recycle();
				return;
			}

			innerNetClient.StartCoroutine(innerNetClient.HandleGameDataInner(reader, msgNum));
		}
	}
}
```

`source/ui/GameFont.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;

namespace AUAnticheat.ui
{
	internal static class GameFont
	{
		private static Font cached;
		private static float nextLookup;
		public static Font Current
		{
			get
			{
				if(cached != null || Time.realtimeSinceStartup < nextLookup) return cached;
				nextLookup = Time.realtimeSinceStartup + 5;
				try
				{
					// Reuse installed game assets; do not redistribute proprietary font files.
					cached = TMP_Settings.defaultFontAsset?.sourceFontFile;
					if(cached != null) return cached;
					foreach(var asset in Resources.FindObjectsOfTypeAll<TMP_FontAsset>())
					{
						if(asset == null || asset.sourceFontFile == null) continue;
						string name = asset.name ?? "";
						if(name.IndexOf("VCR",StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)>=0 || name.IndexOf("Barlow",StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)>=0)
						{ cached = asset.sourceFontFile; break; }
					}
				}
				catch(Exception) { /* Font lookup is cosmetic; never disable the menu. */ }
				return cached;
			}
		}
	}
}
```

`source/ui/LoadoutsEditor.cs`:

```cs
using UnityEngine;
using AUAnticheat.anticheat;
namespace AUAnticheat.ui
{
    internal static class LoadoutsEditor
    {
        public static bool Editing;
        internal static string Draft="";
        private static string pendingName, pendingAction;
        public static void Type(string input)
        {
            if(!Editing || input == null) return;
            foreach(char c in input)
            {
                if(c=='\b') { if(Draft.Length>0) Draft=Draft.Substring(0,Draft.Length-1); }
                else if(c=='\r'||c=='\n'||c=='\u001b') { Editing=false; break; }
                else if(Draft.Length<32 && (char.IsLetterOrDigit(c)||c==' '||c=='-'||c=='_')) Draft+=c;
            }
        }
        public static void Render()
        {
            Styles.Help("Save the host's current game settings, including roles. Apply only in a waiting lobby with the same mode, map and capacity. Up to 20 named loadouts, saved on this device.");
            if(GUILayout.Button(Editing?"Stop naming loadout":"Name new loadout")) { Editing=!Editing; RulesEditor.Editing=sections.ExceptionsSection.Editing=false; }
            GUILayout.Label("Name: "+Draft+(Editing?" |":""));
            if(Editing) Styles.Help("Close game chat before typing. Enter stops typing. Then press Save current lobby settings.");
            if(GUILayout.Button("Save current lobby settings") && LobbyLoadouts.Save(Draft.Trim())) { Editing=false; Draft=""; }
            foreach(string name in LobbyLoadouts.Names)
            {
                GUILayout.Label(name,Styles.Heading);
                GUILayout.BeginHorizontal();
                if(GUILayout.Button("Apply "+name)) { pendingName=name; pendingAction="Apply"; }
                if(GUILayout.Button("Delete "+name)) { pendingName=name; pendingAction="Delete"; }
                GUILayout.EndHorizontal();
            }
            if(pendingName != null)
            {
                Styles.Help(pendingAction+" loadout '"+pendingName+"'?");
                if(GUILayout.Button("Confirm "+pendingAction))
                { if(pendingAction=="Apply") LobbyLoadouts.Apply(pendingName); else LobbyLoadouts.Delete(pendingName); pendingName=null; }
                if(GUILayout.Button("Cancel loadout action")) pendingName=null;
            }
            Styles.Help(LobbyLoadouts.Status);
        }
    }
}
```

`source/ui/MainUI.cs`:

```cs
using AUAnticheat.ui.sections;
using System;
using UnityEngine;

namespace AUAnticheat.ui
{
	public class MainUI : MonoBehaviour
	{
		public bool visible;
		public static float scale = 1;
		public static Vector2 windowPosition = new(90,70);
		public static float EffectiveScale => Math.Max(.1f, Math.Min(scale, Math.Min((Screen.width-24f)/760f,(Screen.height-24f)/600f)));
		public static Vector2 WindowSize => new Vector2(760,600) * EffectiveScale;
		public static Vector2 HeaderSize => new Vector2(760,82) * EffectiveScale;
		public static Vector2 HeaderPosition => windowPosition;
		public static Vector2 SectionListPosition => new(windowPosition.x+16*EffectiveScale,windowPosition.y+94*EffectiveScale);
		public static Vector2 SectionButtonSize => new Vector2(164,47)*EffectiveScale;
		public static Vector2 FeaturePanePosition => new(windowPosition.x+204*EffectiveScale,windowPosition.y+96*EffectiveScale);
		public static Vector2 FeaturePaneSize => new Vector2(532,474)*EffectiveScale;
		private readonly ISection[] sections = { new GeneralSection(), new LobbySection(), new NotificationsSection(), new ModeratorSection(), new ProtectionsSection(), new AnticheatSection(), new MenuSection() };
		private readonly string[] sectionErrors = new string[7];
		public byte activeTab;
		private bool dragging;
		private Vector2 dragOffset;
		public void OnApplicationQuit() { anticheat.SavedSettings.Save(); }
		public void Update()
		{
			anticheat.SavedSettings.Tick();
			if(Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Insert)) visible = !visible;
			if(!visible) { LoadoutsEditor.Editing = ExceptionsSection.Editing = RulesEditor.Editing = false; dragging = false; return; }
			if(activeTab == 1 && LoadoutsEditor.Editing) { LoadoutsEditor.Type(Input.inputString); return; }
			if(activeTab == 1 && RulesEditor.Editing) { RulesEditor.Type(Input.inputString); return; }
			if(activeTab == 1 && ExceptionsSection.Editing) { ExceptionsSection.Type(Input.inputString); return; }
			RulesEditor.Editing = false;
			LoadoutsEditor.Editing = false;
			ExceptionsSection.Editing = false;
			if(Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Escape)) { visible = false; return; }
			if(Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.UpArrow) || Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.DownArrow))
				activeTab = (byte)Math.Clamp(activeTab+(Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.UpArrow)?-1:1),0,sections.Length-1);
		}
		public static void ClampWindow()
		{
			windowPosition.x = Math.Clamp(windowPosition.x,4,Math.Max(4,Screen.width-WindowSize.x-4));
			windowPosition.y = Math.Clamp(windowPosition.y,4,Math.Max(4,Screen.height-WindowSize.y-4));
		}
		public void OnGUI()
		{
			if(!visible) return;
			ClampWindow();
			using var theme = Styles.UseTheme();
			float s = EffectiveScale, x = windowPosition.x, y = windowPosition.y;
			GUI.Box(new Rect(x,y,WindowSize.x,WindowSize.y),"",Styles.WindowBox);
			Styles.DrawBadge(new Rect(x+20*s,y+14*s,48*s,54*s));
			GUI.Label(new Rect(x+82*s,y+12*s,490*s,36*s),"AU-Anticheat",Styles.Title);
			GUI.Label(new Rect(x+84*s,y+49*s,560*s,28*s),"Protection and lobby controls   /   " + AUAnticheatPlugin.DisplayVersion,Styles.Hint);
			if(GUI.Button(new Rect(x+700*s,y+22*s,38*s,36*s),"X",Styles.SectionBox)) { visible = false; ExceptionsSection.Editing = false; }
			for(byte i=0;i<sections.Length;i++)
			{
				var rect = new Rect(SectionListPosition.x,SectionListPosition.y+i*54*s,SectionButtonSize.x,SectionButtonSize.y);
				if(GUI.Button(rect,sections[i].name,activeTab==i?Styles.SectionBoxActive:Styles.SectionBox))
				{ activeTab=i; LoadoutsEditor.Editing=ExceptionsSection.Editing=RulesEditor.Editing=false; }
			}
			GUI.Label(new Rect(x+22*s,y+524*s,165*s,56*s),"INSERT  Open / close\nDrag the top bar to move",Styles.Hint);
			GUI.Label(new Rect(x+22*s,y+577*s,220*s,23*s),"by xav0dev - Boo",Styles.Hint);
			RenderActiveSection(); // Navigation always renders before a fallible panel.
			HandleDrag();
		}
		[Il2CppInterop.Runtime.Attributes.HideFromIl2Cpp]
		private void RenderActiveSection()
		{
			if(activeTab>=sections.Length) activeTab=0;
			var section=sections[activeTab];
			try
			{
				GUILayout.BeginArea(new Rect(FeaturePanePosition.x,FeaturePanePosition.y,FeaturePaneSize.x,FeaturePaneSize.y));
				try
				{
					section.scrollVector=GUILayout.BeginScrollView(section.scrollVector);
					try
					{
						GUILayout.Label(section.name,Styles.Title);
						if(sectionErrors[activeTab]==null) section.Render();
						else { Styles.Help("This panel could not be drawn. Other tabs still work."); if(GUILayout.Button("Retry panel")) sectionErrors[activeTab]=null; }
					}
					finally { GUILayout.EndScrollView(); }
				}
				finally { GUILayout.EndArea(); }
			}
			catch(Exception e) when(e.GetType().Name!="ExitGUIException")
			{ if(sectionErrors[activeTab]==null) AUAnticheatPlugin.Log.LogWarning($"Menu panel {section.name} failed: {e}"); sectionErrors[activeTab]=e.GetType().Name; }
		}
		private void HandleDrag()
		{
			var e=Event.current; if(e==null) return;
			float s=EffectiveScale;
			var header=new Rect(windowPosition.x,windowPosition.y,680*s,82*s);
			if(e.type==EventType.MouseDown && e.button==0 && header.Contains(e.mousePosition)) { dragging=true; dragOffset=e.mousePosition-windowPosition; e.Use(); }
			else if(e.type==EventType.MouseDrag && dragging) { windowPosition=e.mousePosition-dragOffset; ClampWindow(); e.Use(); }
			else if(e.type==EventType.MouseUp) dragging=false;
		}
	}
}
```

```cs
using UnityEngine;
using AUAnticheat.anticheat;
namespace AUAnticheat.ui
{
    internal static class ModerationTools
    {
        public static void Render()
        {
            RemovalRequestGuard.Enabled = Styles.Toggle(RemovalRequestGuard.Enabled,"Guard local kick/ban and late-rejection requests");
            Styles.Help($"Local removal requests stopped: {RemovalRequestGuard.Blocked}. This does not cancel server removals or protect other clients from every remote removal attack.");
            ChatModeration.Ads = Styles.Toggle(ChatModeration.Ads,"Hide lobby ads locally (never ban)");
            ChatModeration.Spam = Styles.Toggle(ChatModeration.Spam,"Hide chat floods locally");
            ChatModeration.WordFilter = Styles.Toggle(ChatModeration.WordFilter,"Use saved word filter");
            ChatModeration.NameFilter = Styles.Toggle(ChatModeration.NameFilter,"Hide chat from filtered names");
            ModerationStore.EnforceBans = Styles.Toggle(ModerationStore.EnforceBans,"Enforce my persistent ban list when hosting");
            ChatModeration.AfkAlerts = Styles.Toggle(ChatModeration.AfkAlerts,"Show possible AFK players in lobby (alert only)");
            ChatModeration.AfkSeconds = (int)GUILayout.HorizontalSlider(ChatModeration.AfkSeconds,60,600);
            Styles.Help($"AFK alert after {ChatModeration.AfkSeconds}s without observed movement/chat. Never kicks automatically; lag and menus can look idle.");
            Styles.Help($"Hidden locally: {ChatModeration.Hidden}. Last reason: {ChatModeration.LastReason}. Other players may still receive filtered chat on official servers. No cheat points or automatic bans for chat filters.");
            Styles.Help("Watchlist = private review reminder, not evidence. Block = hide their chat locally. Persistent ban = host-only admission policy; friend codes are game-reported, not authenticated identities.");
            if(GUILayout.Button("Reload moderation lists and filters")) ModerationStore.Reload();
            Styles.Help("Edit filtered-words.txt or filtered-names.txt (one literal phrase per line), then reload. Folder: " + ModerationStore.DirectoryPath);
            foreach(var entry in ModerationStore.Snapshot())
                if(GUILayout.Button($"Remove {entry.Kind}: {entry.FriendCode}")) ModerationStore.Set(entry.FriendCode,entry.Kind,false);
            Styles.Help(ModerationStore.Status);
        }
        public static void Selected(PlayerControl player)
        {
            string code = ChatModeration.BoundCode(player);
            if(code == null) { Styles.Help("Local lists require a matching, loaded friend code."); return; }
            foreach(string kind in new[]{"block","watch"})
            {
                bool contains = ModerationStore.Contains(code,kind);
                if(GUILayout.Button((contains ? "Remove local " : "Add local ") + kind)) ModerationStore.Set(code,kind,!contains);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`source/ui/Notification.cs`:

```cs
namespace AUAnticheat.ui
{
	public class Notification
	{
		public readonly string title;
		public readonly string message;
		public readonly float ttl;
		public float lifetime;

		public Notification(string title, string message, float ttl)
		{
			this.title = title;
			this.message = message;
			this.ttl = ttl;
			this.lifetime = 0;
		}

		public bool HasExpired
		{
			get { return this.lifetime > ttl; }
		}
	}
}
```

`source/ui/NotificationManager.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace AUAnticheat.ui
{
	internal class NotificationManager : MonoBehaviour
	{
		private const int MaximumQueuedNotifications = 32;
		private const int MaximumHistoryEntries = 200;
		private const int MaximumEventsPerSecond = 12;
		public List<Notification> notifications = new List<Notification>();
		public bool DisableNotifications = false;
		public bool ShowTechnicalDetails = false;
		private readonly Dictionary<string, float> lastShownByMessage = new Dictionary<string, float>();
		private readonly List<string> notificationHistory = new List<string>();
		private readonly List<string> notificationDetails = new List<string>();
		private float eventWindowStarted;
		private int eventsInWindow;
		private string historySessionKey = string.Empty;

		public static Vector2 BoxSize
		{
			get { return new Vector2(380, 154) * MainUI.EffectiveScale; }
		}

		public static Vector2 BoxHeaderSize
		{
			get { return new Vector2(BoxSize.x, 17 * MainUI.scale); }
		}

		public static Vector2 BoxContentPadding
		{
			get { return new Vector2(10, 0) * MainUI.scale; }
		}

		public static Vector2 BoxContentSize
		{
			get { return new Vector2(BoxSize.x - BoxContentPadding.x, BoxSize.y - BoxHeaderSize.y - BoxSliderSize.y); }
		}

		public static Vector2 BoxSliderSize
		{
			get { return new Vector2(BoxSize.x, 20 * MainUI.scale); }
		}

		public void Update()
		{
			anticheat.LobbyServices.Tick();
			EnsureHistorySession();
			int notificationCount = Math.Min(GetMaxNotifications(), notifications.Count);

			for(int i = 0; i < notificationCount; i++)
			{
				Notification notification = notifications[i];
				notification.lifetime += Time.deltaTime;

				if(notification.HasExpired)
				{
					notifications.RemoveAt(i);

					// Since we removed an element from the notifications list, we have to decrement both the current notification index
					// and the max notifications to avoid errors from accessing outside the list length
					i--;
					notificationCount--;
					continue;
				}
			}
		}

		public void OnGUI()
		{
			if(DisableNotifications) return;
			using var theme = Styles.UseTheme();

			int notificationCount = Math.Min(GetMaxNotifications(), notifications.Count);

			for(byte i = 0; i < notificationCount; i++)
			{
				RenderNotification(i, notifications[i]);
			}
		}

		[Il2CppInterop.Runtime.Attributes.HideFromIl2Cpp]
		private void RenderNotification(byte position, Notification notification)
		{
			float s = MainUI.EffectiveScale;
			float boxX = Screen.width - BoxSize.x - 8;
			float boxY = Screen.height - (BoxSize.y + 8*s) * (position + 1);
			GUI.Box(new Rect(boxX, boxY, BoxSize.x, BoxSize.y), "", Styles.MainBox);
			Styles.DrawBadge(new Rect(boxX+12*s,boxY+10*s,24*s,28*s));
			GUI.Label(new Rect(boxX+44*s,boxY+10*s,320*s,28*s),notification.title,Styles.Heading);
			GUI.Label(new Rect(boxX+16*s,boxY+44*s,346*s,98*s),notification.message,Styles.Hint);
		}

		public int GetMaxNotifications()
		{
			return Screen.height / 2 / (int)BoxSize.y;
		}

		// The time to live value for a notification should be five seconds if it is a success message, and ten seconds if it is a failure message
		public void Send(string title, string message, float ttl = 10)
		{
			bool protection = title.StartsWith("Anti-cheat") || title == "Protection";
			string affected = protection ? anticheat.SenderEvidence.TargetSummary : "";
			if(protection) message += "\n" + affected;
			ttl = Math.Max(10, ttl);
			float now = Time.realtimeSinceStartup;

			// Suppressed duplicates must not consume the global event budget; otherwise
			// one repeating warning can hide unrelated notifications from history.
			string duplicateKey = $"{title}\n{message}";
			if(lastShownByMessage.TryGetValue(duplicateKey, out float lastShown) && now - lastShown < 1.0f) return;

			if(now - eventWindowStarted >= 1.0f)
			{
				eventWindowStarted = now;
				eventsInWindow = 0;
			}

			if(eventsInWindow >= MaximumEventsPerSecond) return;
			eventsInWindow++;

			if(lastShownByMessage.Count >= 64) lastShownByMessage.Clear();
			lastShownByMessage[duplicateKey] = now;

			AUAnticheatPlugin.Log.LogMessage($"[Notification] [{title}] {message}");
			EnsureHistorySession();
			notificationDetails.Add($"{DateTime.Now:HH:mm:ss} | {title} | {message}");
			message = anticheat.PublicText.Simple(title, message);
			// Keep the target first on the compact popup, not below text that may clip.
			if(protection)
			{
				if(message.EndsWith("\n" + affected)) message = message.Substring(0,message.Length-affected.Length-1);
				message = affected + "\n" + message;
			}
			notificationHistory.Add($"{DateTime.Now:HH:mm:ss} | {title} | {message}");
			if(notificationHistory.Count > MaximumHistoryEntries) { notificationHistory.RemoveAt(0); notificationDetails.RemoveAt(0); }

			if(DisableNotifications) return;
			if(notifications.Count >= MaximumQueuedNotifications) notifications.RemoveAt(0);

			Notification notification = new Notification(title, message, ttl);
			notifications.Add(notification);
		}

		public void ClearNotifications()
		{
			notifications.Clear();
			lastShownByMessage.Clear();
		}

		public string GetHistorySummary(int maximum = 50)
		{
			EnsureHistorySession();
			if(notificationHistory.Count == 0) return "No notifications recorded in this lobby.";

			int start = Math.Max(0, notificationHistory.Count - maximum);
			return string.Join("\n", (ShowTechnicalDetails ? notificationDetails : notificationHistory).GetRange(start, notificationHistory.Count - start));
		}

		public int HistoryCount
		{
			get { EnsureHistorySession(); return notificationHistory.Count; }
		}

		public void ClearHistory()
		{
			notificationHistory.Clear();
			notificationDetails.Clear();
		}

		private void EnsureHistorySession()
		{
			string current = AmongUsClient.Instance == null ? "offline" : $"{AmongUsClient.Instance.GameId}:{AmongUsClient.Instance.HostId}";
			if(current == historySessionKey) return;

			historySessionKey = current;
			notificationHistory.Clear();
			notificationDetails.Clear();
			lastShownByMessage.Clear();
		}
	}
}
```

`source/ui/PlayerPresentation.cs`:

```cs
using UnityEngine;

namespace AUAnticheat.ui
{
	internal static class PlayerPresentation
	{
		public static string Name(PlayerControl player) => anticheat.PublicText.Name(player?.Data?.PlayerName);
		public static Color GetColor(PlayerControl player)
		{
			var outfit = player?.CurrentOutfit ?? player?.Data?.DefaultOutfit;
			int id = outfit == null ? -1 : outfit.ColorId;
			return Palette.PlayerColors != null && id >= 0 && id < Palette.PlayerColors.Length
				? (Color)Palette.PlayerColors[id] : Color.white;
		}
		public static GUIStyle NameStyle(PlayerControl player, bool selected)
		{
			return Styles.PlayerName(GetColor(player),selected);
		}
		public static string Details(PlayerControl player)
		{
			if(player?.Data == null) return "Player details are loading.";
			var peer = AmongUsClient.Instance?.FindClientById(player.OwnerId);
			// Never borrow an old connection's details for a replacement character.
			if(peer != null && (peer.Character != player || peer.IsBeingCreated)) peer = null;
			string code = anticheat.PublicText.Clean(peer?.FriendCode ?? player.Data.FriendCode,36);
			string account = anticheat.PublicText.Clean(peer?.ProductUserId,64);
			// Peer and player-info updates are not atomic. A display mismatch must
			// not hide both values forever; admission uses separate, stricter checks.
			uint reported = player.Data.PlayerLevel != uint.MaxValue ? player.Data.PlayerLevel : peer?.PlayerLevel ?? uint.MaxValue;
			string level = reported == uint.MaxValue ? "Unavailable (not received)" : ((ulong)reported+1).ToString();
			if(reported == 0) level += " (unconfirmed: may still be initializing)";
			if(player.Data.PlayerLevel != uint.MaxValue && peer != null && peer.PlayerLevel != uint.MaxValue && peer.PlayerLevel != player.Data.PlayerLevel)
				level += $" (peer reports {(ulong)peer.PlayerLevel+1}; records differ)";
			string platform = peer?.PlatformData == null ? "Unavailable" : peer.PlatformData.Platform.ToString() switch
			{
				"StandaloneEpicPC"=>"PC / Epic", "StandaloneSteamPC"=>"PC / Steam", "StandaloneMac"=>"Mac",
				"StandaloneWin10"=>"PC / Microsoft", "StandaloneItch"=>"PC / itch.io", "IPhone"=>"iOS",
				"Android"=>"Android", "Switch"=>"Nintendo Switch", "Xbox"=>"Xbox", "Playstation"=>"PlayStation", _=>"Unknown"
			};
			return $"Friend code: {(code.Length==0?"Unavailable":code)}\nAccount ID: {(account.Length==0?"Unavailable":account)}\nLevel: {level}     Platform: {platform}";
		}
	}
}
```

`source/ui/RulesEditor.cs`:

```cs
using UnityEngine;
using AUAnticheat.anticheat;
namespace AUAnticheat.ui
{
 internal static class RulesEditor
 {
  public static bool Editing;
  internal static string Draft="";
  public static void Type(string input)
  {
   if(!Editing || input==null) return;
   foreach(char c in input)
   {
    if(c=='\b') { if(Draft.Length>0) Draft=Draft.Substring(0,Draft.Length-1); }
    else if(c=='\u001b') Editing=false;
    else if(c=='\r') { if(Draft.Length<4096) Draft+='\n'; }
    else if((c=='\n'||!char.IsControl(c))&&Draft.Length<4096) Draft+=c;
   }
  }
  public static void Render()
  {
   Styles.Help(LobbyRules.MessageInfo);
   if(!Editing)
   {
    if(GUILayout.Button("Edit rules")) { Draft=LobbyRules.Preview; Editing=true; LoadoutsEditor.Editing=sections.ExceptionsSection.Editing=false; }
    if(GUILayout.Button("Reload saved rules")) LobbyRules.Reload();
    GUILayout.Label(LobbyRules.Preview);
    return;
   }
   Styles.Help("Type here. Enter adds a rule; Backspace deletes; Escape stops editing without saving. Close game chat first. All rules must fit one 100-character message including separators. No label is added. Overlong edits cannot replace your saved rules.");
   Styles.Help(LobbyRules.DraftMessageInfo(Draft));
   GUILayout.Label(Draft+" |",Styles.SectionBox);
   if(GUILayout.Button("Clear draft")) Draft="";
   if(GUILayout.Button("Save rules")&&LobbyRules.SaveText(Draft)) Editing=false;
   if(GUILayout.Button("Cancel editing")) Editing=false;
  }
 }
}
```

```cs
using AUAnticheat.anticheat;
using UnityEngine;

namespace AUAnticheat.ui

	internal sealed class StatusIndicator : MonoBehaviour
	{

		private int cachedFontSize;
		public static string StateLabel => !AUAnticheatPlugin.ProtectionsAttached ? "INACTIVE" : Anticheat.Enabled ? "ACTIVE" : "PAUSED";
		public void OnGUI()
		{
			using var theme = Styles.UseTheme();
			float s = Mathf.Clamp(Screen.height/1080f,.65f,1.15f);
			float w=250*s,h=36*s,x=Screen.width-w-5,y=4;
			GUI.Box(new Rect(x,y,w,h),"",Styles.MainBox);
			Styles.DrawBadge(new Rect(x+7*s,y+3*s,27*s,30*s));
			int fontSize=(int)(14*s);
			if(!ReferenceEquals(cachedHeading,Styles.Heading) || cachedFontSize!=fontSize)
			{
				cachedHeading=Styles.Heading; cachedFontSize=fontSize;
				title=new GUIStyle(cachedHeading) { fontSize=fontSize,wordWrap=false };
				status=new GUIStyle(Styles.Hint) { fontSize=(int)(11*s),fontStyle=FontStyle.Bold,wordWrap=false };
			}
			status.normal.textColor = !AUAnticheatPlugin.ProtectionsAttached ? new Color(1,.4f,.4f) : Anticheat.Enabled ? Styles.Accent : new Color(1,.75f,.3f);
			GUI.Label(new Rect(x+40*s,y+5*s,131*s,28*s),"AU-Anticheat",title);
			GUI.Label(new Rect(x+175*s,y+7*s,73*s,25*s),StateLabel,status);
		}
	}
}
```

`source/ui/Styles.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace AUAnticheat.ui
{
	// Code-drawn panels. Never modifies the game's shared GUI skin.
	internal static class Styles
	{
		public enum UIColors { Azure, Carbon, Cardinal, Pesto, Pumpkin, White, Violet }
		public static readonly Dictionary<UIColors, Color> ColorValues = new()
		{
			{ UIColors.Azure, new Color(.12f,.88f,.82f) }, { UIColors.Carbon, new Color(.38f,.48f,.56f) },
			{ UIColors.Cardinal, new Color(1,.35f,.4f) }, { UIColors.Pesto, new Color(.4f,.85f,.44f) },
			{ UIColors.Pumpkin, new Color(1,.68f,.25f) }, { UIColors.White, new Color(.9f,.95f,1) },
			{ UIColors.Violet, new Color(.72f,.55f,1) }
		};
		public static float menuOpacity = .75f;
		public static UIColors primaryColor = UIColors.Cardinal;
		public static Color Accent => ColorValues[primaryColor];
		private static readonly Color Ink = new(.035f,.055f,.085f);
		private static readonly Dictionary<string, Texture2D> textures = new();
		private static readonly Dictionary<string, GUIStyle> styles = new();
		private static GUISkin themedSkin;
		private static float builtScale, builtOpacity;
		private static UIColors builtColor;
		private static int scopes;
		private static Font builtFont;
		public static IDisposable UseTheme()
		{
			Ensure(); var previous = GUI.skin; GUI.skin = themedSkin; scopes++; return new SkinScope(previous);
		}
		private sealed class SkinScope : IDisposable
		{
			private readonly GUISkin previous;
			public SkinScope(GUISkin value) { previous = value; }
			public void Dispose() { GUI.skin = previous; scopes--; }
		}
		private static void Ensure()
		{
			float scale = MainUI.EffectiveScale;
			if(themedSkin != null && scopes > 0) return;
			Font gameFont = GameFont.Current;
			if(themedSkin != null && builtScale == scale && builtColor == primaryColor && builtOpacity == menuOpacity && builtFont == gameFont) return;
			ClearCache(); builtScale = scale; builtColor = primaryColor; builtOpacity = menuOpacity;
			builtFont = gameFont;
			themedSkin = UnityEngine.Object.Instantiate(GUI.skin);
			if(gameFont != null) themedSkin.font = gameFont;
			GUIStyle label = new(themedSkin.label) { fontSize = Size(15), wordWrap = true, richText = false,
				padding = Pad(3,3,5,5), margin = Pad(2,2,2,3) };
			label.normal.textColor = Color.white; themedSkin.label = label;
			if(gameFont != null) label.font = gameFont;
			GUIStyle button = new(themedSkin.button) { fontSize = Size(15), fontStyle = FontStyle.Bold,
				wordWrap = true, richText = false, padding = Pad(12,12,9,9), margin = Pad(2,2,4,4), border = new RectOffset { left=12,right=12,top=12,bottom=12 } };
			bool redTheme = primaryColor == UIColors.Cardinal;
			button.normal.background = Texture("button", redTheme ? new Color(.25f,.10f,.13f) : new Color(.15f,.22f,.28f), redTheme ? new Color(.72f,.40f,.44f) : new Color(.53f,.66f,.72f));
			button.normal.textColor = Color.white;
			if(gameFont != null) button.font = gameFont;
			button.hover.background = Texture("hover", redTheme ? new Color(.36f,.14f,.18f) : new Color(.2f,.32f,.39f), Accent); button.hover.textColor = Color.white;
			button.active.background = Texture("pressed", Accent, Color.white); button.active.textColor = Ink;
			button.onNormal = button.active; button.onHover = button.active; button.onActive = button.active;
			themedSkin.button = button;
			themedSkin.toggle = new GUIStyle(button) { alignment = TextAnchor.MiddleLeft };
			styles["panel"] = new GUIStyle(button) { normal = new GUIStyleState { background = Texture("panel", redTheme ? new Color(.16f,.055f,.075f) : new Color(.075f,.115f,.16f), Color.white, menuOpacity) } };
			// Keep the status/alert panel style unchanged. The interactive menu must
			// not inherit opaque button hover/active backgrounds from the skin.
			var fill = new Color(.16f,.055f,.075f);
			var window = new GUIStyle(styles["panel"]);
			window.normal = new GUIStyleState { background = Texture("window", fill, Color.white, menuOpacity) };
			window.hover = new GUIStyleState { background = Texture("window-hover", fill, Accent, menuOpacity) };
			window.active = window.focused = window.onNormal = window.onHover = window.onActive = window.onFocused = window.hover;
			styles["window"] = window;
			button.normal.background = Texture("menu-button", fill, new Color(.72f,.40f,.44f), menuOpacity);
			button.hover.background = Texture("menu-button-hover", fill, Accent, menuOpacity);
			button.active = button.focused = button.onNormal = button.onHover = button.onActive = button.onFocused = button.hover;
			themedSkin.button = button;
			themedSkin.toggle = new GUIStyle(button) { alignment = TextAnchor.MiddleLeft };
			styles["tab"] = new GUIStyle(button) { alignment = TextAnchor.MiddleLeft, fontSize = Size(16), fixedHeight = 0 };
			styles["selected"] = new GUIStyle(styles["tab"]) { normal = button.active };
			styles["title"] = new GUIStyle(label) { fontSize = Size(23), fontStyle = FontStyle.Bold };
			styles["heading"] = new GUIStyle(label) { fontSize = Size(17), fontStyle = FontStyle.Bold };
			styles["hint"] = new GUIStyle(label) { fontSize = Size(13) }; styles["hint"].normal.textColor = new Color(.67f,.77f,.84f);
			styles["danger"] = new GUIStyle(button); styles["danger"].normal.textColor = new Color(1,.53f,.52f);
		}
		private static int Size(int value) => Math.Max(1, (int)(value * MainUI.EffectiveScale));
		private static RectOffset Pad(int l,int r,int t,int b) => new() { left=Size(l),right=Size(r),top=Size(t),bottom=Size(b) };
		private static GUIStyle Get(string name) { Ensure(); return styles[name]; }
		public static GUIStyle MainBox => Get("panel");
		public static GUIStyle WindowBox => Get("window");
		public static GUIStyle SectionBox => Get("tab");
		public static GUIStyle SectionBoxActive => Get("selected");
		public static GUIStyle Title => Get("title");
		public static GUIStyle Heading => Get("heading");
		public static GUIStyle Hint => Get("hint");
		public static GUIStyle Danger => Get("danger");
		private static bool badgeUnavailable;
		public static void DrawBadge(Rect position)
		{
			if(badgeUnavailable) return;
			try
			{
				Ensure();
				if(!styles.TryGetValue("badge-draw",out var style))
				{
					style = new GUIStyle { border = new RectOffset(), padding = new RectOffset(), margin = new RectOffset() };
					style.normal.background = BadgeIcon;
					styles["badge-draw"] = style;
				}
				// GUI.DrawTexture forwards into a stripped overload in v19. GUI.Box
				// with a background texture uses the available native GUIStyle path.
				GUI.Box(position,"",style);
			}
			catch(Exception error) when(error.GetType().Name != "ExitGUIException")
			{
				badgeUnavailable = true;
				AUAnticheatPlugin.Log.LogWarning($"Decorative badge disabled; menu remains available: {error.GetType().Name}");
			}
		}
		public static GUIStyle PlayerName(Color color, bool selected)
		{
			Ensure();
			string key = $"player:{color.r}:{color.g}:{color.b}:{selected}";
			if(styles.TryGetValue(key,out var cached)) return cached;
			bool dark = .2126f*color.r + .7152f*color.g + .0722f*color.b < .35f;
			var style = new GUIStyle(SectionBox) { richText=false, fontStyle=selected?FontStyle.Bold:FontStyle.Normal };
			style.normal.textColor=style.hover.textColor=style.active.textColor=color;
			var background = Texture(dark ? "name-light" : "name-dark",dark?new Color(.91f,.94f,.96f):new Color(.06f,.09f,.12f),new Color(.5f,.61f,.66f));
			style.normal.background=style.hover.background=style.active.background=background;
			style.hover.background=style.active.background=Texture(dark ? "name-light-hover" : "name-dark-hover",dark?new Color(.91f,.94f,.96f):new Color(.06f,.09f,.12f),Accent);
			style.focused = style.hover;
			if(styles.Count<128) styles[key]=style;
			return style;
		}
		public static bool Toggle(bool value, string text) => GUILayout.Toggle(value, (value ? "ON   " : "OFF   ") + text);
		public static void Help(string text) => GUILayout.Label(text, Hint);
		public static bool Foldout(bool open, string text) => GUILayout.Button((open ? "-  " : "+  ") + text) ? !open : open;
		public static void HeadingText(string text) { GUILayout.Space(8 * MainUI.EffectiveScale); GUILayout.Label(text, Heading); }
		private static Texture2D Texture(string key, Color fill, Color border, float alpha = 1)
		{
			if(textures.TryGetValue(key, out var cached)) return cached;
			var texture = new Texture2D(32,32) { filterMode = FilterMode.Bilinear, wrapMode = TextureWrapMode.Clamp };
			for(int y=0;y<32;y++) for(int x=0;x<32;x++)
			{
				bool outer = Rounded(x,y,1,30,8), inner = Rounded(x,y,4,27,6);
				Color c = !outer ? Color.clear : inner ? fill : border; c.a *= alpha; texture.SetPixel(x,y,c);
			}
			texture.Apply(); textures[key] = texture; return texture;
		}
		private static bool Rounded(int x,int y,int lo,int hi,int radius)
		{
			if(x<lo || y<lo || x>hi || y>hi) return false;
			int dx = Math.Max(lo+radius-x, Math.Max(0,x-(hi-radius)));
			int dy = Math.Max(lo+radius-y, Math.Max(0,y-(hi-radius)));
			return dx*dx+dy*dy <= radius*radius;
		}
		public static Texture2D BadgeIcon
		{
			get
			{
				Ensure(); if(textures.TryGetValue("badge", out var icon)) return icon;
				icon = new Texture2D(64,72) { filterMode = FilterMode.Bilinear, wrapMode = TextureWrapMode.Clamp };
				for(int y=0;y<72;y++) for(int x=0;x<64;x++)
				{
					// A bordered X with transparent surroundings, drawn without GUI.DrawTexture.
					int diagonal = Math.Min(Math.Abs((x-32)-(y-36)),Math.Abs((x-32)+(y-36)));
					bool outer = x>=8 && x<=56 && y>=12 && y<=60 && diagonal<=8;
					bool inner = x>=11 && x<=53 && y>=15 && y<=57 && diagonal<=4;
					Color c = inner ? Accent : outer ? Color.white : Color.clear;
					icon.SetPixel(x,y,c);
				}
				icon.Apply(); textures["badge"] = icon; return icon;
			}
		}
		public static void ClearCache()
		{
			foreach(var texture in textures.Values) UnityEngine.Object.Destroy(texture);
			textures.Clear(); styles.Clear();
			if(themedSkin != null) UnityEngine.Object.Destroy(themedSkin);
			themedSkin = null;
		}
	}
}
```

```cs
using AUAnticheat.anticheat;
using System;
using UnityEngine;
namespace AUAnticheat.ui.sections
{
 internal class AnticheatSection : ISection
 {
  private bool checks;
  public AnticheatSection() : base("Detection") { }
  public override void Render()
  {
   Styles.Help("Checks game actions this client receives, not software installed on another player's device. An alert is not always proof of cheating.");
   Anticheat.Enabled = Styles.Toggle(Anticheat.Enabled, "Cheat detection");
   Anticheat.SendNotification = Styles.Toggle(Anticheat.SendNotification, "Show detection alerts");
   Anticheat.DiscardInvalidMessages = Styles.Toggle(Anticheat.DiscardInvalidMessages, "Block invalid actions");
   Styles.Help("Stops covered invalid messages before this client applies them. Turning this off can allow a flagged action to continue locally.");
   Anticheat.CheckSpoofedPlatforms = Styles.Toggle(Anticheat.CheckSpoofedPlatforms, "Check platform inconsistencies");
   Anticheat.EnhancedMenuBehaviorChecks = Styles.Toggle(Anticheat.EnhancedMenuBehaviorChecks, "Extra behaviour checks");
   Styles.Help("Also groups sustained mixed-action patterns in Alerts > Related suspicious actions. This helps review events, but cannot verify a forged sender or add ban points.");
   Styles.Help("Looks for inconsistent game behaviour, not menu names. Reported platforms can be spoofed; they are not device scans or verified identities.");
   Styles.Help("Scan-animation replays and action floods can be filtered locally. A fake walk animation or hotkey used only on another device cannot be reliably detected here.");
   Styles.HeadingText("Host response");
   GUILayout.Label("Automatic action: " + Anticheat.Punishment);
   GUILayout.BeginHorizontal();
   try { foreach(var action in new[]{Anticheat.Punishments.None,Anticheat.Punishments.Kick,Anticheat.Punishments.Ban})
    if(GUILayout.Button((Anticheat.Punishment==action ? "> " : "")+action)) Anticheat.Punishment=action; }
   finally { GUILayout.EndHorizontal(); }
   Styles.Help("Threshold response: None records evidence without a score-based removal; Kick removes from this lobby; Ban also prevents ordinary rejoining. Confirmed exploit signatures can use a separate immediate-ban path. Only the host can remove a player.");
   GUILayout.Label($"Point threshold: {Anticheat.EnforcementThreshold}");
   Anticheat.EnforcementThreshold=(int)Math.Round(GUILayout.HorizontalSlider(Anticheat.EnforcementThreshold,50,200));
   Styles.Help("Minor: 50 points. Major: 100. Unverified senders get alerts, not points or bans.");
   Styles.Help("A lower threshold acts sooner but leaves less room for mistakes. Review Alerts before changing this. Unknown senders are not blamed on whichever player an action names.");
   checks=Styles.Foldout(checks,"Individual message checks");
   if(checks)
   {
    Styles.Help("Advanced troubleshooting: these are the game's message names. Switching one off disables its check, not the player's ability to use that normal action.");
    foreach(var entry in Anticheat.RpcHandlers)
     entry.Value.Enabled=Styles.Toggle(entry.Value.Enabled,entry.Key.ToString());
   }
  }
 }
}
```

`source/ui/sections/ExceptionsSection.cs`:

```cs
using UnityEngine;
using AUAnticheat.anticheat;

namespace AUAnticheat.ui.sections
{
	internal sealed class ExceptionsSection : ISection
	{
		public static bool Editing;
		internal static string Draft = "";
		private static string status = "";
		public ExceptionsSection() : base("Exceptions") { }
		// Input.inputString works without the stripped native GUI text editor.
		public static void Type(string input)
		{
			if(!Editing || string.IsNullOrEmpty(input)) return;
			foreach(char c in input)
			{
				if(c == '\b') { if(Draft.Length > 0) Draft = Draft.Substring(0, Draft.Length - 1); }
				else if(c == '\n' || c == '\r') { Add(); break; }
				else if(c == '\u001b') { Editing = false; break; }
				else if(Draft.Length < 36 && ((c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') || (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') || (c >= '0' && c <= '9') || c == '#')) Draft += c;
			}
		}
		private static void Add()
		{
			if(FriendCodeExceptions.Add(Draft)) { Draft = ""; Editing = false; status = "Exception saved."; }
			else status = "Enter a full friend code such as name#1234. It may already be listed, or the list is full (128).";
		}
		public override void Render()
		{
			Styles.Help("Friends who can join below your minimum level. Other anti-cheat checks still apply.");
			if(GUILayout.Button(Editing ? "Stop typing" : "Type friend code")) { Editing = !Editing; LoadoutsEditor.Editing=RulesEditor.Editing=false; }
			GUILayout.Label("Friend code: " + (Draft.Length == 0 ? "name#1234" : Draft) + (Editing ? " |" : ""), Styles.Heading);
			Styles.Help(Editing ? "Enter to save. Backspace to edit. Escape to stop." : "Close game chat before typing. Codes are saved only on this device.");
			if(GUILayout.Button("Add exception")) Add();
			if(GUILayout.Button("Clear input")) Draft = "";
			GUILayout.Label(status);
			Styles.HeadingText($"Saved friends ({FriendCodeExceptions.Snapshot().Length})");
			foreach(string code in FriendCodeExceptions.Snapshot())
				if(GUILayout.Button("Remove " + code)) { FriendCodeExceptions.Remove(code); status = "Exception removed."; }
		}
	}
}
```

```cs
using UnityEngine;

namespace AUAnticheat.ui.sections
{
	internal abstract class ISection
	{
		public readonly string name;
		public Vector2 scrollVector;

		public ISection(string name)
		{
			this.name = name;
		}

		public virtual void HandleSubsectionMove(int offset) { }

		public abstract void Render();
	}
}
```

```cs
using AUAnticheat.anticheat;
using UnityEngine;
namespace AUAnticheat.ui.sections
{
 internal sealed class LobbySection : ISection
 {
  private bool rules, level, exceptions, loadouts, moderation;
  private readonly ModeratorSection entry = new();
  private readonly ExceptionsSection friends = new();
  public LobbySection() : base("Lobby") { }
  public override void Render()
  {
   Styles.Help("Configure these at any time. Automatic moderation and public messages require host authority.");
   LobbyChat.ShareAlerts=Styles.Toggle(LobbyChat.ShareAlerts,"Share evidence-based detections in chat");
   Styles.Help("Names a player only after attributable evidence reaches the threshold, or a confirmed exploit. No entry-level or unknown-sender chat notices.");
   RoundReports.Enabled=Styles.Toggle(RoundReports.Enabled,"Post results after each match");
   LobbyExtension.Enabled=Styles.Toggle(LobbyExtension.Enabled,"Accept offered lobby extensions");
   Styles.Help("Results use ONE message after 5 seconds of lobby readiness. Includes winner, duration, impostor roles and observed kills. Overlong results stay in local Alerts. All automatic messages wait for your own chat cooldown too.");
   Styles.Help("Result key: I = Impostor, S = Shapeshifter, P = Phantom, V = Viper; k = observed kills. A ~ marks a shortened name. Full details are saved in Alerts.");
   Styles.Help("Extensions work only when offered and confirmed by the server; this does NOT disable the inactivity countdown.");
   Styles.Help(LobbyExtension.Status);
   moderation=Styles.Foldout(moderation,"Local moderation and chat filters");
   if(moderation) ModerationTools.Render();
   loadouts=Styles.Foldout(loadouts,"Lobby settings loadouts");
   if(loadouts) LoadoutsEditor.Render(); else LoadoutsEditor.Editing=false;
   level=Styles.Foldout(level,"Lobby entry rule");
   if(level) entry.RenderLevelSelector();
   exceptions=Styles.Foldout(exceptions,"Friend-code exceptions");
   if(exceptions) friends.Render(); else ExceptionsSection.Editing=false;
   rules=Styles.Foldout(rules,"Saved lobby rules");
   if(!rules) { RulesEditor.Editing=false; return; }
   LobbyRules.Enabled=Styles.Toggle(LobbyRules.Enabled,"Auto-post rules when lobby is full");
   GUILayout.Label($"Full lobby for: {LobbyRules.DelaySeconds} seconds");
   LobbyRules.DelaySeconds=(int)System.Math.Round(GUILayout.HorizontalSlider(LobbyRules.DelaySeconds,1,120));
   Styles.Help("Host only. ONE rules message each time the lobby fills and the same full roster stays for your chosen delay. A departure or replacement restarts the timer. No repeats while it stays full and unchanged. Your normal chat cooldown still applies. No splitting or truncation.");
   RulesEditor.Render();
   Styles.Help(LobbyRules.Status);
   Styles.Help("Saved file: " + LobbyRules.FilePath);
  }
 }
}
```

```cs
using System;
using UnityEngine;
namespace AUAnticheat.ui.sections
{
 internal class MenuSection : ISection
 {
  private bool confirmEject;
  private bool confirmReset;
  public MenuSection() : base("Settings") { }
  public override void Render()
  {
   Styles.Help(anticheat.SavedSettings.Status);
   if(GUILayout.Button("Reset all settings to defaults")) confirmReset=true;
   if(confirmReset)
   {
    Styles.Help("Resets every preference and clears friend-code exceptions. Keeps your rules text and saved alert history. This does not undo bans.");
    if(GUILayout.Button("Confirm reset all settings",Styles.Danger)) { anticheat.SavedSettings.ResetDefaults(); confirmReset=false; }
    if(GUILayout.Button("Cancel reset")) confirmReset=false;
   }
   Styles.HeadingText("Appearance");
   Styles.Help(GameFont.Current != null ? "Using the game's loaded font." : "Using a fallback font: this game build has not exposed a compatible source font.");
   GUILayout.Label($"Menu size: {MainUI.scale:0.00}x");
   MainUI.scale=(float)Math.Round(GUILayout.HorizontalSlider(MainUI.scale,.75f,1.5f),2);
   Styles.Help("Automatically fits your screen. Drag only the top bar to move.");
   GUILayout.Label($"Background opacity: {Styles.menuOpacity*100:0}%");
   Styles.menuOpacity=(float)Math.Round(GUILayout.HorizontalSlider(Styles.menuOpacity,.35f,1f),2);
   Styles.Help("Lower = more see-through. Applies to this menu, the top-right status badge and pop-up alerts. Text and icons stay solid so they remain readable.");
   if(GUILayout.Button("Reset appearance")) { MainUI.scale=1; Styles.menuOpacity=.75f; Styles.primaryColor=Styles.UIColors.Cardinal; MainUI.windowPosition=new Vector2(90,70); }
   Styles.HeadingText("Privacy");
   Styles.Help("Exceptions are saved locally. Diagnostics can appear in BepInEx logs. Shared alerts use your normal chat.");
   if(GUILayout.Button("Clear session evidence")) { anticheat.Anticheat.ResetSession(); anticheat.ActivityTracker.Reset(); }
   Styles.Help("Clears current detection scores and observation history. It does not undo bans or remove saved friend-code exceptions.");
   Styles.HeadingText("Plugin");
   if(GUILayout.Button("Unload plugin",Styles.Danger)) confirmEject=!confirmEject;
   if(confirmEject) { Styles.Help("This stops protections until you restart the game."); if(GUILayout.Button("Confirm unload",Styles.Danger)) AUAnticheatPlugin.Eject(); }
  }
 }
}
```

`source/ui/sections/ModeratorSection.cs`:

```cs
using UnityEngine;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace AUAnticheat.ui.sections
{
	internal class ModeratorSection : ISection
	{
		private int selectedOwnerId = -1;
		private uint selectedNetId;
		private string pendingAction;
		
		private string session;
		private string minimumLevelInput = "10";
		private bool showLevelKeypad;

		public ModeratorSection() : base("Players") { }

		public override void Render()
		{
			string current = AmongUsClient.Instance == null ? "offline" : $"{AmongUsClient.Instance.GameId}:{AmongUsClient.Instance.HostId}";
			if(current != session) { selectedOwnerId = -1; pendingAction = null; session = current; }
			bool host = AmongUsClient.Instance != null && AmongUsClient.Instance.AmHost;
			Styles.Help(host ? "Select a player to moderate. Names use their visible in-game color." : "Player list. Only the host can remove players.");
			PlayerControl target = FindSelectedPlayer();
			if(target == null) pendingAction = null;
			if(target != null)
			{
				Styles.HeadingText("Selected player");
				GUILayout.Label(PlayerPresentation.Name(target), PlayerPresentation.NameStyle(target, true));
				GUILayout.Label(PlayerPresentation.Details(target));
				Styles.Help("Game-reported details, not verified identity. Visible only to you.");
				ModerationTools.Selected(target);
				Styles.Help("Kick removes a player now. Ban also blocks ordinary rejoining of this lobby; it is not a game-wide account ban. Both require confirmation and host authority.");
			}
			if(target != null && host && target != PlayerControl.LocalPlayer && target.OwnerId != AmongUsClient.Instance.HostId)
			{
				GUILayout.BeginHorizontal();
				try
				{
					if(GUILayout.Button("Kick selected player")) pendingAction = "Kick";
					if(GUILayout.Button("Ban selected player", Styles.Danger)) pendingAction = "Ban";
				}
				finally { GUILayout.EndHorizontal(); }
				if(GUILayout.Button("Save persistent ban", Styles.Danger)) pendingAction = "Save persistent ban";
				if(pendingAction != null)
				{
					Styles.Help(pendingAction == "Save persistent ban" ?
						$"Save {PlayerPresentation.Name(target)} to your local ban list? Applies in this and future waiting lobbies while you host and list enforcement is on. Not a cheating accusation." :
						$"{pendingAction} {PlayerPresentation.Name(target)} from this lobby?");
					if(GUILayout.Button("Confirm " + pendingAction, Styles.Danger))
					{
						if(pendingAction == "Save persistent ban")
						{
							anticheat.ModerationStore.Set(anticheat.ChatModeration.BoundCode(target),"ban",true);
							AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Send("Local ban list",anticheat.ModerationStore.Status);
						}
						else
						{
							bool ban = pendingAction == "Ban";
							AmongUsClient.Instance.KickPlayer(target.OwnerId,ban);
							AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications.Send("Moderation", $"{PlayerPresentation.Name(target)}: {(ban ? "ban" : "kick")} requested by the host.");
						}
						pendingAction = null;
					}
					if(GUILayout.Button("Cancel")) pendingAction = null;
				}
			}
			if(!host) pendingAction = null;
			Styles.HeadingText("Choose a player");
			Styles.Help("Tap a name. Their details open at the top of this panel.");
			var roster = new List<PlayerControl>();
			foreach(var player in PlayerControl.AllPlayerControls)
				if(player != null && player.Data != null && !player.Data.Disconnected) roster.Add(player);
			for(int row = 0; row < roster.Count; row += 3)
			{
				GUILayout.BeginHorizontal();
				try
				{
					for(int i = row; i < System.Math.Min(row + 3, roster.Count); i++)
					{
						var player = roster[i];
						bool selected = player.OwnerId == selectedOwnerId && player.NetId == selectedNetId;
						string suffix = player == PlayerControl.LocalPlayer ? "  (you)" : player.OwnerId == AmongUsClient.Instance?.HostId ? "  (host)" : "";
						if(GUILayout.Button((selected ? ">  " : "") + PlayerPresentation.Name(player) + suffix, PlayerPresentation.NameStyle(player, selected)))
						{ selectedOwnerId = player.OwnerId; selectedNetId = player.NetId; pendingAction = null; scrollVector = new Vector2(0,0); }
					}
				}
				finally { GUILayout.EndHorizontal(); }
			}
			if(roster.Count == 0) Styles.Help("Players appear here when you join a lobby.");
			
			
		}

		internal void RenderLevelSelector()
		{
			anticheat.LobbyLevelPolicy.Enabled = Styles.Toggle(anticheat.LobbyLevelPolicy.Enabled, "Ban below minimum level");
			GUILayout.Label($"Minimum displayed level: {anticheat.LobbyLevelPolicy.MinimumLevel} (host always exempt)");
			Styles.Help("Uses the loaded live level, or the current join level while the live value is unavailable. Level 1 needs an observed level update, complete data, no earlier higher value on this player object, and 5 stable seconds. An unresolved loading value is never banned just because time passed. Host and exceptions remain exempt; reported levels are not authenticated account levels.");
			GUILayout.BeginHorizontal();
			try
			{
				foreach(int step in new[] { -100, -10, -1, 1, 10, 100 })
					if(GUILayout.Button(step > 0 ? $"+{step}" : step.ToString()))
					{
						anticheat.LobbyLevelPolicy.MinimumLevel = anticheat.LevelSelectorRules.Adjust(anticheat.LobbyLevelPolicy.MinimumLevel, step);
						minimumLevelInput = anticheat.LobbyLevelPolicy.MinimumLevel.ToString();
					}
			}
			finally { GUILayout.EndHorizontal(); }
			if(GUILayout.Button(showLevelKeypad ? "Close exact-level keypad" : "Choose exact minimum..."))
			{
				showLevelKeypad = !showLevelKeypad;
				minimumLevelInput = anticheat.LobbyLevelPolicy.MinimumLevel.ToString();
			}
			if(showLevelKeypad)
			{
				// The game's stripped GUI.DoTextField cannot be restored by interop.
				// Use existing supported label/button controls; no native text editor.
				GUILayout.Label($"Pending minimum: {(minimumLevelInput.Length == 0 ? "(empty)" : minimumLevelInput)}");
				for(int row = 0; row < 2; row++)
				{
					GUILayout.BeginHorizontal();
					try
					{
						for(int digit = row * 5; digit < row * 5 + 5; digit++)
							if(GUILayout.Button(digit.ToString())) minimumLevelInput = anticheat.LevelSelectorRules.Append(minimumLevelInput, digit);
					}
					finally { GUILayout.EndHorizontal(); }
				}
				GUILayout.BeginHorizontal();
				try
				{
					if(GUILayout.Button("Clear")) minimumLevelInput = "";
					if(GUILayout.Button("Backspace")) minimumLevelInput = anticheat.LevelSelectorRules.Backspace(minimumLevelInput);
					if(GUILayout.Button("Apply minimum") && anticheat.LevelSelectorRules.TryMinimum(minimumLevelInput, out uint minimum))
						anticheat.LobbyLevelPolicy.MinimumLevel = minimum;
				}
				finally { GUILayout.EndHorizontal(); }
				Styles.Help("Enter a whole number, then Apply. Minimum: 1.");
			}
			Styles.Help("Can be configured with players already here. Host only: waits for the game's ban permission and matching client records, then bans one at a time with a departure wait and 2-second cooldown. No entry-rule chat messages. Host and friend-code exceptions are exempt; levels are not proof of cheating.");
		}

		private PlayerControl FindSelectedPlayer()
		{
			foreach(PlayerControl player in PlayerControl.AllPlayerControls)
			{
				if(player != null && player.Data != null && !player.Data.Disconnected && player.OwnerId == selectedOwnerId && player.NetId == selectedNetId) return player;
			}

			return null;
		}
	}
}
```

```cs
using UnityEngine;
namespace AUAnticheat.ui.sections
{
 internal sealed class NotificationsSection : ISection
 {
  private bool evidence;
  private bool activity;
  private bool sequences;
  public NotificationsSection() : base("Alerts") { }
  public override void Render()
  {
   var notices=AUAnticheatPlugin.notifications;
   GUILayout.Label($"{anticheat.ActivityTracker.Count} players observed   /   {notices.HistoryCount} saved alerts");
   Styles.Help("Unknown senders are never automatically banned. Local protection cannot stop every attack.");
   sequences=Styles.Foldout(sequences,"Related suspicious actions");
   if(sequences)
   {
    Styles.Help("Groups spaced invalid actions involving the same player object. Normal transitions and short bursts are discounted. These are review clues, not proof that the named player cheated. No public accusations are sent.");
    Styles.Help(anticheat.Anticheat.GetSequenceSummary());
   }
   activity=Styles.Foldout(activity,"Activity details");
   if(activity) { Styles.Help(anticheat.ActivityTracker.Summary()); Styles.Help(anticheat.Anticheat.GetRecentIncidentSummary()); }
   notices.DisableNotifications=!Styles.Toggle(!notices.DisableNotifications,"Show pop-up alerts");
   Styles.Help("Pop-ups last 10 seconds. Saved alerts stay until you leave or clear them.");
   if(GUILayout.Button("Clear alert history")) notices.ClearHistory();
   notices.ShowTechnicalDetails=Styles.Toggle(notices.ShowTechnicalDetails,"Technical detail");
   Styles.Help("Leave technical detail off for plain-language alerts. Turn it on when sharing a bug report. A blocked action is not necessarily a ban; an unknown sender cannot be safely identified.");
   GUILayout.Label(notices.GetHistorySummary(200));
   evidence=Styles.Foldout(evidence,"Sender evidence");
   if(!evidence) return;
   Styles.Help("Claimed IDs are not verified senders. No IP or device IDs are collected.");
   if(GUILayout.Button("Clear sender evidence")) anticheat.SenderEvidence.Reset();
   GUILayout.Label(anticheat.SenderEvidence.Summary());
  }
 }
}
```

```cs
   Styles.Help("These guards check incoming message structure, size and numbers to limit malformed-data crashes. They do not prevent network-level denial-of-service attacks.");
   Styles.Help($"Blocked packets: {anticheat.PacketPreflight.BlockedPackets}\nLatest: {anticheat.PacketPreflight.LastBlockReason}");
  }
 }
```

```cs
// Managed stand-ins only: exercise the real adapter's offsets and error paths
// without starting IL2CPP or sending packets to another player.
public enum RpcCalls { CompleteTask = 1 }
public class VoteBanSystem { public void AddVote(int a,int b){} public void CmdAddVote(int id){} }
namespace HarmonyLib { public class HarmonyPatch : Attribute { public HarmonyPatch(Type t,string n){} } }
namespace AUAnticheat.anticheat { internal static class LobbyChat { public static void ProtectionNotice(){} } }
namespace Hazel
{
    internal class MessageReader
    {
        public byte[] Buffer;
        public int Offset;
        public int Position;
        public int Length;
        public int BytesRemaining => Length - Position;
        public int ReadInt32()
        {
            int value = System.Buffers.Binary.BinaryPrimitives.ReadInt32LittleEndian(Buffer.AsSpan(Offset + Position, 4));
            Position += 4;
            return value;
        }
    }
}
internal class AmongUsClient
{
    public static AmongUsClient Instance;
    public readonly HashSet<int> Clients = new();
    public object FindClientById(int id) => Clients.Contains(id) ? this : null;
}
```

`tests/ConfigMigrationTests.cs`:

```cs
using AUAnticheat.anticheat;

internal static class ConfigMigrationTests
{
    internal static void Run(Action<bool, string> check)
    {
        string directory = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "AU-Anticheat-config-test-" + Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));
        Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        string oldPath = Path.Combine(directory, ConfigMigration.LegacyFileName);
        string newPath = Path.Combine(directory, ConfigMigration.CurrentFileName);
        try
        {
            check(!ConfigMigration.ImportIfMissing(directory), "fresh install has no legacy import");
            const string preferences = "[Admission]\nFriendCodeExceptions = example#1234\n[LobbyRules]\nEnabled = true\nFullLobbySeconds = 20\n";
            File.WriteAllText(oldPath, preferences);
            check(ConfigMigration.ImportIfMissing(directory), "previous preferences imported on first renamed startup");
            check(File.ReadAllText(newPath) == preferences, "exception and rule preferences preserved byte for byte");
            check(File.ReadAllText(oldPath) == preferences, "original config is retained");
            File.WriteAllText(newPath, "user's new preferences");
            check(!ConfigMigration.ImportIfMissing(directory) && File.ReadAllText(newPath) == "user's new preferences", "existing new preferences never overwritten");
            File.WriteAllText(newPath, "");
            check(!ConfigMigration.ImportIfMissing(directory), "intentionally empty new config is not overwritten");
            File.Delete(newPath);
            File.WriteAllText(oldPath, new string('x', 65537));
            bool rejected = false;
            try { ConfigMigration.ImportIfMissing(directory); } catch(IOException) { rejected = true; }
            check(rejected && !File.Exists(newPath), "oversized old config is rejected without destination");
        }
        finally
        {
            // Only explicitly named fixtures in this test's freshly created folder.
            if(File.Exists(oldPath)) File.Delete(oldPath);
            if(File.Exists(newPath)) File.Delete(newPath);
            Directory.Delete(directory);
        }
    }
}
```
